# LOT 2 — Import XML, dry-run et rapport

### Task 9: Session partagée entre cas d'usage (unité de travail) et ADR 3

C'est la tâche isolée qui rend possible l'import atomique avec un `SAVEPOINT` par ligne. **Interface commune avec le sous-projet 2 (audit)** : `ea.db.unit_of_work.work(sessions)` est l'unique point par lequel un dépôt obtient sa session. L'écriture d'audit du sous-projet 2 doit s'y brancher (elle recevra la session de `work(...)` et insérera ses événements dans la même transaction) ; ce plan ne construit rien de plus.

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/src/ea/db/unit_of_work.py`, `docs/adr/NN-session-partagee-entre-cas-d-usage.md`
- Modify: `backend/src/ea/domain/ports.py` (dataclass `Transaction`, après `GraphView`) ; `backend/src/ea/repositories/architecture_store.py` (imports l.15-52, `PostgresArchitectureRepository.__init__` l.322-323 et chaque `async with self._sessions…`)
- Test: `backend/tests/unit/test_unit_of_work.py` (new), `backend/tests/integration/test_unit_of_work.py` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]` (`create_session_factory`).
- Produces : `ea.domain.ports.Transaction(commit: bool = True)` (dataclass mutable) ; `ea.db.unit_of_work` : `ambient_session() -> AsyncSession | None`, `work(sessions) -> AbstractAsyncContextManager[AsyncSession]` (session partagée si une transaction partagée est ouverte dans ce contexte, sinon `sessions.begin()`), `shared_transaction(sessions, *, commit: bool) -> AbstractAsyncContextManager[Transaction]` (refuse l'imbrication ; valide si `handle.commit` est vrai à la sortie, annule sinon ou sur exception), `savepoint() -> AbstractAsyncContextManager[None]` (`begin_nested`, `RuntimeError` hors transaction partagée). `PostgresArchitectureRepository` utilise `work` pour **toutes** ses méthodes.

- [ ] **Step 1: Écrire les tests qui échouent**

```python
# backend/tests/unit/test_unit_of_work.py
"""The shared transaction, against fake sessions — no database needed."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

import pytest

from ea.db.unit_of_work import ambient_session, savepoint, shared_transaction, work

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


class FakeTransaction:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.committed = False
        self.rolled_back = False
        self.is_active = True

    async def commit(self) -> None:
        self.committed = True

    async def rollback(self) -> None:
        self.rolled_back = True


class FakeSession:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.transaction = FakeTransaction()
        self.savepoints = 0

    async def begin(self) -> FakeTransaction:
        return self.transaction

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def begin_nested(self) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
        self.savepoints += 1
        yield


class FakeFactory:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.sessions: list[FakeSession] = []
        self.own_units = 0

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def __call__(self) -> AsyncIterator[FakeSession]:
        session = FakeSession()
        self.sessions.append(session)
        yield session

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def begin(self) -> AsyncIterator[FakeSession]:
        self.own_units += 1
        session = FakeSession()
        self.sessions.append(session)
        yield session


async def test_outside_a_shared_transaction_work_opens_its_own_unit() -> None:
    factory = FakeFactory()

    async with work(factory) as session:  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        assert session is factory.sessions[0]

    assert factory.own_units == 1
    assert ambient_session() is None


async def test_inside_a_shared_transaction_work_reuses_the_shared_session() -> None:
    factory = FakeFactory()

    async with shared_transaction(factory, commit=True):  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        async with work(factory) as first, work(factory) as second:  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            assert first is second is factory.sessions[0]

    assert factory.own_units == 0
    assert len(factory.sessions) == 1


async def test_commit_true_commits_and_commit_false_rolls_back() -> None:
    factory = FakeFactory()
    async with shared_transaction(factory, commit=True):  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        pass
    async with shared_transaction(factory, commit=False):  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        pass

    assert factory.sessions[0].transaction.committed
    assert factory.sessions[1].transaction.rolled_back
    assert not factory.sessions[1].transaction.committed


async def test_the_handle_can_cancel_the_commit_from_inside() -> None:
    factory = FakeFactory()
    async with shared_transaction(factory, commit=True) as handle:  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        handle.commit = False

    assert factory.sessions[0].transaction.rolled_back


async def test_an_exception_rolls_back_and_forgets_the_session() -> None:
    factory = FakeFactory()

    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="boom"):
        async with shared_transaction(factory, commit=True):  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            raise RuntimeError("boom")

    assert factory.sessions[0].transaction.rolled_back
    assert ambient_session() is None


async def test_a_second_shared_transaction_in_the_same_context_is_refused() -> None:
    factory = FakeFactory()

    async with shared_transaction(factory, commit=False):  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="already open"):
            async with shared_transaction(factory, commit=False):  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                pass


async def test_a_savepoint_needs_a_shared_transaction() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="shared transaction"):
        async with savepoint():
            pass


async def test_a_savepoint_is_a_nested_transaction_of_the_shared_session() -> None:
    factory = FakeFactory()

    async with shared_transaction(factory, commit=False):  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        async with savepoint():
            pass

    assert factory.sessions[0].savepoints == 1
```

```python
# backend/tests/integration/test_unit_of_work.py
"""The shared transaction against a real PostgreSQL: rollback, commit and savepoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine

from ea.db.postgres import create_session_factory
from ea.db.unit_of_work import savepoint, shared_transaction
from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType as E
from ea.domain.errors import DuplicateElementError
from ea.domain.ports import ElementFilter
from ea.repositories.architecture_store import PostgresArchitectureRepository
from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService

pytestmark = [pytest.mark.asyncio, pytest.mark.postgres]


@pytest.fixture
def parts(engine_at_head: AsyncEngine):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    sessions = create_session_factory(engine_at_head)
    repository = PostgresArchitectureRepository(sessions)
    return sessions, repository, ArchitectureService(repository)


async def test_writes_in_a_rolled_back_transaction_vanish_but_are_visible_inside(parts) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    sessions, repository, service = parts

    async with shared_transaction(sessions, commit=False):
        await service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="a")
        assert await repository.count_elements(ElementFilter()) == 1

    assert await repository.count_elements(ElementFilter()) == 0


async def test_writes_in_a_committed_transaction_stay(parts) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    sessions, repository, service = parts

    async with shared_transaction(sessions, commit=True):
        await service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="a")
        await service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="b")

    assert await repository.count_elements(ElementFilter()) == 2


async def test_a_refused_row_rolls_back_to_its_savepoint_only(parts) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    sessions, repository, service = parts

    async with shared_transaction(sessions, commit=True):
        await service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="a")
        with pytest.raises(DuplicateElementError):
            async with savepoint():
                await service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="a")
        await service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="b")

    assert await repository.count_elements(ElementFilter()) == 2
```

`backend/src/ea/db/unit_of_work.py` :

```python
"""One transaction shared by several use cases (docs/adr/NN).

Every repository opens its own unit of work per call — right for a use case
that writes once, wrong for an import that must create hundreds of elements
and links, undo a refused one and be rolled back as a whole in a dry run.

`shared_transaction` opens one session and *binds it to the current context*;
`work` is how a repository gets its session, and hands that shared one back
while it is bound. The binding is a `ContextVar`, exactly like the caller of
`services/caller.py`: invisible to the routes, private to the task that opened
it, and absent for every other request. Whoever opens the transaction owns
commit and rollback; a repository inside it never commits.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from contextvars import ContextVar
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from ea.domain.ports import Transaction

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from collections.abc import AsyncIterator

    from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker

_shared: ContextVar[AsyncSession | None] = ContextVar("ea_shared_session", default=None)


def ambient_session() -> AsyncSession | None:
    """The session of the shared transaction open in this context, if any."""
    return _shared.get()


@asynccontextmanager
async def work(sessions: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]) -> AsyncIterator[AsyncSession]:
    """The unit of work of one repository call.

    Inside a shared transaction: its session, left open for the owner to commit.
    Otherwise: a session of its own, committed when the call ends — what every
    repository did before this module existed.
    """
    shared = _shared.get()
    if shared is not None:
        yield shared
        return
    async with sessions.begin() as own:
        yield own


@asynccontextmanager
async def shared_transaction(
    sessions: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession], *, commit: bool
) -> AsyncIterator[Transaction]:
    """Open the transaction every `work` call in this context will join.

    It ends with a commit only if `handle.commit` is still true when the block
    exits normally; an exception, or `commit=False`, rolls everything back.
    """
    if _shared.get() is not None:
        msg = "a shared transaction is already open in this context"
        raise RuntimeError(msg)
    async with sessions() as session:
        outer = await session.begin()
        handle = Transaction(commit=commit)
        token = _shared.set(session)
        try:
            yield handle
        except BaseException:
            if outer.is_active:
                await outer.rollback()
            raise
        else:
            if handle.commit:
                await outer.commit()
            else:
                await outer.rollback()
        finally:
            _shared.reset(token)


@asynccontextmanager
async def savepoint() -> AsyncIterator[None]:
    """A `SAVEPOINT` inside the shared transaction: a refusal undoes only its own writes."""
    session = _shared.get()
    if session is None:
        msg = "a savepoint needs a shared transaction"
        raise RuntimeError(msg)
    async with session.begin_nested():
        yield
```

`architecture_store.py` : ajouter l'import `from ea.db.unit_of_work import work` (après `from ea.db.models.architecture import …`) ; dans le bloc `if TYPE_CHECKING:` ajouter `from contextlib import AbstractAsyncContextManager` ; dans `PostgresArchitectureRepository`, après `__init__` :

```python
    def _work(self) -> AbstractAsyncContextManager[AsyncSession]:
        """The session of this call: the shared transaction's, or a unit of its own."""
        return work(self._sessions)
```

Puis deux remplacements (`replace_all`) dans tout le fichier : `async with self._sessions.begin() as session:` → `async with self._work() as session:` et `async with self._sessions() as session:` → `async with self._work() as session:`. Contrôle : `grep -n "self._sessions" backend/src/ea/repositories/architecture_store.py` ne doit plus montrer que `__init__` et `_work`. (Les lectures passent désormais par `begin()` hors transaction partagée : un `COMMIT` sans écriture, sans effet observable.)

```markdown
---
titre: Session partagée entre cas d'usage
date: 2026-10-10
statut: Proposition
affects: db/unit_of_work.py, repositories/architecture_store.py, repositories/diagram_store.py, repositories/exchange_store.py, services/exchanges.py
---

# Session partagée entre cas d'usage

## Contexte

Chaque dépôt ouvre une unité de travail **par appel** (`async with self._sessions.begin()`), ce
qui convient à un cas d'usage qui écrit une fois. L'import d'un fichier d'échange doit créer des
centaines d'éléments et de liens en passant par `ArchitectureService` (pour que chaque règle
ArchiMate s'applique), défaire une ligne refusée sans perdre les autres (`SAVEPOINT`), et pouvoir
tout annuler en dry-run. Le sous-projet « historique et versions » aura besoin de la même chose :
insérer un événement d'audit dans la transaction de l'écriture. Le seul précédent est `replace`
des documents, qui écrit deux tables dans un appel de dépôt — il ne couvre pas plusieurs services.

## Décision

Une **session explicite liée au contexte**, dans `db/unit_of_work.py` :

- `shared_transaction(sessions, commit=…)` ouvre une session, la lie à une `ContextVar`
  (comme l'appelant de `services/caller.py`) et rend un `Transaction` dont `commit` peut être
  basculé à `False` ; elle valide ou annule à la sortie, et annule sur exception.
- `work(sessions)` est le seul point par lequel un dépôt obtient sa session : la session partagée
  si une transaction l'est dans ce contexte, sinon `sessions.begin()` comme avant.
- `savepoint()` ouvre un `SAVEPOINT` (`begin_nested`) dans la session partagée.
- Le service ouvre la transaction via le port `ExchangeRepository.transaction(commit=…)` ; les
  routes ne voient jamais la session. Seuls les dépôts qu'un import traverse sont convertis
  (`architecture_store`, puis `diagram_store`).

Interface commune avec l'audit : le point d'accroche est `work(sessions)` — l'écriture d'audit
recevra la session de l'unité de travail en cours et y insérera ses événements.

## Alternatives envisagées et écartées

| Option | Pour | Contre | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| Passer une `AsyncSession` en paramètre de chaque méthode de dépôt (comme `replace`) | explicite | change toutes les signatures des ports et des doubles, et les services qui appellent des services | écarté |
| Un service d'import qui parle SQL | une seule session | contourne `ArchitectureService` : un graphe que l'API refuserait | écarté |
| Une transaction par ligne | aucun changement | pas d'annulation globale, pas de dry-run honnête, pas de `strict` | écarté |
| Session posée dans `app.state` ou globale | simple | partagée entre requêtes : jamais | écarté |

## Conséquences

- Une tâche asynchrone créée *pendant* une transaction partagée en hérite (copie du contexte) ;
  un test qui veut une seconde connexion indépendante passe `context=contextvars.Context()`.
- Un dépôt non converti ouvrirait sa propre session au milieu d'un import et écrirait *hors* de la
  transaction : tout dépôt qu'un cas d'usage partagé traverse doit passer par `work`.
- L'imbrication de deux `shared_transaction` dans un même contexte est refusée.

## Références

- ADR liés : [0015](0015-socle-postgresql-sqlalchemy-alembic.md), [0033](0033-postgresql-seul-pour-le-graphe.md)
- Code concerné : `backend/src/ea/db/unit_of_work.py`
```

`backend/tests/exchange_files.py` :

```python
"""Small builders for exchange files in tests — text, never a fixture of someone else's."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from ea.domain.exchange import NAMESPACE

DATA = Path(__file__).parent / "data" / "exchange"
_XSI = "http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"


def exchange_xml(elements: str = "", relationships: str = "", extra: str = "") -> bytes:
    body = f"<name>M</name>"
    if elements:
        body += f"<elements>{elements}</elements>"
    if relationships:
        body += f"<relationships>{relationships}</relationships>"
    return (
        f'<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>'
        f'<model xmlns="{NAMESPACE}" xmlns:xsi="{_XSI}" identifier="m">{body}{extra}</model>'
    ).encode()


def el(identifier: str, kind: str, name: str, extra: str = "") -> str:
    return f'<element identifier="{identifier}" xsi:type="{kind}"><name>{name}</name>{extra}</element>'


def rel(identifier: str, kind: str, source: str, target: str, extra: str = "") -> str:
    return (
        f'<relationship identifier="{identifier}" source="{source}" target="{target}" '
        f'xsi:type="{kind}">{extra}</relationship>'
    )
```

Test :

```python
# backend/tests/unit/test_archimate_xml_reader.py
"""Reading an exchange file: what is kept, what is reported, and what is refused outright."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from ea.domain.archimate import AccessType, ElementType, RelationshipType
from ea.domain.errors import InvalidExchangeFileError
from ea.domain.exchange import Category, ExchangeModel, Severity
from ea.formats.archimate_xml import parse_model
from tests.exchange_files import DATA, el, exchange_xml, rel


def codes(model: ExchangeModel) -> list[str]:
    return [issue.code for issue in model.issues]


class TestAWellFormedFile:
    def test_the_minimal_model_is_read(self) -> None:
        model = parse_model((DATA / "minimal-model.xml").read_bytes())

        assert [e.name for e in model.elements] == ["Customer", "Shop", "Ordering"]
        shop = model.elements[1]
        assert shop.element_type is ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT
        assert dict(shop.properties) == {"owner": "retail"}
        assert model.elements[0].documentation == "Buys things"
        assert [r.relationship_type for r in model.relationships] == [
            RelationshipType.ASSOCIATION,
            RelationshipType.SERVING,
            RelationshipType.FLOW,
        ]
        assert model.relationships[1].name == "serves"

    def test_a_junction_is_set_aside_not_read(self) -> None:
        model = parse_model((DATA / "minimal-model.xml").read_bytes())

        (junction,) = model.unreadable
        assert (junction.category, junction.identifier, junction.ignored) == (
            Category.ELEMENTS,
            "id-and",
            True,
        )
        assert "junction_not_supported" in codes(model)

    def test_a_path_locates_each_element(self) -> None:
        model = parse_model((DATA / "minimal-model.xml").read_bytes())

        assert model.elements[1].path == "elements/element[2]"

    def test_the_declared_encoding_is_honoured(self) -> None:
        text = '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="ISO-8859-1"?>'
        raw = exchange_xml(el("a", "Node", "Café")).decode().replace(
            '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>', text
        )

        model = parse_model(raw.encode("latin-1"))

        assert model.elements[0].name == "Café"

    def test_several_languages_keep_the_first_and_warn(self) -> None:
        xml = exchange_xml(
            '<element identifier="a" xsi:type="Node"><name xml:lang="en">One</name>'
            '<name xml:lang="fr">Un</name></element>'
        )

        model = parse_model(xml)

        assert model.elements[0].name == "One"
        assert "multiple_languages" in codes(model)


class TestRefusedOutright:
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("raw", [b"", b"   \n  "])
    def test_an_empty_file(self, raw: bytes) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(InvalidExchangeFileError, match="empty"):
            parse_model(raw)

    def test_malformed_xml_names_a_line_and_never_the_content(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(InvalidExchangeFileError) as raised:
            parse_model(b"<model>\n<oops-secret></model>")

        assert "line 2" in str(raised.value)
        assert "oops-secret" not in str(raised.value)

    def test_the_wrong_root(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(InvalidExchangeFileError, match="not an ArchiMate exchange file"):
            parse_model(b'<other xmlns="urn:x"/>')

    def test_an_unknown_namespace_version(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(InvalidExchangeFileError, match="namespace"):
            parse_model(b'<model xmlns="http://www.opengroup.org/xsd/archimate/9.9/"/>')

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["billion-laughs.xml", "xxe-file.xml"])
    def test_a_dtd_or_entity_is_refused_without_echo(self, name: str) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(InvalidExchangeFileError) as raised:
            parse_model((DATA / name).read_bytes())

        message = str(raised.value)
        assert "lol" not in message and "hostname" not in message and "secret" not in message

    def test_too_many_items(self, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
        monkeypatch.setattr("ea.formats.archimate_xml.MAX_EXCHANGE_ITEMS", 2)
        xml = exchange_xml(el("a", "Node", "A") + el("b", "Node", "B") + el("c", "Node", "C"))

        with pytest.raises(InvalidExchangeFileError, match="at most 2"):
            parse_model(xml)


class TestRowsThatCannotBeRead:
    def test_a_repeated_identifier_keeps_the_first_and_flags_the_second(self) -> None:
        model = parse_model(exchange_xml(el("a", "Node", "One") + el("a", "Node", "Two")))

        assert [e.name for e in model.elements] == ["One"]
        (duplicate,) = model.unreadable
        assert duplicate.cascade is False
        assert "duplicate_identifier" in codes(model)

    def test_a_missing_identifier_and_an_unknown_type_are_rejected_rows(self) -> None:
        xml = exchange_xml(
            '<element xsi:type="Node"><name>X</name></element>' + el("b", "Spaceship", "Y")
        )

        model = parse_model(xml)

        assert model.elements == ()
        assert {i.code for i in model.issues} == {"missing_identifier", "unknown_type"}
        assert all(i.severity is Severity.ERROR for i in model.issues)
        assert len(model.unreadable) == 2

    def test_a_relationship_without_an_end_is_rejected(self) -> None:
        xml = exchange_xml(
            el("a", "Node", "A"), '<relationship identifier="r" source="a" xsi:type="Flow"/>'
        )

        model = parse_model(xml)

        assert model.relationships == ()
        assert "missing_end" in codes(model)
        assert model.unreadable[0].category is Category.RELATIONSHIPS


class TestRelationshipDetails:
    def parse_one(self, kind: str, attributes: str = "", body: str = "") -> ExchangeModel:
        link = (
            f'<relationship identifier="r" source="a" target="b" xsi:type="{kind}" '
            f"{attributes}>{body}</relationship>"
        )
        return parse_model(exchange_xml(el("a", "Node", "A") + el("b", "Node", "B"), link))

    def test_access_defaults_to_write_and_reads_the_attribute(self) -> None:
        assert self.parse_one("Access").relationships[0].access_type is AccessType.WRITE
        read = self.parse_one("Access", 'accessType="ReadWrite"')
        assert read.relationships[0].access_type is AccessType.READ_WRITE

    def test_an_unknown_access_type_falls_back_with_a_warning(self) -> None:
        model = self.parse_one("Access", 'accessType="Teleport"')

        assert model.relationships[0].access_type is AccessType.WRITE
        assert "unknown_access_type" in codes(model)

    def test_association_direction_and_influence_modifier(self) -> None:
        assert self.parse_one("Association", 'isDirected="true"').relationships[0].directed
        assert not self.parse_one("Association").relationships[0].directed
        influence = self.parse_one("Influence", 'modifier="++"')
        assert dict(influence.relationships[0].properties) == {"modifier": "++"}

    def test_a_relationship_documentation_is_dropped_with_a_note(self) -> None:
        model = self.parse_one("Flow", body="<documentation>why</documentation>")

        assert "documentation_dropped" in codes(model)


class TestProperties:
    def definitions(self, *rows: tuple[str, str]) -> str:
        return "<propertyDefinitions>" + "".join(
            f'<propertyDefinition identifier="{i}" type="string"><name>{n}</name></propertyDefinition>'
            for i, n in rows
        ) + "</propertyDefinitions>"

    def prop(self, ref: str, value: str) -> str:
        return f'<property propertyDefinitionRef="{ref}"><value>{value}</value></property>'

    def test_reserved_invalid_and_repeated_keys_are_handled(self) -> None:
        extra = (
            "<properties>"
            + self.prop("d1", "keep")
            + self.prop("d2", "text")
            + self.prop("d3", "lost")
            + self.prop("d4", "lost")
            + self.prop("d5", "first")
            + self.prop("d5", "second")
            + self.prop("dx", "orphan")
            + "</properties>"
        )
        xml = exchange_xml(
            el("a", "Node", "A", extra),
            extra=self.definitions(
                ("d1", "owner"), ("d2", "ea.description"), ("d3", "Owner name"),
                ("d4", "source_id"), ("d5", "tier"),
            ),
        )

        element = parse_model(xml).elements[0]

        assert dict(element.properties) == {"owner": "keep", "tier": "second"}
        assert element.description == "text"
        assert set(codes(parse_model(xml))) >= {
            "invalid_property_key",
            "reserved_property_dropped",
            "duplicate_property",
            "unknown_property_definition",
        }
```

Fin de `backend/src/ea/domain/exchange.py` (ajouter aux imports en tête `from collections.abc import Iterable` et `from ea.domain.model import is_property_key`) :

```python
MAX_EXCHANGE_ITEMS: Final = 50_000


class Severity(StrEnum):
    ERROR = "error"
    WARNING = "warning"
    INFO = "info"


class Category(StrEnum):
    ELEMENTS = "elements"
    RELATIONSHIPS = "relationships"
    PACKAGES = "packages"
    VIEWS = "views"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Issue:
    """One line of the import report. `path` locates it in an XML file, `line` in a CSV."""

    severity: Severity
    code: str
    message: str
    source_id: str | None = None
    line: int | None = None
    path: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Unreadable:
    """A row the reader could not turn into an element or link, set aside for the report.

    `ignored` marks what is deliberately not imported (a junction) as opposed to
    refused. `cascade` is false for a repeated identifier: refusing the second
    must not refuse the first, which is the row everything points at.
    """

    category: Category
    identifier: str
    ignored: bool = False
    cascade: bool = True


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ExchangeModel:
    elements: tuple[ExchangeElement, ...] = ()
    relationships: tuple[ExchangeRelationship, ...] = ()
    unreadable: tuple[Unreadable, ...] = ()
    issues: tuple[Issue, ...] = ()


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class FoldedProperties:
    properties: Mapping[str, str]
    description: str
    issues: tuple[Issue, ...]


def fold_properties(pairs: Iterable[tuple[str, str]]) -> FoldedProperties:
    """Sort a file's properties into what is stored, the description, and what is reported.

    `ea.description` is the description; any other `ea.*` and `source_id` belong
    to the exchange itself; a key the model would refuse is dropped, never
    renamed (two keys could collide); the last of a repeated key wins.
    """
    properties: dict[str, str] = {}
    description = ""
    issues: list[Issue] = []
    for key, value in pairs:
        if key == DESCRIPTION_KEY:
            description = value
        elif key.startswith(RESERVED_PREFIX):
            issues.append(
                Issue(Severity.INFO, "reserved_property_dropped",
                      f"property {key!r} belongs to the exchange format and is not stored")
            )
        elif key == SOURCE_ID:
            issues.append(
                Issue(Severity.WARNING, "reserved_property_dropped",
                      "property 'source_id' is the file's own identifier and is not stored as a property")
            )
        elif not is_property_key(key):
            issues.append(
                Issue(Severity.WARNING, "invalid_property_key",
                      f"property {key!r} is not a plain identifier and was dropped")
            )
        else:
            if key in properties:
                issues.append(
                    Issue(Severity.WARNING, "duplicate_property",
                          f"property {key!r} appears twice; the last value wins")
                )
            properties[key] = value
    return FoldedProperties(MappingProxyType(properties), description, tuple(issues))
```

Fin de `backend/src/ea/formats/archimate_xml.py` (imports à ajouter en tête : `from dataclasses import replace`, `from typing import TYPE_CHECKING`, `from defusedxml import ElementTree as SafeET`, `from defusedxml.common import DTDForbidden, EntitiesForbidden, ExternalReferenceForbidden`, `from defusedxml.ElementTree import ParseError`, et depuis `ea.domain.exchange` : `ACCESS_BY_NAME, JUNCTION_NAMES, MAX_EXCHANGE_ITEMS, Category, ExchangeModel, Issue, Severity, Unreadable, element_type_of, fold_properties, relationship_type_of`, `from ea.domain.errors import InvalidExchangeFileError`; `AccessType`, `RelationshipType`, `MODIFIER_KEY`, `NAMESPACE`, `ExchangeElement`, `ExchangeRelationship` sont déjà importés par la Task 3) :

```python
if TYPE_CHECKING:
    # Annotation only — nothing is parsed with it; every parse goes through defusedxml.
    from xml.etree.ElementTree import Element  # nosec B405

_XSI_TYPE: Final = f"{{{_XSI}}}type"
_NS_PREFIX: Final = "http://www.opengroup.org/xsd/archimate/"


def _q(tag: str) -> str:
    return f"{{{NAMESPACE}}}{tag}"


def _refuse(message: str) -> InvalidExchangeFileError:
    return InvalidExchangeFileError(message)


def _load(data: bytes) -> Element:
    if not data.strip():
        raise _refuse("the file is empty")
    try:
        root = SafeET.fromstring(data, forbid_dtd=True, forbid_entities=True, forbid_external=True)
    except DTDForbidden:
        raise _refuse("a DOCTYPE declaration is not allowed in an exchange file") from None
    except (EntitiesForbidden, ExternalReferenceForbidden):
        raise _refuse("entities and external references are not allowed") from None
    except ParseError as error:
        line = error.position[0] if getattr(error, "position", None) else None
        where = f" (line {line})" if line else ""
        raise _refuse(f"the file is not well-formed XML{where}") from None
    if root.tag == _q("model"):
        return root
    if root.tag.startswith(f"{{{_NS_PREFIX}") and root.tag.endswith("}model"):
        raise _refuse("unsupported exchange namespace; only the 3.x namespace is read")
    raise _refuse("not an ArchiMate exchange file: the root element is not 'model'")


def _child_text(node: Element, tag: str, issues: list[Issue]) -> str:
    found = node.findall(_q(tag))
    if len(found) > 1:
        issues.append(Issue(Severity.WARNING, "multiple_languages",
                            f"several <{tag}> elements; the first is kept"))
    return (found[0].text or "").strip() if found else ""


def _definitions(root: Element) -> dict[str, str]:
    out: dict[str, str] = {}
    for definition in root.findall(f"{_q('propertyDefinitions')}/{_q('propertyDefinition')}"):
        name = definition.find(_q("name"))
        if definition.get("identifier") and name is not None:
            out[definition.get("identifier", "")] = (name.text or "").strip()
    return out


def _pairs(
    node: Element, definitions: dict[str, str], issues: list[Issue]
) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    pairs: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    for prop in node.findall(f"{_q('properties')}/{_q('property')}"):
        key = definitions.get(prop.get("propertyDefinitionRef") or "")
        if key is None:
            issues.append(Issue(Severity.WARNING, "unknown_property_definition",
                                "a property refers to a definition the file does not declare"))
            continue
        value = prop.find(_q("value"))
        pairs.append((key, (value.text or "") if value is not None else ""))
    return pairs


def parse_model(data: bytes) -> ExchangeModel:
    """Read an exchange file; raise `InvalidExchangeFileError` only if nothing can be read."""
    root = _load(data)
    element_nodes = root.findall(f"{_q('elements')}/{_q('element')}")
    link_nodes = root.findall(f"{_q('relationships')}/{_q('relationship')}")
    if len(element_nodes) + len(link_nodes) > MAX_EXCHANGE_ITEMS:
        raise _refuse(f"an exchange file holds at most {MAX_EXCHANGE_ITEMS} elements and links")
    definitions = _definitions(root)
    seen: set[str] = set()
    issues: list[Issue] = []
    unreadable: list[Unreadable] = []
    elements = []
    relationships = []

    def note(issue: Issue, identifier: str | None, path: str) -> None:
        issues.append(replace(issue, source_id=identifier, path=path))

    for index, node in enumerate(element_nodes, start=1):
        path = f"elements/element[{index}]"
        identifier = node.get("identifier") or ""
        local: list[Issue] = []
        kind = node.get(_XSI_TYPE) or ""
        if not identifier:
            note(Issue(Severity.ERROR, "missing_identifier", "an element has no identifier"), None, path)
            unreadable.append(Unreadable(Category.ELEMENTS, f"element[{index}]"))
            continue
        if identifier in seen:
            note(Issue(Severity.ERROR, "duplicate_identifier",
                       f"identifier {identifier!r} is already used; this one is ignored"), identifier, path)
            unreadable.append(Unreadable(Category.ELEMENTS, identifier, cascade=False))
            continue
        seen.add(identifier)
        if kind in JUNCTION_NAMES:
            note(Issue(Severity.INFO, "junction_not_supported",
                       "junctions cannot be stored; this one is ignored"), identifier, path)
            unreadable.append(Unreadable(Category.ELEMENTS, identifier, ignored=True))
            continue
        element_type = element_type_of(kind)
        if element_type is None:
            note(Issue(Severity.ERROR, "unknown_type", f"{kind!r} is not an ArchiMate element type"),
                 identifier, path)
            unreadable.append(Unreadable(Category.ELEMENTS, identifier))
            continue
        folded = fold_properties(_pairs(node, definitions, local))
        elements.append(
            ExchangeElement(
                identifier=identifier,
                element_type=element_type,
                name=_child_text(node, "name", local),
                description=folded.description,
                documentation=_child_text(node, "documentation", local),
                properties=folded.properties,
                path=path,
            )
        )
        for issue in (*local, *folded.issues):
            note(issue, identifier, path)

    for index, node in enumerate(link_nodes, start=1):
        path = f"relationships/relationship[{index}]"
        identifier = node.get("identifier") or ""
        local = []
        if not identifier:
            note(Issue(Severity.ERROR, "missing_identifier", "a relationship has no identifier"), None, path)
            unreadable.append(Unreadable(Category.RELATIONSHIPS, f"relationship[{index}]"))
            continue
        if identifier in seen:
            note(Issue(Severity.ERROR, "duplicate_identifier",
                       f"identifier {identifier!r} is already used; this one is ignored"), identifier, path)
            unreadable.append(Unreadable(Category.RELATIONSHIPS, identifier, cascade=False))
            continue
        seen.add(identifier)
        kind = node.get(_XSI_TYPE) or ""
        relationship_type = relationship_type_of(kind)
        source, target = node.get("source"), node.get("target")
        if relationship_type is None:
            note(Issue(Severity.ERROR, "unknown_type", f"{kind!r} is not an ArchiMate relationship type"),
                 identifier, path)
            unreadable.append(Unreadable(Category.RELATIONSHIPS, identifier))
            continue
        if not source or not target:
            note(Issue(Severity.ERROR, "missing_end", "a relationship needs a source and a target"),
                 identifier, path)
            unreadable.append(Unreadable(Category.RELATIONSHIPS, identifier))
            continue
        folded = fold_properties(_pairs(node, definitions, local))
        properties = dict(folded.properties)
        access_type = None
        if relationship_type is RelationshipType.ACCESS:
            raw = node.get("accessType") or "Write"  # the schema's default
            access_type = ACCESS_BY_NAME.get(raw)
            if access_type is None:
                local.append(Issue(Severity.WARNING, "unknown_access_type",
                                   "unknown accessType; 'write' is used"))
                access_type = AccessType.WRITE
        if relationship_type is RelationshipType.INFLUENCE and node.get("modifier"):
            properties[MODIFIER_KEY] = node.get("modifier", "")
        if node.find(_q("documentation")) is not None:
            local.append(Issue(Severity.INFO, "documentation_dropped",
                               "a relationship has no documentation field; it is not stored"))
        relationships.append(
            ExchangeRelationship(
                identifier=identifier,
                relationship_type=relationship_type,
                source=source,
                target=target,
                name=_child_text(node, "name", local),
                access_type=access_type,
                directed=relationship_type is RelationshipType.ASSOCIATION
                and node.get("isDirected") in ("true", "1"),
                properties=properties,
                path=path,
            )
        )
        for issue in (*local, *folded.issues):
            note(issue, identifier, path)

    return ExchangeModel(tuple(elements), tuple(relationships), tuple(unreadable), tuple(issues))
```

(`RelationshipType` est déjà importé par la Task 3 ; `MODIFIER_KEY`, `ExchangeElement` et `ExchangeRelationship` aussi. Le helper d'échappement `_text(value)` de la Task 3 garde son nom : celui-ci s'appelle `_child_text`.)

Les trois dernières exigent une transaction partagée ouverte (`RuntimeError` sinon).

```python
async def test_the_import_lock_admits_one_transaction_at_a_time(
    exchange: PostgresExchangeRepository,
) -> None:
    async def another() -> bool:
        async with exchange.transaction(commit=False):
            return await exchange.try_import_lock()

    async with exchange.transaction(commit=False):
        assert await exchange.try_import_lock() is True
        # a fresh context: a task copies the current one, session included
        task = asyncio.create_task(another(), context=contextvars.Context())
        assert await task is False

    async with exchange.transaction(commit=False):
        assert await exchange.try_import_lock() is True  # released with the first


async def test_the_known_maps_see_what_the_transaction_wrote(
    exchange: PostgresExchangeRepository, graph_service: ArchitectureService
) -> None:
    async with exchange.transaction(commit=False):
        a = await graph_service.create_element(
            element_type=E.NODE, name="a", properties={SOURCE_ID: "n-1"}
        )
        b = await graph_service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="b")
        await graph_service.connect(relationship_type=R.FLOW, source_id=a.id, target_id=b.id)

        elements = await exchange.known_elements()
        keys = await exchange.known_relationship_keys()

    assert {e.name for e in elements} == {"a", "b"}
    assert keys == frozenset({(R.FLOW, a.id, b.id)})


async def test_the_known_maps_need_a_transaction(exchange: PostgresExchangeRepository) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="shared transaction"):
        await exchange.known_elements()
```

```python
    def transaction(self, *, commit: bool) -> AbstractAsyncContextManager[Transaction]:
        """One transaction every write of an import joins; rolled back unless committed."""
        ...

    def savepoint(self) -> AbstractAsyncContextManager[None]:
        """A `SAVEPOINT` inside that transaction: a refused row undoes only itself."""
        ...

    async def try_import_lock(self) -> bool:
        """Take the one advisory lock imports share, for this transaction; `False` if held."""
        ...

    async def known_elements(self) -> tuple[Element, ...]:
        """Every element of the catalogue — the map an import matches `(type, name)` against."""
        ...

    async def known_relationship_keys(self) -> frozenset[tuple[RelationshipType, UUID, UUID]]:
        """`(type, source, target)` of every link, to tell a new link from an existing one."""
        ...
```

`exchange_store.py` (imports : `hashlib`, `from sqlalchemy import func`, `from ea.db.unit_of_work import ambient_session, savepoint, shared_transaction`, `from ea.domain.archimate import RelationshipType`) :

```python
#: One lock for every import: a 64-bit key derived from a fixed name, so it is
#: the same in every process and collides with nothing else by accident.
IMPORT_LOCK_KEY = int.from_bytes(hashlib.sha256(b"ea.exchange.import").digest()[:8], "big", signed=True)
```

et dans la classe :

```python
    def transaction(self, *, commit: bool) -> AbstractAsyncContextManager[Transaction]:
        return shared_transaction(self._sessions, commit=commit)

    def savepoint(self) -> AbstractAsyncContextManager[None]:
        return savepoint()

    @staticmethod
    def _shared() -> AsyncSession:
        session = ambient_session()
        if session is None:
            msg = "this read needs a shared transaction: open one with `transaction()`"
            raise RuntimeError(msg)
        return session

    async def try_import_lock(self) -> bool:
        # `pg_try_advisory_xact_lock`: released with the transaction, and with the
        # connection if the process dies — a stuck import never blocks the others.
        statement = select(func.pg_try_advisory_xact_lock(IMPORT_LOCK_KEY))
        return bool((await self._shared().execute(statement)).scalar_one())

    async def known_elements(self) -> tuple[Element, ...]:
        rows = await self._shared().scalars(select(ElementRecord))
        return tuple(element_from_row(row) for row in rows)

    async def known_relationship_keys(
        self,
    ) -> frozenset[tuple[RelationshipType, UUID, UUID]]:
        rows = await self._shared().execute(
            select(
                RelationshipRecord.relationship_type,
                RelationshipRecord.source_id,
                RelationshipRecord.target_id,
            )
        )
        return frozenset((RelationshipType(kind), source, target) for kind, source, target in rows)
```
(imports `Transaction` depuis `ea.domain.ports`, `AbstractAsyncContextManager` et `UUID` sous `TYPE_CHECKING`.)

```python
from ea.api.schemas import ElementCreate
from ea.domain.errors import RowRefusedError
from ea.domain.exchange import (
    MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH, MAX_DOCUMENTATION_LENGTH, MAX_NAME_LENGTH, Category, Counts,
    ExchangeElement, ExchangeFormat, Issue, Outcome, ReportBuilder, Severity, digest_of,
    validate_element,
)


def test_the_limits_are_the_ones_the_http_schema_declares() -> None:
    schema = ElementCreate.model_json_schema()["properties"]

    assert schema["name"]["maxLength"] == MAX_NAME_LENGTH
    assert schema["description"]["maxLength"] == MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH
    assert schema["documentation"]["maxLength"] == MAX_DOCUMENTATION_LENGTH


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("fields", "code"),
    [
        ({"name": "  "}, "blank_name"),
        ({"name": "x" * (MAX_NAME_LENGTH + 1)}, "name_too_long"),
        ({"description": "x" * (MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH + 1)}, "description_too_long"),
        ({"documentation": "x" * (MAX_DOCUMENTATION_LENGTH + 1)}, "documentation_too_long"),
    ],
)
def test_a_row_outside_the_bounds_is_refused_with_its_code(fields: dict[str, str], code: str) -> None:
    item = ExchangeElement("a", ElementType.NODE, **{"name": "ok", **fields})  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    with pytest.raises(RowRefusedError) as raised:
        validate_element(item)

    assert raised.value.code == code


def test_a_single_file_is_digested_like_shasum_and_several_are_length_prefixed() -> None:
    import hashlib

    assert digest_of(b"abc") == hashlib.sha256(b"abc").hexdigest()
    assert digest_of(b"a", b"bc") != digest_of(b"ab", b"c")


def test_the_report_counts_and_truncates_but_keeps_the_counts_complete(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr("ea.domain.exchange.MAX_REPORT_ISSUES", 2)
    builder = ReportBuilder()
    for _ in range(5):
        builder.count(Category.ELEMENTS, Outcome.REJECTED)
        builder.add(Issue(Severity.ERROR, "x", "m"))
    builder.add(Issue(Severity.ERROR, "strict_refused", "m"), always=True)

    report = builder.build(
        dry_run=True, applied=False, format=ExchangeFormat.ARCHIMATE_XML, sha256="0"
    )

    assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS] == Counts(rejected=5)
    assert len(report.issues) == 3 and report.truncated
    assert report.issues[-1].code == "strict_refused"
    assert builder.has_rejections()
```

`backend/tests/unit/test_exchange_import.py` :

```python
"""Importing elements: the collision rule, the report, and dry-run."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import logging

import pytest

from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType as E
from ea.domain.errors import (
    ExchangeFileTooLargeError,
    InvalidExchangeFileError,
    NotAuthenticatedError,
    NotAuthorisedError,
)
from ea.domain.exchange import (
    SOURCE_ID, Category, ConflictPolicy, ExchangeFormat, ImportContent, ImportOptions,
)
from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService
from ea.services.caller import acting_as
from ea.services.exchanges import ExchangeService
from tests.conftest import InMemoryRepository, a_reader
from tests.exchange_files import DATA, el, exchange_xml

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio
REAL = ImportOptions(dry_run=False)


def xml(data: bytes) -> ImportContent:
    return ImportContent(ExchangeFormat.ARCHIMATE_XML, data)


class TestTheDefaultIsToWriteNothing:
    async def test_an_import_with_default_options_is_a_dry_run(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, repository: InMemoryRepository
    ) -> None:
        content = xml((DATA / "minimal-model.xml").read_bytes())

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(content, ImportOptions())

        assert report.dry_run and not report.applied
        assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS].created == 3
        assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS].ignored == 1  # the junction
        assert repository.elements == {}

    async def test_a_real_import_writes_and_reports_the_same_counts(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, repository: InMemoryRepository
    ) -> None:
        content = xml((DATA / "minimal-model.xml").read_bytes())

        simulated = await exchange_service.import_exchange(content, ImportOptions())
        real = await exchange_service.import_exchange(content, REAL)

        assert real.applied and not real.dry_run
        assert real.counts == simulated.counts
        assert real.issues == simulated.issues
        assert real.sha256 == simulated.sha256 == hashlib.sha256(content.main).hexdigest()
        stored = {e.name: e for e in repository.elements.values()}
        assert stored["Shop"].properties == {"owner": "retail", SOURCE_ID: "id-app"}
        assert stored["Customer"].documentation == "Buys things"


class TestTheCollisionRule:
    async def test_an_existing_element_is_reused_and_only_a_blank_description_is_filled(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        kept = await service.create_element(
            element_type=E.NODE, name="srv", description="mine", properties={"tier": "gold"}
        )
        empty = await service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="bare")
        described = (
            '<properties><property propertyDefinitionRef="d"><value>from the file</value>'
            "</property></properties>"
        )
        content = xml(
            exchange_xml(
                el("a", "Node", "srv", "<documentation>x</documentation>") + el("b", "Node", "bare", described),
                extra=(
                    '<propertyDefinitions><propertyDefinition identifier="d" type="string">'
                    "<name>ea.description</name></propertyDefinition></propertyDefinitions>"
                ),
            )
        )

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(content, REAL)

        assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS].reused == 2
        assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS].created == 0
        after_kept = await service.get_element(kept.id)
        after_empty = await service.get_element(empty.id)
        assert after_kept.description == "mine"
        assert dict(after_kept.properties) == {"tier": "gold", SOURCE_ID: "a"}
        assert after_empty.description == "from the file"

    async def test_on_conflict_fail_makes_an_existing_element_a_rejected_row(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        await service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="srv")

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(
            xml(exchange_xml(el("a", "Node", "srv"))),
            ImportOptions(dry_run=False, on_conflict=ConflictPolicy.FAIL),
        )

        assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS].rejected == 1
        assert report.issues[0].code == "already_exists"
        assert report.issues[0].source_id == "a"

    async def test_the_same_type_and_name_under_two_identifiers_is_merged_with_a_warning(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, repository: InMemoryRepository
    ) -> None:
        content = xml(exchange_xml(el("a", "Node", "srv") + el("b", "Node", "srv")))

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(content, REAL)

        assert len(repository.elements) == 1
        assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS].created == 1
        assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS].reused == 1
        assert [i.code for i in report.issues] == ["duplicate_in_file"]


class TestRowsThatAreRefused:
```

Fin de `domain/exchange.py` (imports à ajouter : `import hashlib`, `from ea.domain.errors import RowRefusedError`) :

```python
MAX_REPORT_ISSUES: Final = 1_000
MAX_NAME_LENGTH: Final = 200
MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH: Final = 2_000
MAX_DOCUMENTATION_LENGTH: Final = 20_000


class ConflictPolicy(StrEnum):
    REUSE = "reuse"
    FAIL = "fail"


class Outcome(StrEnum):
    CREATED = "created"
    REUSED = "reused"
    REJECTED = "rejected"
    IGNORED = "ignored"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ImportOptions:
    dry_run: bool = True
    on_conflict: ConflictPolicy = ConflictPolicy.REUSE
    strict: bool = False


def digest_of(*parts: bytes) -> str:
    """SHA-256 of one file as `shasum` would print it; several files, length-prefixed."""
    if len(parts) == 1:
        return hashlib.sha256(parts[0]).hexdigest()
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    for part in parts:
        digest.update(len(part).to_bytes(8, "big"))
        digest.update(part)
    return digest.hexdigest()


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ImportContent:
    """The bytes received: one XML file, or `elements.csv` with its companions."""

    format: ExchangeFormat
    main: bytes
    relations: bytes | None = None
    properties: bytes | None = None

    @property
    def parts(self) -> tuple[bytes, ...]:
        return tuple(p for p in (self.main, self.relations, self.properties) if p is not None)

    @property
    def size(self) -> int:
        return sum(len(part) for part in self.parts)

    @property
    def sha256(self) -> str:
        return digest_of(*self.parts)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Counts:
    created: int = 0
    reused: int = 0
    rejected: int = 0
    ignored: int = 0


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ImportReport:
    dry_run: bool
    #: True only when the transaction was committed: a dry run and a refused
    #: `strict` import both leave it False, and both wrote nothing.
    applied: bool
    format: ExchangeFormat
    sha256: str
    counts: Mapping[Category, Counts]
    issues: tuple[Issue, ...]
    truncated: bool


class ReportBuilder:
    """Counts every row, keeps the first `MAX_REPORT_ISSUES` lines."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._counts = {category: dict.fromkeys(Outcome, 0) for category in Category}
        self._issues: list[Issue] = []
        self._truncated = False

    def count(self, category: Category, outcome: Outcome) -> None:
        self._counts[category][outcome] += 1

    def add(self, issue: Issue, *, always: bool = False) -> None:
        if len(self._issues) >= MAX_REPORT_ISSUES and not always:
            self._truncated = True
            return
        self._issues.append(issue)

    def has_rejections(self) -> bool:
        return any(by[Outcome.REJECTED] for by in self._counts.values())

    def build(
        self, *, dry_run: bool, applied: bool, format: ExchangeFormat, sha256: str  # noqa: A002
    ) -> ImportReport:
        counts = {
            category: Counts(**{outcome.value: n for outcome, n in by.items()})
            for category, by in self._counts.items()
        }
        return ImportReport(
            dry_run, applied, format, sha256, counts, tuple(self._issues), self._truncated
        )


def validate_element(item: ExchangeElement) -> None:
    """Refuse an element the HTTP schema would refuse (same bounds, tested equal)."""
    if not item.name.strip():
        raise RowRefusedError("blank_name", "an element name cannot be blank")
    if len(item.name.strip()) > MAX_NAME_LENGTH:
        raise RowRefusedError("name_too_long", f"a name is at most {MAX_NAME_LENGTH} characters")
    if len(item.description.strip()) > MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH:
        raise RowRefusedError(
            "description_too_long", f"a description is at most {MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH} characters"
        )
    if len(item.documentation.strip()) > MAX_DOCUMENTATION_LENGTH:
        raise RowRefusedError(
            "documentation_too_long",
            f"documentation is at most {MAX_DOCUMENTATION_LENGTH} characters",
        )
```

Service (`services/exchanges.py`) — ajouter aux imports : `from dataclasses import dataclass, field`, `from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType`, `from ea.domain.errors import DomainError, ExchangeFileTooLargeError, ImportInProgressError, RowRefusedError`, `from ea.domain.exchange import (SOURCE_ID, Category, ConflictPolicy, ExchangeModel, ImportContent, ImportOptions, ImportReport, Issue, Outcome, ReportBuilder, Severity, validate_element)`, `from ea.formats.archimate_xml import parse_model`, `from ea.services.caller import require_editor`, `from ea.domain.model import Element`. Ajouter la propriété et les méthodes à `ExchangeService` :

```python
    @property
    def max_bytes(self) -> int:
        return self._max_bytes

    async def import_exchange(self, content: ImportContent, options: ImportOptions) -> ImportReport:
        """Apply (or simulate) an exchange file through the architecture service.

        Editors only, *dry run included*: the simulation really performs the
        writes, then rolls them back — that is what makes it honest.
        """
        caller = require_editor()
        if content.size > self._max_bytes:
            msg = f"an exchange file is at most {self._max_bytes} bytes"
            raise ExchangeFileTooLargeError(msg)
        model = self._read(content)
        report = ReportBuilder()
        for issue in model.issues:
            report.add(issue)
        async with self._repository.transaction(commit=not options.dry_run) as transaction:
            if not await self._repository.try_import_lock():
                msg = "another import is running; try again when it has finished"
                raise ImportInProgressError(msg)
            await self._apply(model, options, report)
            if options.strict and report.has_rejections():
                transaction.commit = False
                report.add(
                    Issue(Severity.ERROR, "strict_refused",
                          "strict mode: at least one row was refused, so nothing was written"),
                    always=True,
                )
        built = report.build(
            dry_run=options.dry_run,
            applied=transaction.commit and not options.dry_run,
            format=content.format,
            sha256=content.sha256,
        )
        self._log_import(caller.subject, content, built)
        return built

    def _read(self, content: ImportContent) -> ExchangeModel:
        return parse_model(content.main)

    async def _apply(
        self, model: ExchangeModel, options: ImportOptions, report: ReportBuilder
    ) -> None:
        known = await self._repository.known_elements()
        batch = _Batch(
            by_key={(e.element_type, e.name): e for e in known},
            by_source={e.properties[SOURCE_ID]: e.id for e in known if SOURCE_ID in e.properties},
        )
        for item in model.unreadable:
            report.count(item.category, Outcome.IGNORED if item.ignored else Outcome.REJECTED)
            if item.cascade:
                batch.refused.add(item.identifier)
        await self._apply_elements(model, options, report, batch)

    async def _apply_elements(
        self, model: ExchangeModel, options: ImportOptions, report: ReportBuilder, batch: _Batch
    ) -> None:
        for item in model.elements:
            try:
                async with self._repository.savepoint():
                    outcome, element = await self._element(item, options, report, batch)
            except (DomainError, ValueError) as refusal:
                batch.refused.add(item.identifier)
                report.count(Category.ELEMENTS, Outcome.REJECTED)
                report.add(_refusal(refusal, item.identifier, item.line, item.path))
                continue
            batch.ids[item.identifier] = element.id
            report.count(Category.ELEMENTS, outcome)

    async def _element(
        self, item: ExchangeElement, options: ImportOptions, report: ReportBuilder, batch: _Batch
    ) -> tuple[Outcome, Element]:
        validate_element(item)
        key = (item.element_type, item.name.strip())
        match = batch.by_key.get(key)
        if match is not None:
            if options.on_conflict is ConflictPolicy.FAIL:
                msg = f"a {item.element_type.value} named {item.name.strip()!r} already exists"
                raise RowRefusedError("already_exists", msg)
            if key in batch.created:
                report.add(
                    Issue(Severity.WARNING, "duplicate_in_file",
                          "the same type and name appear under another identifier; merged",
                          item.identifier, item.line, item.path)
                )
            return Outcome.REUSED, await self._complete(match, item, report)
        created = await self._architecture.create_element(
            element_type=item.element_type,
            name=item.name,
            description=item.description,
            documentation=item.documentation,
            properties={**item.properties, SOURCE_ID: item.identifier},
        )
        batch.by_key[key] = created
        batch.by_source[item.identifier] = created.id
        batch.created.add(key)
        return Outcome.CREATED, created

    async def _complete(self, match: Element, item: ExchangeElement, report: ReportBuilder) -> Element:
        """Fill what is empty — a description, a `source_id` — and touch nothing else."""
        description = item.description if item.description and not match.description else None
        needs_source = SOURCE_ID not in match.properties
        if description is None and not needs_source:
            return match
        properties = {**match.properties, SOURCE_ID: item.identifier} if needs_source else None
        try:
            async with self._repository.savepoint():
                return await self._architecture.update_element(
                    match.id, description=description, properties=properties
                )
        except DomainError as refusal:
            report.add(
                Issue(Severity.WARNING, "not_completed", str(refusal),
                      item.identifier, item.line, item.path)
            )
            return match

    @staticmethod
    def _log_import(actor: str, content: ImportContent, report: ImportReport) -> None:
        # `rows_*` and not `created`: that is a `LogRecord` attribute (its timestamp),
        # and `extra=` refuses a key that collides with one.
        totals = {
            f"rows_{outcome}": sum(getattr(counts, outcome) for counts in report.counts.values())
            for outcome in ("created", "reused", "rejected", "ignored")
        }
        logger.info(
            "exchange file imported",
            extra={
                "action": "imported",
                "dry_run": report.dry_run,
                "applied": report.applied,
                "format": report.format.value,
                "size_bytes": content.size,
                "sha256": report.sha256,
                "actor": actor,
                **totals,
            },
        )
```

Hors de la classe :

```python
@dataclass(slots=True)
class _Batch:
    """What one import has learned so far: the catalogue as it was, plus what it added."""

    by_key: dict[tuple[ElementType, str], Element]
    by_source: dict[str, UUID]
    ids: dict[str, UUID] = field(default_factory=dict)
    refused: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    created: set[tuple[ElementType, str]] = field(default_factory=set)


def _refusal(error: Exception, identifier: str, line: int | None, path: str | None) -> Issue:
    code = error.code if isinstance(error, RowRefusedError) else _CODES.get(type(error), "invalid_row")
    return Issue(Severity.ERROR, code, str(error), identifier, line, path)
```

avec en tête de module (imports `DuplicateElementError`, `AddressAlreadyAssignedError`, `IllegalRelationshipError`, `CyclicContainmentError`, `ElementNotFoundError` depuis `ea.domain.errors`) :

```python
#: The stable name a report gives each refusal the services can raise.
_CODES: Final = {
    IllegalRelationshipError: "relationship_not_allowed",
    CyclicContainmentError: "cyclic_containment",
    DuplicateElementError: "duplicate_element",
    AddressAlreadyAssignedError: "address_taken",
    ElementNotFoundError: "unknown_end",
}
```
(importer `Final` depuis `typing`.) `_complete` déclare `ExchangeElement` : l'importer depuis `ea.domain.exchange`.

`tests/conftest.py` — étendre `InMemoryExchange` :

```python
from ea.domain.ports import Transaction   # with the other ports imports


    @asynccontextmanager
    async def transaction(self, *, commit: bool) -> AsyncIterator[Transaction]:
        """All-or-nothing over the in-memory graph, like the real shared transaction."""
        before = self._copy()
        handle = Transaction(commit=commit)
        try:
            yield handle
        except BaseException:
            self._restore(before)
            raise
        if not handle.commit:
            self._restore(before)

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def savepoint(self) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
        before = self._copy()
        try:
            yield
        except BaseException:
            self._restore(before)
            raise

    def _copy(self) -> tuple[dict[UUID, Element], dict[UUID, Relationship]]:
        return dict(self.repository.elements), dict(self.repository.relationships)

    def _restore(self, saved: tuple[dict[UUID, Element], dict[UUID, Relationship]]) -> None:
        self.repository.elements, self.repository.relationships = dict(saved[0]), dict(saved[1])

    async def try_import_lock(self) -> bool:
        return not self.lock_held

    async def known_elements(self) -> tuple[Element, ...]:
        return tuple(self.repository.elements.values())

    async def known_relationship_keys(self) -> frozenset[tuple[R, UUID, UUID]]:
        return frozenset(
            (link.relationship_type, link.source_id, link.target_id)
            for link in self.repository.relationships.values()
        )
```

```python
class TestRelationships:
    async def test_a_relationship_is_created_with_its_identifier_as_source_id(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, repository: InMemoryRepository
    ) -> None:
        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(
            xml((DATA / "minimal-model.xml").read_bytes()), REAL
        )

        assert report.counts[Category.RELATIONSHIPS].created == 2  # the third touches the junction
        assert report.counts[Category.RELATIONSHIPS].rejected == 1
        served = next(r for r in repository.relationships.values() if r.name == "serves")
        assert served.properties[SOURCE_ID] == "id-r2"
        assert any(i.code == "end_rejected" and i.source_id == "id-r3" for i in report.issues)

    async def test_a_relationship_outside_the_matrix_is_reported_and_the_rest_is_written(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, repository: InMemoryRepository
    ) -> None:
        assert not permits(R.SPECIALIZATION, E.BUSINESS_ACTOR, E.NODE)
        content = xml((DATA / "refused-relations.xml").read_bytes())

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(content, REAL)

        assert len(repository.elements) == 3
        assert [r.relationship_type for r in repository.relationships.values()] == [R.ASSOCIATION]
        refusal = next(i for i in report.issues if i.code == "relationship_not_allowed")
        assert refusal.severity is Severity.ERROR and refusal.source_id == "r-bad"
        assert report.applied

    async def test_strict_writes_nothing_when_any_row_is_refused(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, repository: InMemoryRepository
    ) -> None:
        content = xml((DATA / "refused-relations.xml").read_bytes())

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(
            content, ImportOptions(dry_run=False, strict=True)
        )

        assert repository.elements == {} and repository.relationships == {}
        assert not report.applied
        assert report.issues[-1].code == "strict_refused"

    async def test_a_relationship_to_an_unknown_identifier_is_a_rejected_row(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService
    ) -> None:
        content = xml(
            exchange_xml(el("a", "Node", "A"), '<relationship identifier="r" source="a" '
                         'target="ghost" xsi:type="Association"/>')
        )

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(content, REAL)

        assert [i.code for i in report.issues] == ["unknown_end"]

    async def test_a_relationship_may_name_an_element_already_in_the_catalogue_by_source_id(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, service: ArchitectureService,
        repository: InMemoryRepository,
    ) -> None:
        await service.create_element(
            element_type=E.NODE, name="old", properties={SOURCE_ID: "legacy-1"}
        )
        content = xml(
            exchange_xml(el("n", "Node", "new"), '<relationship identifier="r" source="n" '
                         'target="legacy-1" xsi:type="Association"/>')
        )

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(content, REAL)

        assert report.counts[Category.RELATIONSHIPS].created == 1
        assert len(repository.relationships) == 1

    async def test_an_existing_link_is_reused_and_a_repeated_one_is_warned_about(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService
    ) -> None:
        content = xml(
            exchange_xml(
                el("a", "Node", "A") + el("b", "Node", "B"),
                rel("r1", "Association", "a", "b") + rel("r2", "Association", "a", "b"),
            )
        )

        first = await exchange_service.import_exchange(content, REAL)
        again = await exchange_service.import_exchange(content, REAL)

        assert first.counts[Category.RELATIONSHIPS].created == 1
        assert first.counts[Category.RELATIONSHIPS].reused == 1
        assert "duplicate_relationship" in [i.code for i in first.issues]
        assert again.counts[Category.RELATIONSHIPS].created == 0
        assert again.counts[Category.RELATIONSHIPS].reused == 2

    async def test_on_conflict_fail_refuses_an_existing_link(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService
    ) -> None:
        content = xml(exchange_xml(el("a", "Node", "A") + el("b", "Node", "B"),
                                   rel("r1", "Association", "a", "b")))
        await exchange_service.import_exchange(content, REAL)

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(
            content, ImportOptions(dry_run=False, on_conflict=ConflictPolicy.FAIL)
        )

        assert report.counts[Category.RELATIONSHIPS].rejected == 1

    async def test_a_composition_loop_in_a_file_rejects_the_closing_row(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, repository: InMemoryRepository
    ) -> None:
        content = xml(
            exchange_xml(
                el("a", "ApplicationComponent", "A") + el("b", "ApplicationComponent", "B"),
                rel("r1", "Composition", "a", "b") + rel("r2", "Composition", "b", "a"),
            )
        )

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(content, REAL)

        assert len(repository.relationships) == 1
        assert [i.code for i in report.issues] == ["cyclic_containment"]

    async def test_a_held_lock_is_a_409_and_writes_nothing(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, exchange_store: InMemoryExchange,
        repository: InMemoryRepository,
    ) -> None:
        exchange_store.lock_held = True

        with pytest.raises(ImportInProgressError):
            await exchange_service.import_exchange(xml(exchange_xml(el("a", "Node", "A"))), REAL)

        assert repository.elements == {}

    async def test_the_report_keeps_complete_counts_when_its_lines_are_cut(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
    ) -> None:
        monkeypatch.setattr("ea.domain.exchange.MAX_REPORT_ISSUES", 2)
        rows = "".join(el(f"e{i}", "Node", " ") for i in range(5))

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(xml(exchange_xml(rows)), REAL)

        assert report.truncated and len(report.issues) == 2
        assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS].rejected == 5
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implémenter** — dans `_apply`, après l'appel à `_apply_elements`, ajouter `await self._apply_relationships(model, options, report, batch)` et la méthode (imports : `from ea.domain.exchange import ExchangeRelationship`) :

```python
    async def _apply_relationships(
        self, model: ExchangeModel, options: ImportOptions, report: ReportBuilder, batch: _Batch
    ) -> None:
        known = set(await self._repository.known_relationship_keys())
        added: set[tuple[RelationshipType, UUID, UUID]] = set()

        def reject(item: ExchangeRelationship, code: str, message: str) -> None:
            report.count(Category.RELATIONSHIPS, Outcome.REJECTED)
            report.add(Issue(Severity.ERROR, code, message, item.identifier, item.line, item.path))

        for item in model.relationships:
            source, target = batch.resolve(item.source), batch.resolve(item.target)
            if item.source in batch.refused or item.target in batch.refused:
                reject(item, "end_rejected", "an end of this relationship was rejected or ignored")
                continue
            if source is None or target is None:
                reject(item, "unknown_end", "an end of this relationship is not in the file or the catalogue")
                continue
            key = (item.relationship_type, source, target)
            if key in known:
                if options.on_conflict is ConflictPolicy.FAIL:
                    reject(item, "already_exists", "this relationship already exists")
                    continue
                if key in added:
                    report.add(
                        Issue(Severity.WARNING, "duplicate_relationship",
                              "the same type, source and target appear twice; one is kept",
                              item.identifier, item.line, item.path)
                    )
                report.count(Category.RELATIONSHIPS, Outcome.REUSED)
                continue
            try:
                async with self._repository.savepoint():
                    await self._architecture.connect(
                        relationship_type=item.relationship_type,
                        source_id=source,
                        target_id=target,
                        name=item.name,
                        access_type=item.access_type,
                        directed=item.directed,
                        properties={**item.properties, SOURCE_ID: item.identifier},
                    )
            except (DomainError, ValueError) as refusal:
                issue = _refusal(refusal, item.identifier, item.line, item.path)
                reject(item, issue.code, issue.message)
                continue
            known.add(key)
            added.add(key)
            report.count(Category.RELATIONSHIPS, Outcome.CREATED)
```

`_Batch.resolve` :

```python
    def resolve(self, identifier: str) -> UUID | None:
        """The element an identifier names: one of this file's, else one already in the catalogue."""
        return self.ids.get(identifier) or self.by_source.get(identifier)
```

```python
# backend/tests/unit/test_body_limit.py
"""The body ceiling is enforced as the bytes arrive, not from a header."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import AsyncIterator

import httpx
import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI, Request

from ea.api.body_limit import LimitingRequestBodies

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio
SEEN: list[int] = []


def make_app(limit: int = 1000) -> FastAPI:
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.post("/exchanges/imports")
    async def imports(request: Request) -> dict[str, int]:
        body = await request.body()
        SEEN.append(len(body))
        return {"size": len(body)}

    @app.post("/other")
    async def other(request: Request) -> dict[str, int]:
        return {"size": len(await request.body())}

    app.add_middleware(LimitingRequestBodies, path_suffix="/exchanges/imports", limit=limit)
    return app


async def post(app: FastAPI, path: str, content: bytes | AsyncIterator[bytes]) -> httpx.Response:
    transport = httpx.ASGITransport(app=app)
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=transport, base_url="http://test") as client:
        return await client.post(path, content=content)


async def test_a_body_under_the_limit_passes() -> None:
    response = await post(make_app(), "/exchanges/imports", b"x" * 500)

    assert response.status_code == 200 and response.json() == {"size": 500}


async def test_a_declared_length_over_the_limit_is_refused_before_reading() -> None:
    SEEN.clear()

    response = await post(make_app(), "/exchanges/imports", b"x" * 5000)

    assert response.status_code == 413
    assert response.json()["error"] == "file_too_large"
    assert SEEN == []


async def test_a_chunked_body_is_cut_as_it_arrives_without_trusting_any_header() -> None:
    SEEN.clear()

    async def chunks() -> AsyncIterator[bytes]:
        for _ in range(50):
            yield b"x" * 100

    response = await post(make_app(), "/exchanges/imports", chunks())

    assert response.status_code == 413
    assert response.json()["error"] == "file_too_large"
    assert SEEN == [] or SEEN[0] < 5000


async def test_other_paths_are_not_limited() -> None:
    response = await post(make_app(), "/other", b"x" * 5000)

    assert response.status_code == 200
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implémenter**

`backend/src/ea/api/body_limit.py` :

```python
"""A ceiling on one endpoint's request body, enforced as the bytes arrive.

Starlette parses a multipart body *before* the route runs, so a handler that
reads `limit + 1` bytes of an `UploadFile` has already let the whole upload be
received and spooled. This middleware counts the bytes of the ASGI `receive`
stream itself and answers `413` the moment the ceiling is crossed — it trusts
no `Content-Length` (a chunked body has none, and a lying one costs nothing).
Pure ASGI, like the other middlewares here, so a stream is never buffered.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Final

from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from starlette.types import ASGIApp, Message, Receive, Scope, Send

#: The multipart envelope around a file: boundaries, headers, form fields.
BODY_ENVELOPE_MARGIN: Final = 64 * 1024


class LimitingRequestBodies:
    def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp, *, path_suffix: str, limit: int) -> None:
        self._app = app
        self._suffix = path_suffix
        self._limit = limit

    def _too_large(self) -> JSONResponse:
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=413,
            content={
                "error": "file_too_large",
                "detail": f"the request is larger than the {self._limit - BODY_ENVELOPE_MARGIN} "
                "byte ceiling for an exchange file",
            },
        )

    async def __call__(self, scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:
        # `endswith`, not `==`: behind the NGINX prefix, `path` may carry `/api`.
        if (
            scope["type"] != "http"
            or scope["method"] != "POST"
            or not scope["path"].endswith(self._suffix)
        ):
            await self._app(scope, receive, send)
            return
        declared = dict(scope["headers"]).get(b"content-length", b"0")
        if declared.isdigit() and int(declared) > self._limit:
            await self._too_large()(scope, receive, send)
            return

        seen = 0
        exceeded = False

        async def counting() -> Message:
            nonlocal seen, exceeded
            message = await receive()
            if message["type"] == "http.request":
                seen += len(message.get("body", b""))
                if seen > self._limit:
                    exceeded = True
                    # Hang up on the application: it stops reading, and whatever it
                    # answers next is muted below and replaced by the 413.
                    return {"type": "http.disconnect"}
            return message

        async def muted(message: Message) -> None:
            if not exceeded:
                await send(message)

        try:
            await self._app(scope, counting, muted)
        except Exception:
            if not exceeded:
                raise
        if exceeded:
            await self._too_large()(scope, receive, send)
```
