```python
    #: The largest exchange file an import accepts. A setting and not a
    #: constant because a deployment sits behind a proxy with its own ceiling
    #: (51 MB on the Infra NGINX); it is read once, at boot, never at runtime.
    exchange_max_bytes: int = Field(default=MAX_EXCHANGE_BYTES, ge=1024)
```

`schemas.py` (imports `Category, ExchangeFormat, ImportReport, Issue, Severity` depuis `ea.domain.exchange`; fin du fichier) :

```python
class IssueRead(BaseModel):
    """One line of an import report."""

    severity: Severity
    code: str
    message: str
    source_id: str | None
    line: int | None
    path: str | None

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, issue: Issue) -> IssueRead:
        return cls(
            severity=issue.severity, code=issue.code, message=issue.message,
            source_id=issue.source_id, line=issue.line, path=issue.path,
        )


class CountsRead(BaseModel):
    created: int
    reused: int
    rejected: int
    ignored: int


class ImportCountsRead(BaseModel):
    elements: CountsRead
    relationships: CountsRead
    packages: CountsRead
    views: CountsRead


class ImportReportRead(BaseModel):
    """What an import did, or would do: counts that are always complete, lines that may be cut."""

    dry_run: bool
    applied: bool = Field(description="True only if the transaction was committed.")
    format: ExchangeFormat
    sha256: str = Field(description="Digest of the received file, to check a confirmation re-sends it.")
    counts: ImportCountsRead
    issues: list[IssueRead]
    truncated: bool = Field(description="True when `issues` was cut at the report ceiling.")

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, report: ImportReport) -> ImportReportRead:
        def counts(category: Category) -> CountsRead:
            c = report.counts[category]
            return CountsRead(created=c.created, reused=c.reused, rejected=c.rejected, ignored=c.ignored)

        return cls(
            dry_run=report.dry_run, applied=report.applied, format=report.format,
            sha256=report.sha256,
            counts=ImportCountsRead(
                elements=counts(Category.ELEMENTS), relationships=counts(Category.RELATIONSHIPS),
                packages=counts(Category.PACKAGES), views=counts(Category.VIEWS),
            ),
            issues=[IssueRead.of(issue) for issue in report.issues],
            truncated=report.truncated,
        )
```

`api/exchanges.py` — ajouter (imports : `File, Form, UploadFile`, `ConflictPolicy, ImportContent, ImportOptions`, `ImportReportRead`) :

```python
IMPORT_ERRORS: dict[int | str, dict[str, type[ErrorResponse]]] = {
    **REJECTED,
    status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT: {"model": ErrorResponse},
    status.HTTP_413_CONTENT_TOO_LARGE: {"model": ErrorResponse},
}


@router.post("/imports", response_model=ImportReportRead, responses=IMPORT_ERRORS)
async def import_model(
    exchanges: Exchanges,
    file: Annotated[UploadFile, File(description="An ArchiMate exchange file (XML).")],
    import_format: Annotated[ExchangeFormat, Form(alias="format")] = ExchangeFormat.ARCHIMATE_XML,
    dry_run: Annotated[bool, Form()] = True,
    on_conflict: Annotated[ConflictPolicy, Form()] = ConflictPolicy.REUSE,
    strict: Annotated[bool, Form()] = False,
) -> ImportReportRead:
    """Simulate (the default) or apply an exchange file; editors only, simulation included.

    Writing needs `dry_run=false`: a client that forgets destroys nothing. To
    confirm, send the same file again with `dry_run=false` — the report's
    `sha256` says it is the file that was simulated.
    """
    raw = await file.read(exchanges.max_bytes + 1)
    report = await exchanges.import_exchange(
        ImportContent(import_format, raw),
        ImportOptions(dry_run=dry_run, on_conflict=on_conflict, strict=strict),
    )
    return ImportReportRead.of(report)
```
(`status.HTTP_413_CONTENT_TOO_LARGE` : si l'installation de Starlette ne l'a pas, utiliser `413` littéral.)

`main.py` : `app.add_middleware(LimitingRequestBodies, path_suffix="/exchanges/imports", limit=settings.exchange_max_bytes + BODY_ENVELOPE_MARGIN)` placé **juste après** `app.add_middleware(AnsweringUnexpectedFailures)` et avant le `CORSMiddleware` (il reste ainsi à l'intérieur de CORS : le 413 porte les en-têtes CORS et l'identifiant de requête) ; passer `max_bytes=settings.exchange_max_bytes` aux deux constructions de `ExchangeService` ; importer `LimitingRequestBodies, BODY_ENVELOPE_MARGIN` depuis `ea.api.body_limit`. `backend/.env.example` : ajouter `# EA_EXCHANGE_MAX_BYTES=20971520`.

```python
# backend/tests/integration/test_exchange_roundtrip.py
"""export → import → export is the identity, against a real PostgreSQL."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import contextvars

import pytest
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine

from ea.db.postgres import create_session_factory
from ea.domain.archimate import AccessType
from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType as E
from ea.domain.archimate import RelationshipType as R
from ea.domain.errors import ImportInProgressError
from ea.domain.exchange import SOURCE_ID, Category, ExportRequest, ImportContent, ImportOptions
from ea.domain.exchange import ExchangeFormat
from ea.domain.ports import ElementFilter
from ea.repositories.architecture_store import PostgresArchitectureRepository
from ea.repositories.exchange_store import PostgresExchangeRepository
from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService
from ea.services.caller import acting_as
from ea.services.exchanges import ExchangeService
from tests.conftest import an_editor
from tests.exchange_files import DATA

pytestmark = [pytest.mark.asyncio, pytest.mark.postgres]
REAL = ImportOptions(dry_run=False)


@pytest.fixture
def stack(engine_at_head: AsyncEngine):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    sessions = create_session_factory(engine_at_head)
    repository = PostgresArchitectureRepository(sessions)
    architecture = ArchitectureService(repository)
    store = PostgresExchangeRepository(sessions)
    return repository, architecture, store, ExchangeService(store, architecture)


async def exported(exchange: ExchangeService) -> bytes:
    export = await exchange.export(ExportRequest())
    return b"".join([chunk async for chunk in export.chunks])


async def reference_model(architecture: ArchitectureService) -> None:
    """Every kind of content a round trip must carry, with and without a `source_id`."""
    actor = await architecture.create_element(
        element_type=E.BUSINESS_ACTOR, name='Alice & "Bob" <team>', description="Pays invoices",
        documentation="Long\ntext with é and 😀", properties={SOURCE_ID: "actor-1", "owner": "finance"},
    )
    app = await architecture.create_element(
        element_type=E.APPLICATION_COMPONENT, name="Billing", properties={"tier": "gold"}
    )
    run = await architecture.create_element(element_type=E.APPLICATION_FUNCTION, name="Invoice run")
    data = await architecture.create_element(element_type=E.DATA_OBJECT, name="Invoice")
    goal = await architecture.create_element(element_type=E.GOAL, name="Get paid")
    await architecture.connect(relationship_type=R.SERVING, source_id=app.id, target_id=actor.id,
                               name="serves")
    await architecture.connect(relationship_type=R.ACCESS, source_id=run.id, target_id=data.id,
                               access_type=AccessType.READ_WRITE)
    await architecture.connect(relationship_type=R.ASSOCIATION, source_id=actor.id,
                               target_id=app.id, directed=True)
    await architecture.connect(relationship_type=R.INFLUENCE, source_id=app.id, target_id=goal.id,
                               properties={"modifier": "++", "why": "revenue"})


async def empty_the_graph(repository: PostgresArchitectureRepository,
                          architecture: ArchitectureService) -> None:
    for element in await repository.list_elements(ElementFilter(limit=1000)):
        await architecture.delete_element(element.id)


async def test_export_import_export_is_byte_for_byte_the_same(stack) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    repository, architecture, _, exchange = stack
    await reference_model(architecture)
    first = await exported(exchange)

    await empty_the_graph(repository, architecture)
    report = await exchange.import_exchange(ImportContent(ExchangeFormat.ARCHIMATE_XML, first), REAL)

    assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS].created == 5
    assert report.counts[Category.RELATIONSHIPS].created == 4
    assert not [i for i in report.issues if i.severity.value == "error"]
    assert await exported(exchange) == first


async def test_importing_an_export_into_the_same_database_creates_nothing(stack) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    _, architecture, _, exchange = stack
    await reference_model(architecture)
    first = await exported(exchange)

    report = await exchange.import_exchange(ImportContent(ExchangeFormat.ARCHIMATE_XML, first), REAL)

    assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS].created == 0
    assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS].reused == 5
    assert report.counts[Category.RELATIONSHIPS].created == 0
    assert report.counts[Category.RELATIONSHIPS].reused == 4
    assert await exported(exchange) == first


async def test_a_dry_run_reports_what_the_real_import_does_and_changes_nothing(stack) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    repository, _, _, exchange = stack
    content = ImportContent(ExchangeFormat.ARCHIMATE_XML, (DATA / "minimal-model.xml").read_bytes())

    simulated = await exchange.import_exchange(content, ImportOptions())
    assert await repository.count_elements(ElementFilter()) == 0
    real = await exchange.import_exchange(content, REAL)

    assert (real.counts, real.issues) == (simulated.counts, simulated.issues)
    assert await repository.count_elements(ElementFilter()) == 3


async def test_a_refused_relation_leaves_the_rest_and_strict_leaves_nothing(stack) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    repository, _, _, exchange = stack
    content = ImportContent(ExchangeFormat.ARCHIMATE_XML, (DATA / "refused-relations.xml").read_bytes())

    strict = await exchange.import_exchange(content, ImportOptions(dry_run=False, strict=True))
    assert not strict.applied and await repository.count_elements(ElementFilter()) == 0

    report = await exchange.import_exchange(content, REAL)
    assert report.applied and await repository.count_elements(ElementFilter()) == 3
    assert [i.code for i in report.issues] == ["relationship_not_allowed"]


async def test_two_imports_at_once_one_gets_a_409(stack) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    _, _, store, exchange = stack
    content = ImportContent(ExchangeFormat.ARCHIMATE_XML, (DATA / "minimal-model.xml").read_bytes())

    async def second() -> None:
        # A fresh context holds no caller either: it is an editor's own request.
        with acting_as(an_editor()):
            await exchange.import_exchange(content, ImportOptions())

    async with store.transaction(commit=False):
        assert await store.try_import_lock()
        task = asyncio.create_task(second(), context=contextvars.Context())
        with pytest.raises(ImportInProgressError):
            await task
```

Note de l'implémenteur sur le deuxième test : les éléments natifs (sans `source_id`) reçoivent leur identifiant `id-<uuid>` à l'export ; à la réimportation dans la même base ils sont *réutilisés* et leur `source_id` est posé (« seulement s'il est vide »). Le deuxième export est alors identique au premier **parce que** l'identifiant `id-<uuid>` est inchangé ; l'assertion finale est donc valide et doit passer (c'est ce qu'elle vérifie).

```python
# backend/tests/unit/test_exchange_csv_writer.py
"""Writing the three Archi-style CSV files."""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import io
from collections import defaultdict
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Iterable
from typing import TypeVar

import pytest

from ea.domain.archimate import AccessType, ElementType, RelationshipType
from ea.domain.exchange import ExchangeElement, ExchangeRelationship
from ea.formats.csv_files import guard_cell, render_csv, unguard_cell

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio
T = TypeVar("T")


async def aiter_of(items: Iterable[T]) -> AsyncIterator[T]:
    for item in items:
        yield item


async def written(
    elements: Iterable[ExchangeElement] = (),
    relationships: Iterable[ExchangeRelationship] = (),
    *,
    safe_cells: bool = True,
) -> dict[str, list[list[str]]]:
    files: dict[str, str] = defaultdict(str)
    async for name, text in render_csv(
        aiter_of(elements), aiter_of(relationships), safe_cells=safe_cells
    ):
        files[name] += text
    return {name: list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(text, newline=""))) for name, text in files.items()}


def actor(**fields: object) -> ExchangeElement:
    return ExchangeElement("a-1", ElementType.BUSINESS_ACTOR, "Alice", **fields)  # type: ignore[arg-type]


async def test_the_headers_follow_the_archi_layout() -> None:
    files = await written()

    assert files["elements.csv"] == [["ID", "Type", "Name", "Documentation", "Specialization"]]
    assert files["relations.csv"] == [
        ["ID", "Type", "Name", "Documentation", "Source", "Target", "Specialization"]
    ]
    assert files["properties.csv"] == [["ID", "Key", "Value"]]


async def test_an_element_row_and_its_property_rows() -> None:
    files = await written([actor(description="Who pays", documentation="Doc", properties={"z": "1", "a": "2"})])

    assert files["elements.csv"][1] == ["a-1", "BusinessActor", "Alice", "Doc", ""]
    assert files["properties.csv"][1:] == [
        ["a-1", "ea.description", "Who pays"],
        ["a-1", "a", "2"],
        ["a-1", "z", "1"],
    ]


async def test_a_relationship_row_carries_its_type_with_the_relationship_suffix() -> None:
    link = ExchangeRelationship(
        "r-1", RelationshipType.ACCESS, "a-1", "b-1", name="reads",
        access_type=AccessType.READ_WRITE, properties={"why": "x"},
    )
    directed = ExchangeRelationship("r-2", RelationshipType.ASSOCIATION, "a-1", "b-1", directed=True)

    files = await written([actor()], [link, directed])

    assert files["relations.csv"][1] == ["r-1", "AccessRelationship", "reads", "", "a-1", "b-1", ""]
    assert files["properties.csv"][1:] == [
        ["r-1", "access_type", "read_write"],
        ["r-1", "why", "x"],
        ["r-2", "directed", "true"],
    ]


async def test_cells_that_would_run_as_a_formula_are_neutralised() -> None:
    files = await written([ExchangeElement("=1+1", ElementType.NODE, "=cmd|' /C calc'!A0")])

    assert files["elements.csv"][1][0] == "'=1+1"
    assert files["elements.csv"][1][2] == "'=cmd|' /C calc'!A0"


async def test_safe_cells_can_be_turned_off() -> None:
    files = await written([ExchangeElement("a", ElementType.NODE, "=raw")], safe_cells=False)

    assert files["elements.csv"][1][2] == "=raw"


async def test_commas_quotes_and_newlines_survive_quoting() -> None:
    files = await written([actor(documentation='one, "two"\nthree')])

    assert files["elements.csv"][1][3] == 'one, "two"\nthree'


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "value", ["=1+1", "+x", "-5", "@a", "'=a", "''-b", "plain", "", "'", "a=b", "-"]
)
def test_unguarding_undoes_guarding_exactly(value: str) -> None:
    assert unguard_cell(guard_cell(value)) == value


def test_only_dangerous_cells_are_changed() -> None:
    assert guard_cell("plain") == "plain"
    assert guard_cell("=x") == "'=x"
    assert guard_cell("'=x") == "''=x"
```

```python
ELEMENTS_FILE: Final = "elements.csv"
RELATIONS_FILE: Final = "relations.csv"
PROPERTIES_FILE: Final = "properties.csv"
CSV_FILES: Final = (ELEMENTS_FILE, RELATIONS_FILE, PROPERTIES_FILE)
MAX_CSV_ROWS: Final = 100_000

_RELATIONSHIP_SUFFIX: Final = "Relationship"


def lookup_element_type(raw: str) -> ElementType | None:
    """`BusinessActor` or `business_actor` — Archi writes the first, people the second."""
    found = _ELEMENTS_BY_NAME.get(raw.strip())
    if found is not None:
        return found
    try:
        candidate = ElementType(raw.strip())
    except ValueError:
        return None
    return None if candidate.is_connector else candidate


def lookup_relationship_type(raw: str) -> RelationshipType | None:
    """`CompositionRelationship`, `Composition` or `composition`."""
    name = raw.strip().removesuffix(_RELATIONSHIP_SUFFIX)
    found = _RELATIONSHIPS_BY_NAME.get(name)
    if found is not None:
        return found
    try:
        return RelationshipType(name)
    except ValueError:
        return None
```

`backend/src/ea/formats/csv_files.py` :

```python
"""Archi-style CSV (docs/adr/NN): `elements.csv`, `relations.csv` and `properties.csv`.

RFC 4180: UTF-8, comma, every cell quoted, CRLF. Cells that a spreadsheet would
run as a formula are neutralised on the way out and restored on the way in.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import io
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Callable, Iterator, Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Final

from ea.domain.archimate import RelationshipType
from ea.domain.exchange import (
    ELEMENTS_FILE,
    PROPERTIES_FILE,
    RELATIONS_FILE,
    ExchangeElement,
    ExchangeRelationship,
    exchange_name,
)

ELEMENTS_HEADER: Final = ("ID", "Type", "Name", "Documentation", "Specialization")
RELATIONS_HEADER: Final = (
    "ID", "Type", "Name", "Documentation", "Source", "Target", "Specialization",
)
PROPERTIES_HEADER: Final = ("ID", "Key", "Value")
_FORMULA: Final = ("=", "+", "-", "@")


def guard_cell(value: str) -> str:
    """Prefix a cell a spreadsheet would run as a formula with `'`.

    A cell that already starts with `'` followed by such a sign is prefixed too,
    so that `unguard_cell` — which removes exactly one leading `'` in both cases —
    gives every value back unchanged.
    """
    if value.startswith(_FORMULA) or (
        value.startswith("'") and value.lstrip("'").startswith(_FORMULA)
    ):
        return "'" + value
    return value


def unguard_cell(value: str) -> str:
    if value.startswith("'") and value.lstrip("'").startswith(_FORMULA):
        return value[1:]
    return value


def _row(cells: Sequence[str]) -> str:
    buffer = io.StringIO()
    csv.writer(buffer, quoting=csv.QUOTE_ALL, lineterminator="\r\n").writerow(cells)
    return buffer.getvalue()


def _identity(value: str) -> str:
    return value


def _property_rows(
    leading: Sequence[tuple[str, str]], properties: Mapping[str, str]
) -> Iterator[tuple[str, str]]:
    for key, value in leading:
        yield key, value
    for key in sorted(properties):
        yield key, properties[key]


async def render_csv(
    elements: AsyncIterator[ExchangeElement],
    relationships: AsyncIterator[ExchangeRelationship],
    *,
    safe_cells: bool,
) -> AsyncIterator[tuple[str, str]]:
    """The three files as `(file name, text)` pieces, a consumer routing by name."""
    guard: Callable[[str], str] = guard_cell if safe_cells else _identity
    yield ELEMENTS_FILE, _row(ELEMENTS_HEADER)
    yield RELATIONS_FILE, _row(RELATIONS_HEADER)
    yield PROPERTIES_FILE, _row(PROPERTIES_HEADER)
    async for item in elements:
        identifier = guard(item.identifier)
        yield ELEMENTS_FILE, _row(
            [identifier, exchange_name(item.element_type), guard(item.name),
             guard(item.documentation), ""]
        )
        leading = [("ea.description", item.description)] if item.description else []
        for key, value in _property_rows(leading, item.properties):
            yield PROPERTIES_FILE, _row([identifier, key, guard(value)])
    async for link in relationships:
        identifier = guard(link.identifier)
        yield RELATIONS_FILE, _row(
            [identifier, f"{exchange_name(link.relationship_type)}Relationship", guard(link.name),
             "", guard(link.source), guard(link.target), ""]
        )
        leading = []
        if link.relationship_type is RelationshipType.ACCESS and link.access_type is not None:
            leading.append(("access_type", link.access_type.value))
        if link.directed:
            leading.append(("directed", "true"))
        for key, value in _property_rows(leading, link.properties):
            yield PROPERTIES_FILE, _row([identifier, key, guard(value)])
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces : `parse_csv(main: bytes, relations: bytes | None = None, properties: bytes | None = None, *, safe_cells: bool = True) -> ExchangeModel` (`main` = `elements.csv`). Lève `InvalidExchangeFileError` : fichier vide, octet NUL, non UTF-8, en-tête absent ou incomplet, CSV invalide (ligne citée, jamais le contenu), plus de `MAX_CSV_ROWS` lignes. Chaque ligne lue porte `line` (numéro de ligne CSV).

```python
# backend/tests/unit/test_exchange_csv_reader.py
"""Reading the Archi-style CSV files: what is accepted, reported, and refused."""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import io

import pytest

from ea.domain.archimate import AccessType, ElementType, RelationshipType
from ea.domain.errors import InvalidExchangeFileError
from ea.domain.exchange import Category
from ea.formats.csv_files import parse_csv

ELEMENT_HEADER = ["ID", "Type", "Name", "Documentation", "Specialization"]
RELATION_HEADER = ["ID", "Type", "Name", "Documentation", "Source", "Target", "Specialization"]
PROPERTY_HEADER = ["ID", "Key", "Value"]


def table(*rows: list[str]) -> bytes:
    buffer = io.StringIO()
    csv.writer(buffer, quoting=csv.QUOTE_ALL, lineterminator="\r\n").writerows(rows)
    return buffer.getvalue().encode("utf-8")


def elements(*rows: list[str]) -> bytes:
    return table(ELEMENT_HEADER, *rows)


def codes(model) -> list[str]:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    return [issue.code for issue in model.issues]


class TestElements:
    def test_both_spellings_of_a_type_and_the_line_of_each_row(self) -> None:
        model = parse_csv(
            elements(["a", "BusinessActor", "Alice", "doc", ""], ["b", "application_component", "CRM", "", ""])
        )

        assert [(e.identifier, e.element_type, e.name, e.line) for e in model.elements] == [
            ("a", ElementType.BUSINESS_ACTOR, "Alice", 2),
            ("b", ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT, "CRM", 3),
        ]
        assert model.elements[0].documentation == "doc"

    def test_the_utf8_bom_is_accepted(self) -> None:
        raw = b"\xef\xbb\xbf" + elements(["a", "Node", "Café", "", ""])

        assert parse_csv(raw).elements[0].name == "Café"

    def test_a_quoted_cell_may_hold_commas_quotes_and_newlines(self) -> None:
        model = parse_csv(elements(["a", "Node", "N", 'one, "two"\nthree', ""]))

        assert model.elements[0].documentation == 'one, "two"\nthree'

    def test_a_neutralised_formula_is_restored_unless_safe_cells_is_off(self) -> None:
        raw = elements(["a", "Node", "'=cmd", "", ""])

        assert parse_csv(raw).elements[0].name == "=cmd"
        assert parse_csv(raw, safe_cells=False).elements[0].name == "'=cmd"

    def test_a_specialization_is_ignored_with_a_warning(self) -> None:
        model = parse_csv(elements(["a", "Node", "N", "", "Server"]))

        assert "specialization_ignored" in codes(model)

    def test_a_junction_is_set_aside_and_an_unknown_type_is_rejected(self) -> None:
        model = parse_csv(elements(["j", "AndJunction", "", "", ""], ["x", "Spaceship", "S", "", ""]))

        assert model.elements == ()
        assert [(u.identifier, u.ignored) for u in model.unreadable] == [("j", True), ("x", False)]

    def test_a_repeated_identifier_keeps_the_first(self) -> None:
        model = parse_csv(elements(["a", "Node", "One", "", ""], ["a", "Node", "Two", "", ""]))

        assert [e.name for e in model.elements] == ["One"]
        assert "duplicate_identifier" in codes(model)

    def test_a_short_row_is_read_with_empty_cells(self) -> None:
        model = parse_csv(table(ELEMENT_HEADER, ["a", "Node", "N"]))

        assert model.elements[0].documentation == ""


class TestRelationsAndProperties:
    def test_relations_read_both_type_spellings_and_both_ends(self) -> None:
        relations = table(
            RELATION_HEADER,
            ["r1", "CompositionRelationship", "", "", "a", "b", ""],
            ["r2", "serving", "serves", "doc", "a", "b", ""],
        )

        model = parse_csv(elements(["a", "Node", "A", "", ""], ["b", "Node", "B", "", ""]), relations)

        assert [(r.relationship_type, r.source, r.target) for r in model.relationships] == [
            (RelationshipType.COMPOSITION, "a", "b"),
            (RelationshipType.SERVING, "a", "b"),
        ]
        assert model.relationships[1].name == "serves"
        assert "documentation_dropped" in codes(model)

    def test_properties_reach_elements_and_relations_by_id(self) -> None:
        props = table(
            PROPERTY_HEADER,
            ["a", "owner", "finance"],
            ["a", "ea.description", "Who pays"],
            ["r1", "access_type", "read_write"],
            ["r1", "why", "x"],
            ["r2", "directed", "true"],
            ["r3", "access_type", "read"],  # r3 is not an access relationship
        )
        relations = table(
            RELATION_HEADER,
            ["r1", "Access", "", "", "a", "a", ""],
            ["r2", "Association", "", "", "a", "a", ""],
        )

        model = parse_csv(elements(["a", "Node", "A", "", ""]), relations, props)

        assert dict(model.elements[0].properties) == {"owner": "finance"}
        assert model.elements[0].description == "Who pays"
        access, association = model.relationships
        assert access.access_type is AccessType.READ_WRITE and dict(access.properties) == {"why": "x"}
        assert association.directed is True
        assert "unknown_property_owner" in codes(model)

    def test_an_access_without_a_stated_type_defaults_to_write(self) -> None:
        relations = table(RELATION_HEADER, ["r1", "Access", "", "", "a", "a", ""])

        model = parse_csv(elements(["a", "Node", "A", "", ""]), relations)

        assert model.relationships[0].access_type is AccessType.WRITE

    def test_a_relation_without_an_end_is_rejected(self) -> None:
        relations = table(RELATION_HEADER, ["r1", "Flow", "", "", "a", "", ""])

        model = parse_csv(elements(["a", "Node", "A", "", ""]), relations)

        assert model.relationships == ()
        assert model.unreadable[0].category is Category.RELATIONSHIPS


class TestRefusedFiles:
    def test_an_empty_file(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(InvalidExchangeFileError, match="empty"):
            parse_csv(b"")

    def test_a_nul_byte(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(InvalidExchangeFileError, match="NUL"):
            parse_csv(b'"ID","Type","Name"\r\n"a","Node","x\x00y"\r\n')

    def test_a_file_that_is_not_utf8(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(InvalidExchangeFileError, match="UTF-8"):
            parse_csv('"ID","Type","Name"\r\n"a","Node","Caf\xe9"\r\n'.encode("latin-1"))

    def test_a_header_missing_a_required_column(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(InvalidExchangeFileError, match="header"):
            parse_csv(table(["ID", "Name"], ["a", "x"]))

    def test_a_field_over_the_csv_module_limit_is_a_line_not_a_crash(self) -> None:
        huge = elements(["a", "Node", "N", "x" * 200_000, ""])

        with pytest.raises(InvalidExchangeFileError, match="line 2") as raised:
            parse_csv(huge)

        assert "xxxx" not in str(raised.value)

    def test_too_many_rows(self, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
        monkeypatch.setattr("ea.formats.csv_files.MAX_CSV_ROWS", 2)

        with pytest.raises(InvalidExchangeFileError, match="at most 2"):
            parse_csv(elements(*[[f"e{i}", "Node", f"n{i}", "", ""] for i in range(3)]))

    def test_a_relations_file_has_its_own_header_check(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(InvalidExchangeFileError, match="relations.csv"):
            parse_csv(elements(["a", "Node", "A", "", ""]), table(["ID", "Type"], ["r", "Flow"]))
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implémenter** (imports à ajouter en tête de `csv_files.py` : `from dataclasses import replace`, `from ea.domain.errors import InvalidExchangeFileError`, `from ea.domain.archimate import AccessType`, et depuis `ea.domain.exchange` : `ACCESS_BY_NAME`? non — `JUNCTION_NAMES, MAX_CSV_ROWS, MAX_EXCHANGE_ITEMS, Category, ExchangeModel, Issue, Severity, Unreadable, fold_properties, lookup_element_type, lookup_relationship_type`)

```python
def _decode(data: bytes, name: str) -> str:
    if not data.strip():
        msg = f"{name} is empty"
        raise InvalidExchangeFileError(msg)
    if b"\x00" in data:
        msg = f"{name} holds a NUL byte"
        raise InvalidExchangeFileError(msg)
    try:
        return data.decode("utf-8-sig")
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        msg = f"{name} is not UTF-8"
        raise InvalidExchangeFileError(msg) from None


def _table(
    data: bytes, name: str, required: tuple[str, ...]
) -> list[tuple[int, dict[str, str]]]:
    """The rows of one file keyed by lower-cased header, with the line each came from."""
    reader = csv.reader(io.StringIO(_decode(data, name), newline=""))
    try:
        header = [cell.strip().lower() for cell in next(reader)]
    except StopIteration:
        msg = f"{name} has no header"
        raise InvalidExchangeFileError(msg) from None
    except csv.Error:
        msg = f"{name} is not valid CSV (line 1)"
        raise InvalidExchangeFileError(msg) from None
    if any(column not in header for column in required):
        msg = f"the header of {name} must name: {', '.join(required)}"
        raise InvalidExchangeFileError(msg)
    rows: list[tuple[int, dict[str, str]]] = []
    try:
        for cells in reader:
            if not any(cell.strip() for cell in cells):
                continue
            if len(rows) >= MAX_CSV_ROWS:
                msg = f"{name} holds at most {MAX_CSV_ROWS} rows"
                raise InvalidExchangeFileError(msg)
            padded = [*cells, *[""] * max(0, len(header) - len(cells))]  # a short row has empty cells
            rows.append((reader.line_num, dict(zip(header, padded, strict=False))))
    except csv.Error:
        msg = f"{name} is not valid CSV (line {reader.line_num})"
        raise InvalidExchangeFileError(msg) from None
    return rows


def parse_csv(
    main: bytes,
    relations: bytes | None = None,
    properties: bytes | None = None,
    *,
    safe_cells: bool = True,
) -> ExchangeModel:
    """Read `elements.csv` (+ `relations.csv`, `properties.csv`) into an `ExchangeModel`."""
    unguard: Callable[[str], str] = unguard_cell if safe_cells else _identity
    issues: list[Issue] = []
    unreadable: list[Unreadable] = []
    owners: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]] = {}
    if properties is not None:
        for _, row in _table(properties, PROPERTIES_FILE, ("id", "key", "value")):
            owners.setdefault(unguard(row["id"]), []).append((row["key"], unguard(row["value"])))

    seen: set[str] = set()
    elements: list[ExchangeElement] = []
    for line, row in _table(main, ELEMENTS_FILE, ("id", "type", "name")):
        identifier = unguard(row["id"]).strip()
        kind = row["type"].strip()

        def note(issue: Issue, line: int = line, identifier: str = identifier) -> None:
            issues.append(replace(issue, source_id=identifier or None, line=line))

        if not identifier:
            note(Issue(Severity.ERROR, "missing_identifier", "a row has no ID"))
            unreadable.append(Unreadable(Category.ELEMENTS, f"line {line}"))
            continue
        if identifier in seen:
            note(Issue(Severity.ERROR, "duplicate_identifier", "this ID is already used; the row is ignored"))
            unreadable.append(Unreadable(Category.ELEMENTS, identifier, cascade=False))
            continue
        seen.add(identifier)
        if kind in JUNCTION_NAMES or kind.lower() == "junction":
            note(Issue(Severity.INFO, "junction_not_supported", "junctions cannot be stored; ignored"))
            unreadable.append(Unreadable(Category.ELEMENTS, identifier, ignored=True))
            continue
        element_type = lookup_element_type(kind)
        if element_type is None:
            note(Issue(Severity.ERROR, "unknown_type", f"{kind!r} is not an ArchiMate element type"))
            unreadable.append(Unreadable(Category.ELEMENTS, identifier))
            continue
        if row.get("specialization", "").strip():
            note(Issue(Severity.WARNING, "specialization_ignored", "the Specialization column is not stored"))
        folded = fold_properties(owners.get(identifier, ()))
        elements.append(
            ExchangeElement(
                identifier=identifier,
                element_type=element_type,
                name=unguard(row["name"]),
                description=folded.description,
                documentation=unguard(row.get("documentation", "")),
                properties=folded.properties,
                line=line,
            )
        )
        for issue in folded.issues:
            note(issue)

    links: list[ExchangeRelationship] = []
    if relations is not None:
        for line, row in _table(relations, RELATIONS_FILE, ("id", "type", "source", "target")):
            identifier = unguard(row["id"]).strip()
            kind = row["type"].strip()

            def note_link(issue: Issue, line: int = line, identifier: str = identifier) -> None:
                issues.append(replace(issue, source_id=identifier or None, line=line))

            if not identifier or identifier in seen:
                code = "missing_identifier" if not identifier else "duplicate_identifier"
                note_link(Issue(Severity.ERROR, code, "this relation has no usable ID; the row is ignored"))
                unreadable.append(
                    Unreadable(Category.RELATIONSHIPS, identifier or f"line {line}", cascade=not identifier)
                )
                continue
            seen.add(identifier)
            relationship_type = lookup_relationship_type(kind)
            source, target = unguard(row["source"]).strip(), unguard(row["target"]).strip()
            if relationship_type is None or not source or not target:
                note_link(
                    Issue(Severity.ERROR,
                          "unknown_type" if relationship_type is None else "missing_end",
                          f"{kind!r} is not an ArchiMate relationship type"
                          if relationship_type is None
                          else "a relation needs a Source and a Target")
                )
                unreadable.append(Unreadable(Category.RELATIONSHIPS, identifier))
                continue
            pairs = owners.get(identifier, [])
            stated = dict(pairs)
            rest = [(k, v) for k, v in pairs if k not in ("access_type", "directed")]
            folded = fold_properties(rest)
            access_type = None
            if relationship_type is RelationshipType.ACCESS:
                access_type = (
                    _access_named(stated.get("access_type")) or AccessType.WRITE
                )
            if row.get("documentation", "").strip():
                note_link(Issue(Severity.INFO, "documentation_dropped",
                                "a relationship has no documentation field; it is not stored"))
            links.append(
                ExchangeRelationship(
                    identifier=identifier,
                    relationship_type=relationship_type,
                    source=source,
                    target=target,
                    name=unguard(row.get("name", "")),
                    access_type=access_type,
                    directed=relationship_type is RelationshipType.ASSOCIATION
                    and stated.get("directed", "").lower() in ("true", "1"),
                    properties=folded.properties,
                    line=line,
                )
            )
            for issue in folded.issues:
                note_link(issue)

    if len(elements) + len(links) > MAX_EXCHANGE_ITEMS:
        msg = f"an exchange file holds at most {MAX_EXCHANGE_ITEMS} elements and links"
        raise InvalidExchangeFileError(msg)
    for owner in sorted(set(owners) - seen):
        issues.append(Issue(Severity.WARNING, "unknown_property_owner",
                            "properties.csv names an ID that elements.csv and relations.csv do not", owner))
    return ExchangeModel(tuple(elements), tuple(links), tuple(unreadable), tuple(issues))


def _access_named(raw: str | None) -> AccessType | None:
    try:
        return AccessType(raw) if raw else None
    except ValueError:
        return None
```
(Le message d'en-tête incomplet contient « header » et le nom du fichier : les tests ciblent `match="header"` et `match="relations.csv"`.)

```python
import io
import zipfile

from ea.domain.exchange import Category, ExchangeFormat, ImportContent, ImportOptions

CSV = ExportRequest(format=ExchangeFormat.CSV)


async def zip_of(service: ExchangeService, request: ExportRequest = CSV) -> bytes:
    export = await service.export(request)
    return b"".join([chunk async for chunk in export.chunks])


class TestCsvExport:
    async def test_it_is_a_zip_of_the_three_files_named_by_the_server(
        self, service: ArchitectureService, exchange_service: ExchangeService
    ) -> None:

        export = await exchange_service.export(CSV)

        assert export.media_type == "application/zip"
        assert export.filename == "ea-model-2026-09-07.zip"
        archive = zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(b"".join([c async for c in export.chunks])))
        assert archive.namelist() == ["elements.csv", "relations.csv", "properties.csv"]
        rows = archive.read("elements.csv").decode("utf-8")
        assert '"app-1","ApplicationComponent","Billing"' in rows

    async def test_two_exports_are_byte_identical_including_the_archive(
        self, service: ArchitectureService, exchange_service: ExchangeService
    ) -> None:

        assert await zip_of(exchange_service) == await zip_of(exchange_service)

    async def test_a_formula_in_a_name_is_neutralised_unless_asked_not_to(
        self, service: ArchitectureService, exchange_service: ExchangeService
    ) -> None:

            io.BytesIO(await zip_of(exchange_service, ExportRequest(format=ExchangeFormat.CSV, safe_cells=False)))
        )

class TestCsvImport:

        self, service: ArchitectureService, exchange_service: ExchangeService
    ) -> None:
        await two_linked_elements(service)
        archive = zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(await zip_of(exchange_service)))
        # a different, empty catalogue to import into
        for element in list((await service.list_elements(ElementFilter(limit=100)))):
            await service.delete_element(element.id)

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(
            ImportContent(
                ExchangeFormat.CSV,
                archive.read("elements.csv"),

        assert report.counts[Category.RELATIONSHIPS].created == 1
        billing = next(e for e in await service.list_elements(ElementFilter(limit=100)) if e.name == "Billing")
        assert (billing.description, dict(billing.properties)["owner"]) == ("Invoices", "finance")
```

`services/exchanges.py` — remplacer `export` et `_xml` (Task 5) par ce qui suit, et ajouter `_csv` (imports : `shutil`, `tempfile`, `zipfile`, `ExchangeFormat`, `CSV_FILES`, `render_csv`, `parse_csv`) :

```python
    async def export(self, request: ExportRequest) -> Export:
        require_caller()
        is_csv = request.format is ExchangeFormat.CSV
        return Export(
            filename=f"ea-{request.scope.label}-{self._now():%Y-%m-%d}.{'zip' if is_csv else 'xml'}",
            media_type="application/zip" if is_csv else "application/xml",
            chunks=self._csv(request) if is_csv else self._xml(request),
        )

    async def _xml(self, request: ExportRequest) -> AsyncIterator[bytes]:
        async with self._repository.snapshot(request.scope) as snapshot:
            streams = _Streams(snapshot, self._monotonic)
            async for chunk in buffered(
                render_model("EA catalogue", streams.elements(), streams.relationships())
            ):
                yield chunk
        self._log_export(request, streams)

    async def _csv(self, request: ExportRequest) -> AsyncIterator[bytes]:
        # ponytail: three spooled files then one archive, written synchronously on the
        # loop — fine to the 20 MB ceiling; stream a zip64 writer if exports outgrow it.
        spools = {name: tempfile.SpooledTemporaryFile(max_size=1 << 20) for name in CSV_FILES}  # noqa: SIM115
        archive = tempfile.SpooledTemporaryFile(max_size=1 << 20)  # noqa: SIM115
        try:
            async with self._repository.snapshot(request.scope) as snapshot:
                streams = _Streams(snapshot, self._monotonic)
                async for name, text in render_csv(
                    streams.elements(), streams.relationships(), safe_cells=request.safe_cells
                ):
                    spools[name].write(text.encode("utf-8"))
            with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, "w") as bundle:
                for name in CSV_FILES:
                    # A fixed date: the archive is a pure function of the model.
                    info = zipfile.ZipInfo(name, date_time=(1980, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0))
                    info.compress_type = zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED
                    info.external_attr = 0o644 << 16
                    spools[name].seek(0)
                    with bundle.open(info, "w") as entry:
                        shutil.copyfileobj(spools[name], entry)
            archive.seek(0)
            while chunk := archive.read(65_536):
                yield chunk
            self._log_export(request, streams)
        finally:
            archive.close()
            for spool in spools.values():
                spool.close()

    @staticmethod
    def _log_export(request: ExportRequest, streams: _Streams) -> None:
        logger.info(
            "model exported",
            extra={"action": "exported", "format": request.format.value, **streams.counts},
        )
```

et la classe aide (en bas du module, remplaçant la fonction `_relationships` de la Task 5) :

```python
class _Streams:
    """The two paged iterators of one export. The second needs what the first learns:
    a link is written with the identifiers of its ends, so elements are read first."""

    def __init__(self, snapshot: ExportSnapshot, monotonic: Callable[[], float]) -> None:
        self._snapshot = snapshot
        self._monotonic = monotonic
        self._deadline = monotonic() + EXPORT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
        self.identifiers: dict[UUID, str] = {}
        self.counts = {"elements": 0, "relationships": 0}

    def _within_budget(self) -> None:
        if self._monotonic() > self._deadline:
            msg = f"the export ran past its {EXPORT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:.0f} s budget"
            raise TimeoutError(msg)

    async def elements(self) -> AsyncIterator[ExchangeElement]:
        offset = 0
        while page := await self._snapshot.elements_page(offset, EXPORT_PAGE_SIZE):
            self._within_budget()
            for element in page:
                item = exchange_element_of(element)
                self.identifiers[element.id] = item.identifier
                self.counts["elements"] += 1
                yield item
            offset += len(page)

    async def relationships(self) -> AsyncIterator[ExchangeRelationship]:
        offset = 0
        while page := await self._snapshot.relationships_page(offset, EXPORT_PAGE_SIZE):
            self._within_budget()
            for link in page:
                # A link with an end outside the scope is not exported (spec §11).
                if link.source_id in self.identifiers and link.target_id in self.identifiers:
                    self.counts["relationships"] += 1
                    yield exchange_relationship_of(
                        link,
                        source=self.identifiers[link.source_id],
                        target=self.identifiers[link.target_id],
                    )
            offset += len(page)
```
(Le test de budget de la Task 5 reste valable : `_Streams.__init__` consomme le premier tick `0.0`, `_within_budget` le second `10000.0`.) `_read` devient :

```python
        if content.format is ExchangeFormat.CSV:
            return parse_csv(
                content.main, content.relations, content.properties, safe_cells=options.safe_cells
            )
        return parse_model(content.main)
```

`api/exchanges.py` — remplacer la route d'import :

```python
@router.post("/imports", response_model=ImportReportRead, responses=IMPORT_ERRORS)
async def import_model(
    exchanges: Exchanges,
    file: Annotated[UploadFile | None, File(description="The XML file (`archimate-xml`).")] = None,
    elements: Annotated[UploadFile | None, File(description="`elements.csv` (`csv`).")] = None,
    relations: Annotated[UploadFile | None, File(description="`relations.csv`.")] = None,
    properties: Annotated[UploadFile | None, File(description="`properties.csv`.")] = None,
    import_format: Annotated[ExchangeFormat, Form(alias="format")] = ExchangeFormat.ARCHIMATE_XML,
    dry_run: Annotated[bool, Form()] = True,
    on_conflict: Annotated[ConflictPolicy, Form()] = ConflictPolicy.REUSE,
    strict: Annotated[bool, Form()] = False,
    safe_cells: Annotated[bool, Form()] = True,
) -> ImportReportRead:
    """Simulate (the default) or apply an exchange file; editors only, simulation included.

    Writing needs `dry_run=false`: a client that forgets destroys nothing. To
    confirm, send the same file(s) again with `dry_run=false` — the report's
    `sha256` says it is what was simulated. XML takes `file`; CSV takes
    `elements` and, optionally, `relations` and `properties`.
    """
    cap = exchanges.max_bytes + 1
    if import_format is ExchangeFormat.CSV:
        if elements is None:
            msg = "a CSV import needs the `elements` file"
            raise InvalidExchangeFileError(msg)
        content = ImportContent(
            import_format,
            await elements.read(cap),
            await relations.read(cap) if relations is not None else None,
            await properties.read(cap) if properties is not None else None,
        )
    else:
        if file is None:
            msg = "an XML import needs the `file` part"
            raise InvalidExchangeFileError(msg)
        content = ImportContent(import_format, await file.read(cap))
    report = await exchanges.import_exchange(
        content,
        ImportOptions(dry_run=dry_run, on_conflict=on_conflict, strict=strict, safe_cells=safe_cells),
    )
    return ImportReportRead.of(report)
```

- Modify: `backend/tests/integration/test_exchange_roundtrip.py` (ajouts)

**Interfaces:** consomme le lot 3 complet sur la base jetable.

- [ ] **Step 1: Écrire les tests**

```python
import io
import zipfile

CSV_EXPORT = ExportRequest(format=ExchangeFormat.CSV)


async def csv_parts(exchange: ExchangeService) -> dict[str, bytes]:
    export = await exchange.export(CSV_EXPORT)
    archive = zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(b"".join([c async for c in export.chunks])))
    return {name: archive.read(name) for name in archive.namelist()}


async def test_csv_export_import_export_gives_the_same_three_files(stack) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    repository, architecture, _, exchange = stack
    await reference_model(architecture)
    await architecture.create_element(
        element_type=E.NODE, name="=SUM(A1)", documentation="+1\n-2", properties={"note": "@home"}
    )
    first = await csv_parts(exchange)

    await empty_the_graph(repository, architecture)
    report = await exchange.import_exchange(
        ImportContent(
            ExchangeFormat.CSV, first["elements.csv"], first["relations.csv"], first["properties.csv"]
        ),
        REAL,
    )

    assert not [i for i in report.issues if i.severity.value == "error"]
    assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS].created == 6
    assert report.counts[Category.RELATIONSHIPS].created == 4
    assert await csv_parts(exchange) == first


async def test_csv_import_of_its_own_export_into_the_same_database_creates_nothing(stack) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    _, architecture, _, exchange = stack
    await reference_model(architecture)
    first = await csv_parts(exchange)

    report = await exchange.import_exchange(
        ImportContent(
            ExchangeFormat.CSV, first["elements.csv"], first["relations.csv"], first["properties.csv"]
        ),
        REAL,
    )

    assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS].created == 0
    assert report.counts[Category.ELEMENTS].reused == 5
    assert report.counts[Category.RELATIONSHIPS].created == 0
```

### Task 22: Rendu des vues dans le XML

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/src/ea/domain/exchange.py` (fin de fichier), `backend/src/ea/formats/archimate_xml.py` (`render_model`, nouvelle `view_xml`)
- Test: `backend/tests/unit/test_archimate_xml_writer.py` (ajouts)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3.
- Produces (domain) : `ExchangeBox(element: str, x: float, y: float, width: float, height: float)` ; `ExchangeView(identifier: str, name: str, boxes: tuple[ExchangeBox, ...], description: str = "", connections_ignored: int = 0, path: str | None = None)`. (formats) : `render_model(name, elements, relationships, *, views: AsyncIterator[ExchangeView] | None = None)` ; la section `<views><diagrams>` vient **après** `propertyDefinitions` (ordre du XSD) et est omise sans vue ; chaque boîte est un `<node xsi:type="Element" elementRef=… x y w h>` d'identifiant `<identifiant de la vue>-<identifiant de l'élément>` ; coordonnées arrondies à l'entier (Exchange ne connaît que des entiers) ; aucune `<connection>` n'est jamais écrite.

- [ ] **Step 1: Écrire les tests qui échouent** (ajouter à `test_archimate_xml_writer.py` ; importer `ExchangeBox, ExchangeView`)

```python
async def render_with_views(views: Iterable[ExchangeView]) -> str:
    parts = render_model(
        "M", aiter_of([actor("a-1")]), aiter_of([]), views=aiter_of(views)
    )
    return "".join([part async for part in parts])


async def test_a_view_lists_its_boxes_with_integer_geometry() -> None:
    view = ExchangeView(
        "view-Overview", "Overview", (ExchangeBox("a-1", 10.4, 20.6, 120.0, 55.0),), "Main picture"
    )

    root = ET.fromstring(await render_with_views([view]))

    node_view = root.find("x:views/x:diagrams/x:view", NS)
    assert node_view is not None and node_view.get("identifier") == "view-Overview"
    assert node_view.get(XSI_TYPE) == "Diagram"
    assert node_view.find("x:name", NS).text == "Overview"  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    assert node_view.find("x:documentation", NS).text == "Main picture"  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    box = node_view.find("x:node", NS)
    assert box is not None
    assert (box.get("identifier"), box.get("elementRef"), box.get(XSI_TYPE)) == (
        "view-Overview-a-1", "a-1", "Element",
    )
    assert (box.get("x"), box.get("y"), box.get("w"), box.get("h")) == ("10", "21", "120", "55")
    assert node_view.find("x:connection", NS) is None


async def test_views_come_last_and_a_model_without_views_has_no_views_section() -> None:
    with_views = ET.fromstring(
        await render_with_views([ExchangeView("view-V", "V", ())])
    )
    without = ET.fromstring(await render([actor()]))

    assert [c.tag.split("}")[1] for c in with_views][-1] == "views"
    assert without.find("x:views", NS) is None
```

`domain/exchange.py` (fin de fichier) :

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ExchangeBox:
    """One element drawn on a view: where, and how big. `element` is an identifier."""

    element: str
    x: float
    y: float
    width: float
    height: float


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ExchangeView:
    """A view of the file. A view owns no fact: only its boxes are ever stored."""

    identifier: str
    name: str
    boxes: tuple[ExchangeBox, ...]
    description: str = ""
    #: Connections and shapes the reader set aside (a view link is never a fact).
    connections_ignored: int = 0
    shapes_ignored: int = 0
    path: str | None = None
```

`archimate_xml.py` — importer `ExchangeView` ; ajouter :

```python
def view_xml(view: ExchangeView) -> str:
    body = _lang("name", view.name)
    if view.description:
        body += _lang("documentation", view.description)
    for box in view.boxes:
        body += (
            f'<node identifier="{_attr(view.identifier)}-{_attr(box.element)}" '
            f'elementRef="{_attr(box.element)}" xsi:type="Element" '
            f'x="{round(box.x)}" y="{round(box.y)}" w="{round(box.width)}" h="{round(box.height)}"/>'
        )
    return f'<view identifier="{_attr(view.identifier)}" xsi:type="Diagram">{body}</view>'
```

`render_model` : ajouter le paramètre `*, views: AsyncIterator[ExchangeView] | None = None` et, juste avant `yield "</model>\n"` :

```python
    if views is not None:
        opened = False
        async for view in views:
            if not opened:
                yield "<views><diagrams>"
                opened = True
            yield view_xml(view)
        if opened:
            yield "</diagrams></views>"
```

```python
from ea.db.models.diagram import DiagramNodeRecord, DiagramRecord


async def test_views_come_ordered_by_name_with_their_boxes_in_identifier_order(
    exchange: PostgresExchangeRepository, graph_service: ArchitectureService, engine_at_head: AsyncEngine
) -> None:
    from datetime import UTC, datetime
    from uuid import uuid4

    from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import async_sessionmaker

    first = await graph_service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="b", properties={SOURCE_ID: "z-last"})
    second = await graph_service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="a", properties={SOURCE_ID: "a-first"})
    diagram_id, now = uuid4(), datetime.now(UTC)
    async with async_sessionmaker(engine_at_head).begin() as session:
        session.add(DiagramRecord(id=diagram_id, name="V", description="d", created_at=now, updated_at=now))
        await session.flush()
        session.add_all(
            DiagramNodeRecord(diagram_id=diagram_id, element_id=e.id, x=1, y=2, width=3, height=4)
            for e in (first, second)
        )

    async with exchange.snapshot(ExportScope()) as snapshot:
        (view,) = await snapshot.views_page(0, 10)

    assert (view.name, view.description) == ("V", "d")
    assert [box.element_id for box in view.boxes] == [second.id, first.id]
```

`exchange_store.py` (imports `DiagramNodeRecord, DiagramRecord` depuis `ea.db.models.diagram`, `ExportedBox, ExportedView`) — dans `_Snapshot` :

```python
    async def views_page(self, offset: int, limit: int) -> tuple[ExportedView, ...]:
        diagrams = (
            await self._session.execute(
                select(DiagramRecord.id, DiagramRecord.name, DiagramRecord.description)
                .order_by(DiagramRecord.name, DiagramRecord.id)
                .offset(offset)
                .limit(limit)
            )
        ).all()
        if not diagrams:
            return ()
        boxes = await self._session.execute(
            select(
                DiagramNodeRecord.diagram_id, DiagramNodeRecord.element_id, DiagramNodeRecord.x,
                DiagramNodeRecord.y, DiagramNodeRecord.width, DiagramNodeRecord.height,
            )
            .join(ElementRecord, ElementRecord.id == DiagramNodeRecord.element_id)
            .where(DiagramNodeRecord.diagram_id.in_([d.id for d in diagrams]))
            .order_by(DiagramNodeRecord.diagram_id, _identifier(ElementRecord))
        )
        by_diagram: dict[UUID, list[ExportedBox]] = {d.id: [] for d in diagrams}
        for diagram_id, element_id, x, y, width, height in boxes:
            by_diagram[diagram_id].append(ExportedBox(element_id, x, y, width, height))
        return tuple(
            ExportedView(d.id, d.name, d.description, tuple(by_diagram[d.id])) for d in diagrams
        )
```

Service — dans `_Streams` (champ `self.skipped_views = 0`) :

```python
    async def views(self, with_views: bool) -> AsyncIterator[ExchangeView]:
        if not with_views:
            return
        taken: set[str] = set()
        offset = 0
        while page := await self._snapshot.views_page(offset, EXPORT_PAGE_SIZE):
            self._within_budget()
            for view in page:
                # A view whose boxes are not all in the export would be a different picture.
                if any(box.element_id not in self.identifiers for box in view.boxes):
                    self.skipped_views += 1
                    continue
                identifier = base = xml_identifier(f"view-{view.name}")
                suffix = 2
                while identifier in taken:
                    identifier, suffix = f"{base}-{suffix}", suffix + 1
                taken.add(identifier)
                self.counts["views"] += 1
                yield ExchangeView(
                    identifier=identifier,
                    name=view.name,
                    description=view.description,
                    boxes=tuple(
                        ExchangeBox(self.identifiers[b.element_id], b.x, b.y, b.width, b.height)
                        for b in view.boxes
                    ),
                )
            offset += len(page)
```
(`self.counts = {"elements": 0, "relationships": 0, "views": 0}` ; importer `ExchangeBox, ExchangeView, xml_identifier`.) Dans `_xml`, passer `views=streams.views(request.with_views)` à `render_model(…)`. **Ordre de consommation** : `render_model` lit les éléments, puis les relations, puis les vues : `identifiers` est complet quand les vues arrivent.

Doubles (`tests/conftest.py`) : `InMemoryExchange.__init__(self, repository, diagrams: InMemoryDiagrams | None = None)` ; `_copy`/`_restore` copient aussi `diagrams.diagrams` et `diagrams.nodes` quand `diagrams` est fourni ; `_MemorySnapshot.__init__(self, repository, diagrams)` et

```python
    async def views_page(self, offset: int, limit: int) -> tuple[ExportedView, ...]:
        if self._diagrams is None:
            return ()
        ordered = sorted(self._diagrams.diagrams.values(), key=lambda d: (d.name, str(d.id)))
        elements = self._repository.elements

        def boxes(diagram_id: UUID) -> tuple[ExportedBox, ...]:
            placed = sorted(
                self._diagrams.nodes.get(diagram_id, ()),  # type: ignore[union-attr]
                key=lambda n: self._identifier(elements[n.element_id]),
            )
            return tuple(ExportedBox(n.element_id, n.x, n.y, n.width, n.height) for n in placed)

        return tuple(
            ExportedView(d.id, d.name, d.description, boxes(d.id)) for d in ordered[offset : offset + limit]
        )
```
fixtures : `exchange_store(repository, diagrams)` passe `diagrams` ; `exchange_service(exchange_store, service)` inchangé.

```python
class TestViews:
    def test_boxes_are_flattened_to_absolute_coordinates_and_shapes_are_counted(self) -> None:
        model = parse_model((DATA / "views-model.xml").read_bytes())

        (view,) = model.views
        assert (view.identifier, view.name, view.description) == ("v1", "Overview", "Main picture")
        by_element = {box.element: box for box in view.boxes}
        # n1 sits inside the container at (100, 100): 10 + 100, 20 + 100
        assert (by_element["e1"].x, by_element["e1"].y) == (110.0, 120.0)
        assert (by_element["e1"].width, by_element["e1"].height) == (120.0, 55.0)
        assert [b.element for b in view.boxes] == ["e1", "e2", "ghost", "e1"]
        assert (view.connections_ignored, view.shapes_ignored) == (1, 2)

    def test_a_box_with_a_bad_coordinate_is_skipped_with_a_warning(self) -> None:
        xml = exchange_xml(
            el("a", "Node", "A"),
            extra='<views><diagrams><view identifier="v" xsi:type="Diagram"><name>V</name>'
            '<node identifier="n" elementRef="a" xsi:type="Element" x="oops" y="0" w="1" h="1"/>'
            "</view></diagrams></views>",
        )

        model = parse_model(xml)

        assert model.views[0].boxes == ()
        assert "invalid_box" in codes(model)

    def test_a_view_without_a_name_or_with_a_repeated_identifier_is_rejected(self) -> None:
        xml = exchange_xml(
            extra='<views><diagrams><view identifier="v" xsi:type="Diagram"><name>A</name></view>'
            '<view identifier="v" xsi:type="Diagram"><name>B</name></view>'
            '<view xsi:type="Diagram"><name>C</name></view></diagrams></views>'
        )

        model = parse_model(xml)

        assert [v.name for v in model.views] == ["A"]
        assert {u.category for u in model.unreadable} == {Category.VIEWS}
        assert {"duplicate_identifier", "missing_identifier"} <= set(codes(model))

    def test_nodes_nested_beyond_the_ceiling_are_refused(self) -> None:
        inner = "".join('<node identifier="n%d" xsi:type="Container" x="0" y="0" w="1" h="1">' % i for i in range(201))
        xml = exchange_xml(
            extra='<views><diagrams><view identifier="v" xsi:type="Diagram"><name>V</name>'
            + inner + "</node>" * 201 + "</view></diagrams></views>"
        )

        with pytest.raises(InvalidExchangeFileError, match="nested"):
            parse_model(xml)
```

et

```python
def _boxes(view: Element, issues: list[Issue]) -> tuple[list[ExchangeBox], int, int]:
    """The boxes of a view in absolute coordinates; shapes and connections only counted.

    Iterative, with the depth bounded, so a file nested a hundred thousand
    levels deep cannot exhaust the interpreter's stack.
    """
    boxes: list[ExchangeBox] = []
    shapes = 0
    stack = [(child, 0.0, 0.0, 1) for child in reversed(view.findall(_q("node")))]
    while stack:
        node, dx, dy, depth = stack.pop()
        if depth > MAX_ORGANIZATION_DEPTH:
            raise _refuse(f"view nodes are nested more than {MAX_ORGANIZATION_DEPTH} levels deep")
        try:
            x, y = float(node.get("x", "0")) + dx, float(node.get("y", "0")) + dy
            width, height = float(node.get("w", "0")), float(node.get("h", "0"))
        except ValueError:
            issues.append(Issue(Severity.WARNING, "invalid_box", "a node has a coordinate that is not a number; skipped"))
            continue
        if node.get(_XSI_TYPE) == "Element" and node.get("elementRef"):
            boxes.append(ExchangeBox(node.get("elementRef", ""), x, y, width, height))
        else:
            shapes += 1
        stack.extend((child, x, y, depth + 1) for child in reversed(node.findall(_q("node"))))
    return boxes, len(view.findall(_q("connection"))), shapes
```
(importer `MAX_ORGANIZATION_DEPTH, ExchangeBox, ExchangeView`.)


Service — `__init__` gagne `diagrams: DiagramService | None = None` (importer `DiagramService` sous `TYPE_CHECKING`), `self._diagrams = diagrams` ; dans `_apply`, après `_apply_relationships` : `await self._apply_views(model, options, report, batch)` ; importer `MAX_VIEW_BOXES, ExchangeView, DuplicateDiagramError, DiagramNode` :

```python
    async def _apply_views(
        self, model: ExchangeModel, options: ImportOptions, report: ReportBuilder, batch: _Batch
    ) -> None:
        for view in model.views:
            def note(severity: Severity, code: str, message: str, view: ExchangeView = view) -> None:
                report.add(Issue(severity, code, message, view.identifier, path=view.path))

            if not options.with_views or self._diagrams is None:
                report.count(Category.VIEWS, Outcome.IGNORED)
                continue
            if len(view.boxes) > MAX_VIEW_BOXES:
                report.count(Category.VIEWS, Outcome.REJECTED)
                note(Severity.ERROR, "view_too_large", f"a view has at most {MAX_VIEW_BOXES} boxes")
                continue
            nodes: list[DiagramNode] = []
            placed: set[UUID] = set()
            for box in view.boxes:
                target = None if box.element in batch.refused else batch.resolve(box.element)
                if target is None:
                    note(Severity.WARNING, "box_omitted", "a box shows an element that was not imported; omitted")
                elif target in placed:
                    note(Severity.WARNING, "box_duplicate", "an element is drawn twice on this view; the first box is kept")
                else:
                    placed.add(target)
                    nodes.append(DiagramNode(target, box.x, box.y, box.width, box.height))
            try:
                async with self._repository.savepoint():
                    diagram = await self._create_diagram(view)
                    await self._diagrams.replace_layout(diagram.id, nodes)
            except (DomainError, ValueError) as refusal:
                report.count(Category.VIEWS, Outcome.REJECTED)
                report.add(_refusal(refusal, view.identifier, None, view.path))
                continue
            report.count(Category.VIEWS, Outcome.CREATED)
            if view.connections_ignored:
                note(Severity.INFO, "connections_ignored",
                     f"{view.connections_ignored} connection(s) not imported: a view link is never a fact")
            if view.shapes_ignored:
                note(Severity.INFO, "shapes_ignored", f"{view.shapes_ignored} container(s) or label(s) not imported")

    async def _create_diagram(self, view: ExchangeView) -> Diagram:
        """Create the diagram, suffixing ` (import N)` until the name is free."""
        assert self._diagrams is not None  # noqa: S101 - narrowed by the caller
        base = view.name.strip()
        for attempt in range(1, 1000):
            suffix = "" if attempt == 1 else f" (import {attempt})"
            name = base[: MAX_NAME_LENGTH - len(suffix)] + suffix
            try:
                async with self._repository.savepoint():
                    return await self._diagrams.create(name=name, description=view.description)
            except DuplicateDiagramError:
                continue
        raise RowRefusedError("name_exhausted", "no free name for this view")
```
(importer `Diagram`, `MAX_NAME_LENGTH`, `RowRefusedError`.) Le `assert` est remplacé par un `if self._diagrams is None: raise RuntimeError(...)` si bandit (`B101`) le signale.

### Task 26: Paquets — `organizations` ↔ arbre de paquets

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/src/ea/domain/exchange.py`, `backend/src/ea/domain/ports.py` (`ExportSnapshot.packages`), `backend/src/ea/formats/archimate_xml.py`, `backend/src/ea/repositories/exchange_store.py`, `backend/src/ea/services/exchanges.py`, `backend/src/ea/api/exchanges.py` (champ `target_package_id`), `backend/src/ea/main.py` (adaptateur), `backend/tests/conftest.py` (`InMemoryPackages`, fixtures)
- Create: `backend/src/ea/services/exchange_packages.py`, `backend/tests/integration/exchange_support.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/unit/test_archimate_xml_writer.py`, `test_archimate_xml_reader.py`, `test_exchange_import.py`, `test_exchange_service.py`, `backend/tests/integration/test_exchange_packages.py` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (sous-projet 1, noms confirmés à la Task 25) : `Element.package_id: UUID | None` ; table `packages(id, parent_id, name, description, created_at, updated_at)` ; son service de paquets.
- Produces (domain) : `PackageNode(id, parent_id, name)` ; `ExchangeFolder(label, items, children)` ; `ExchangeModel.folders` ; `ExchangeElement.package_id` ; `ImportOptions.target_package_id` ; `build_organization(packages, members) -> ExchangeFolder` (racine anonyme `label=""`). (port) `ExportSnapshot.packages() -> tuple[PackageNode, ...]`. (writer) `render_model(..., organizations: Callable[[], ExchangeFolder | None] | None = None)`. (service) protocole `Packages` — `async tree() -> Sequence[PackageNode]`, `async create(name: str, parent_id: UUID | None) -> UUID`, `async place(element_ids: Sequence[UUID], package_id: UUID | None) -> None` — et `ExchangeService(..., packages: Packages | None = None)`.

Règles : un élément **créé** par l'import prend le paquet du fichier (ou `target_package_id` s'il n'est dans aucun dossier) ; un élément **réutilisé** garde son paquet, avec l'avertissement `package_not_applied` si le fichier le range ailleurs ; un dossier existant (même parent, même nom sans casse) est réutilisé ; les dossiers sont créés sous `target_package_id` quand il est donné ; un libellé qu'un paquet ne peut pas porter (`/`, vide, > 200) est assaini avec l'avertissement `package_renamed`.

```python
class TestPackages:
    ORGANIZED = exchange_xml(
        el("a", "Node", "A") + el("b", "Node", "B") + el("c", "Node", "C"),
        extra='<organizations><item><label>Infra</label><item identifierRef="a"/>'
        '<item><label>Net</label><item identifierRef="b"/></item></item></organizations>',
    )

    async def test_folders_become_packages_and_created_elements_are_placed(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, packages: InMemoryPackages,
        repository: InMemoryRepository,
    ) -> None:
        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(xml(self.ORGANIZED), REAL)

        assert report.counts[Category.PACKAGES].created == 2
        by_package = {p.name: p for p in packages.nodes.values()}
        assert by_package["Net"].parent_id == by_package["Infra"].id
        by_element = {e.name: e.id for e in repository.elements.values()}
        assert packages.placed[by_element["A"]] == by_package["Infra"].id
        assert packages.placed[by_element["B"]] == by_package["Net"].id
        assert by_element["C"] not in packages.placed

    async def test_an_existing_package_of_another_case_is_reused(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, packages: InMemoryPackages,
        repository: InMemoryRepository,
    ) -> None:
        existing = await packages.create("infra", None)

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(xml(self.ORGANIZED), REAL)

        assert report.counts[Category.PACKAGES].reused == 1
        assert report.counts[Category.PACKAGES].created == 1
        by_element = {e.name: e.id for e in repository.elements.values()}
        assert packages.placed[by_element["A"]] == existing

    async def test_the_target_package_receives_the_unfiled_and_the_new_folders(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, packages: InMemoryPackages,
        repository: InMemoryRepository,
    ) -> None:
        target = await packages.create("Landing", None)

        await exchange_service.import_exchange(
            xml(self.ORGANIZED), ImportOptions(dry_run=False, target_package_id=target)
        )

        infra = next(p for p in packages.nodes.values() if p.name == "Infra")
        assert infra.parent_id == target
        by_element = {e.name: e.id for e in repository.elements.values()}
        assert packages.placed[by_element["C"]] == target

    async def test_an_existing_element_keeps_its_package_with_a_warning(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, service: ArchitectureService,
        packages: InMemoryPackages, repository: InMemoryRepository,
    ) -> None:
        elsewhere = await packages.create("Elsewhere", None)
        existing = await service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="A")
        repository.elements[existing.id] = dataclasses.replace(existing, package_id=elsewhere)

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(xml(self.ORGANIZED), REAL)

        assert existing.id not in packages.placed
        assert "package_not_applied" in [i.code for i in report.issues]

    async def test_a_label_a_package_cannot_carry_is_cleaned_with_a_warning(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, packages: InMemoryPackages
    ) -> None:
        content = exchange_xml(
            el("a", "Node", "A"),
            extra='<organizations><item><label>A/B</label><item identifierRef="a"/></item></organizations>',
        )

        report = await exchange_service.import_exchange(xml(content), REAL)

        assert [p.name for p in packages.nodes.values()] == ["A-B"]
        assert "package_renamed" in [i.code for i in report.issues]

    async def test_a_dry_run_leaves_the_packages_alone(
        self, exchange_service: ExchangeService, packages: InMemoryPackages
    ) -> None:
        await exchange_service.import_exchange(xml(self.ORGANIZED), ImportOptions())

        assert packages.nodes == {} and packages.placed == {}
```

Intégration (`tests/integration/exchange_support.py`, puis `test_exchange_packages.py`) :

```python
# backend/tests/integration/exchange_support.py
"""Arranging packages and views straight in the throwaway database, for the exchange tests."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Text, Uuid, column, delete, insert, table, update

from ea.db.models.architecture import ElementRecord
from ea.db.models.diagram import DiagramNodeRecord, DiagramRecord

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker

PACKAGES = table(
    "packages",
    column("id", Uuid),
    column("parent_id", Uuid),
    column("name", Text),
    column("description", Text),
    column("created_at", DateTime(timezone=True)),
    column("updated_at", DateTime(timezone=True)),
)


async def make_package(
    sessions: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession], name: str, parent: UUID | None = None
) -> UUID:
    package_id, now = uuid4(), datetime.now(UTC)
    async with sessions.begin() as session:
        await session.execute(
            insert(PACKAGES).values(
                id=package_id, parent_id=parent, name=name, description="",
                created_at=now, updated_at=now,
            )
        )
    return package_id


async def file_element(
    sessions: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession], element_id: UUID, package_id: UUID
) -> None:
    async with sessions.begin() as session:
        await session.execute(
            update(ElementRecord).where(ElementRecord.id == element_id).values(package_id=package_id)
        )


async def empty_packages(sessions: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]) -> None:
    """Children first: the parent link is `ON DELETE RESTRICT`."""
    async with sessions.begin() as session:
        await session.execute(delete(PACKAGES).where(PACKAGES.c.parent_id.is_not(None)))
        await session.execute(delete(PACKAGES))


async def make_view(
    sessions: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession], name: str, element_ids: list[UUID]
) -> UUID:
    diagram_id, now = uuid4(), datetime.now(UTC)
    async with sessions.begin() as session:
        session.add(DiagramRecord(id=diagram_id, name=name, description="", created_at=now, updated_at=now))
        await session.flush()
        session.add_all(
            DiagramNodeRecord(diagram_id=diagram_id, element_id=e, x=1, y=2, width=3, height=4)
            for e in element_ids
        )
    return diagram_id
```

```python
# backend/tests/integration/test_exchange_packages.py
"""The package tree through the export, against a real PostgreSQL."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from defusedxml import ElementTree as ET
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine

from ea.db.postgres import create_session_factory
from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType as E
from ea.domain.exchange import NAMESPACE, ExportRequest, ExportScope
from ea.repositories.exchange_store import PostgresExchangeRepository
from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService
from ea.services.exchanges import ExchangeService
from tests.integration.exchange_support import file_element, make_package

pytestmark = [pytest.mark.asyncio, pytest.mark.postgres]
NS = {"x": NAMESPACE}


async def test_the_snapshot_lists_the_packages_and_the_export_nests_them(
    engine_at_head: AsyncEngine, graph_service: ArchitectureService
) -> None:
    sessions = create_session_factory(engine_at_head)
    store = PostgresExchangeRepository(sessions)
    top = await make_package(sessions, "Top")
    sub = await make_package(sessions, "Sub", top)
    a = await graph_service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="a")
    b = await graph_service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="b")
    await file_element(sessions, a.id, top)
    await file_element(sessions, b.id, sub)

    async with store.snapshot(ExportScope()) as snapshot:
        assert {(p.name, p.parent_id) for p in await snapshot.packages()} == {("Top", None), ("Sub", top)}

    export = await ExchangeService(store, graph_service).export(ExportRequest())
    root = ET.fromstring(b"".join([c async for c in export.chunks]))
    folder = root.find("x:organizations/x:item", NS)
    assert folder is not None and folder.find("x:label", NS).text == "Top"  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    assert folder.find("x:item/x:label", NS).text == "Sub"  # type: ignore[union-attr]
```

`domain/exchange.py` (imports `Sequence` ; `PackageNode` et `ExchangeFolder` avant `ExchangeModel`) :

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PackageNode:
    id: UUID
    parent_id: UUID | None
    name: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ExchangeFolder:
    """A folder of `organizations`: a package's label, the elements directly in it."""

    label: str
    items: tuple[str, ...]
    children: tuple[ExchangeFolder, ...]


def build_organization(
    packages: Sequence[PackageNode], members: Mapping[UUID | None, Sequence[str]]
) -> ExchangeFolder:
    """The anonymous root: unfiled elements, and every package whose parent is not listed."""
    known = {p.id for p in packages}
    by_parent: dict[UUID | None, list[PackageNode]] = {}
    for package in sorted(packages, key=lambda p: (p.name, str(p.id))):
        parent = package.parent_id if package.parent_id in known else None
        by_parent.setdefault(parent, []).append(package)

    def folder(package: PackageNode) -> ExchangeFolder:
        return ExchangeFolder(
            package.name,
            tuple(sorted(members.get(package.id, ()))),
            tuple(folder(child) for child in by_parent.get(package.id, ())),
        )

    return ExchangeFolder(
        "",
        tuple(sorted(members.get(None, ()))),
        tuple(folder(package) for package in by_parent.get(None, ())),
    )
```

```python
def folder_xml(folder: ExchangeFolder) -> str:
    body = "".join(folder_xml(child) for child in folder.children)
    body += "".join(f'<item identifierRef="{_attr(ref)}"/>' for ref in folder.items)
    return f"<item>{_lang('label', folder.label)}{body}</item>"
```
