```python
    known_references = seen | {e.identifier for e in elements}
    folders = tuple(
        _folder(item, known_references, issues, depth=1)
        for item in root.findall(f"{_q('organizations')}/{_q('item')}")
        if item.find(_q("label")) is not None
    )
```
```python
def _folder(node: Element, known: set[str], issues: list[Issue], depth: int) -> ExchangeFolder:
    if depth > MAX_ORGANIZATION_DEPTH:
        raise _refuse(f"organizations are nested more than {MAX_ORGANIZATION_DEPTH} levels deep")
    items: list[str] = []
    children: list[ExchangeFolder] = []
    for child in node.findall(_q("item")):
        reference = child.get("identifierRef")
        if reference is not None:
            if reference in known:
                items.append(reference)
            else:
                issues.append(
                    Issue(Severity.WARNING, "unknown_reference",
                          "an organization item refers to an identifier the file does not define",
                          reference)
                )
        elif child.find(_q("label")) is not None:
            children.append(_folder(child, known, issues, depth + 1))
    return ExchangeFolder((node.findtext(_q("label")) or "").strip(), tuple(items), tuple(children))
```

```python
class Packages(Protocol):
    """The packages of sub-project 1, as an exchange needs them (see `exchange_packages.py`)."""

    async def tree(self) -> Sequence[PackageNode]: ...

    async def create(self, name: str, parent_id: UUID | None) -> UUID: ...

    async def place(self, element_ids: Sequence[UUID], package_id: UUID | None) -> None: ...
```
`ExchangeService.__init__` gagne `packages: Packages | None = None` → `self._packages`. Modifier `_Batch` (Task 12) : ajouter `by_id: dict[UUID, Element]`, `created_ids: dict[str, UUID]`, `filed: set[UUID]` (`by_id={e.id: e for e in known}` dans `_apply`) ; dans `_element`, après la création : `batch.created_ids[item.identifier] = created.id` et `batch.by_id[created.id] = created`. Dans `_apply`, après `_apply_elements` : `await self._apply_packages(model, options, report, batch)`. Ajouter :

```python
    async def _apply_packages(
        self, model: ExchangeModel, options: ImportOptions, report: ReportBuilder, batch: _Batch
    ) -> None:
        target = options.target_package_id
        if self._packages is None or not (model.folders or target):
            return
        packages = self._packages
        tree = {(p.parent_id, p.name.casefold()): p.id for p in await packages.tree()}
        pending: dict[UUID, list[UUID]] = {}

        async def walk(folder: ExchangeFolder, parent: UUID | None) -> None:
            name = _package_name(folder.label)
            if name != folder.label:
                report.add(
                    Issue(Severity.WARNING, "package_renamed",
                          f"package {folder.label!r} was stored as {name!r}")
                )
            package = tree.get((parent, name.casefold()))
            if package is None:
                try:
                    async with self._repository.savepoint():
                        package = await packages.create(name, parent)
                except (DomainError, ValueError) as refusal:
                    report.count(Category.PACKAGES, Outcome.REJECTED)
                    report.add(Issue(Severity.ERROR, "package_refused", str(refusal)))
                    return
                tree[(parent, name.casefold())] = package
                report.count(Category.PACKAGES, Outcome.CREATED)
            else:
                report.count(Category.PACKAGES, Outcome.REUSED)
            for identifier in folder.items:
                self._file(identifier, package, batch, report, pending)
            for child in folder.children:
                await walk(child, package)

        for folder in model.folders:
            await walk(folder, target)
        if target is not None:
            pending.setdefault(target, []).extend(
                uid for uid in batch.created_ids.values() if uid not in batch.filed
            )
        for package_id, element_ids in pending.items():
            await packages.place(element_ids, package_id)

    @staticmethod
    def _file(
        identifier: str, package: UUID, batch: _Batch, report: ReportBuilder,
        pending: dict[UUID, list[UUID]],
    ) -> None:
        """Queue a created element for its folder; a reused one keeps the package it has."""
        uid = None if identifier in batch.refused else batch.ids.get(identifier)
        if uid is None:
            return
        if batch.created_ids.get(identifier) == uid:
            pending.setdefault(package, []).append(uid)
            batch.filed.add(uid)
            return
        existing = batch.by_id.get(uid)
        if existing is not None and existing.package_id != package:
            report.add(
                Issue(Severity.WARNING, "package_not_applied",
                      "an element already in the catalogue keeps its package", identifier)
            )
```
```python
def _package_name(label: str) -> str:
    """A name a package can carry: no `/`, 1 to 200 characters."""
    return label.replace("/", "-").strip()[:MAX_NAME_LENGTH] or "Untitled"
```

```python
"""The packages of sub-project 1, seen as an exchange needs them: a tree, a creation, a placement."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from ea.domain.exchange import PackageNode

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from collections.abc import Sequence
    from uuid import UUID

    from ea.services.packages import PackageService  # sub-project 1


class PackagesOfTheCatalogue:
    def __init__(self, packages: PackageService) -> None:
        self._packages = packages

    async def tree(self) -> Sequence[PackageNode]:
        return tuple(
            PackageNode(p.id, p.parent_id, p.name) for p in await self._packages.list_packages()
        )

    async def create(self, name: str, parent_id: UUID | None) -> UUID:
        return (await self._packages.create_package(name=name, parent_id=parent_id)).id

    async def place(self, element_ids: Sequence[UUID], package_id: UUID | None) -> None:
        await self._packages.move_elements(element_ids, package_id)
```

```python
class InMemoryPackages:
    """The `Packages` protocol over dictionaries."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.nodes: dict[UUID, PackageNode] = {}
        self.placed: dict[UUID, UUID] = {}

    async def tree(self) -> tuple[PackageNode, ...]:
        return tuple(self.nodes.values())

    async def create(self, name: str, parent_id: UUID | None) -> UUID:
        node = PackageNode(uuid4(), parent_id, name)
        self.nodes[node.id] = node
        return node.id

    async def place(self, element_ids: Sequence[UUID], package_id: UUID | None) -> None:
        for element_id in element_ids:
            self.placed[element_id] = package_id  # type: ignore[assignment]
```

### Task 27: Périmètres « paquet » et « requête enregistrée »

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/src/ea/domain/exchange.py`, `backend/src/ea/domain/ports.py`, `backend/src/ea/repositories/exchange_store.py`, `backend/src/ea/services/exchanges.py`, `backend/src/ea/services/exchange_packages.py`, `backend/src/ea/main.py`, `backend/tests/conftest.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/unit/test_exchange_service.py`, `backend/tests/e2e/test_exchange_api.py`, `backend/tests/integration/test_exchange_scopes.py` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (sous-projet 1, noms de la Task 25) : `ElementQuery` (`ea.domain.query`) et sa traduction SQL (`_matching` étendu dans `architecture_store.py`), le service des requêtes enregistrées, `PackageNotFoundError`.
- Produces : `ScopeKind.PACKAGE`, `ScopeKind.QUERY` ; `ExportScope.package_ids: frozenset[UUID] | None` et `ExportScope.query: ElementQuery | None` (résolus par le service, jamais fournis par le client) ; `ExchangeRepository.count_skipped_views(scope) -> int` ; protocoles `Packages.subtree(package_id) -> frozenset[UUID]` (ajouté) et `Queries.query_of(query_id) -> ElementQuery` ; `Export.skipped_views` rempli, d'où l'en-tête `X-Export-Skipped-Views`. `UnknownScopeError` : `package` sans `package_id` (ou avec `query_id`), `query` sans `query_id` (ou avec `package_id`), `model` avec l'un ou l'autre.

Règles : seuls les éléments du périmètre sont exportés ; une relation n'est exportée que si **ses deux** extrémités y sont ; une vue n'est exportée que si **toutes** ses boîtes y sont ; l'arbre `organizations` ne contient que le sous-arbre demandé, dont la racine devient un dossier de premier niveau ; pour `scope=query`, l'arbre exporté est le squelette complet des paquets (dossiers vides compris).

```python
class StubQueries:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.asked: list[UUID] = []

    async def query_of(self, query_id: UUID) -> ElementQuery:
        self.asked.append(query_id)
        return ElementQuery()


class TestScopes:
    async def arranged(self, service, packages, repository):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
        top = await packages.create("Top", None)
        child = await packages.create("Child", top)
        other = await packages.create("Other", None)
        inside = await service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="in")
        deep = await service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="deep")
        outside = await service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="out")
        for element, package in ((inside, top), (deep, child), (outside, other)):
            repository.elements[element.id] = dataclasses.replace(element, package_id=package)
        return top, inside, deep, outside

    async def test_a_package_scope_exports_its_subtree_and_the_links_inside_it(
        self, service, exchange_service, packages, repository
    ) -> None:
        top, inside, deep, outside = await self.arranged(service, packages, repository)
        await service.connect(relationship_type=R.ASSOCIATION, source_id=inside.id, target_id=deep.id)
        await service.connect(relationship_type=R.ASSOCIATION, source_id=inside.id, target_id=outside.id)

        request = ExportRequest(scope=ExportScope(ScopeKind.PACKAGE, package_id=top))
        root = ET.fromstring(await exported(exchange_service, request))

        names = {e.find("x:name", NS).text for e in root.findall("x:elements/x:element", NS)}  # type: ignore[union-attr]
        assert names == {"in", "deep"}
        assert len(root.findall("x:relationships/x:relationship", NS)) == 1
        assert root.find("x:organizations/x:item/x:label", NS).text == "Top"  # type: ignore[union-attr]

    async def test_a_view_with_a_box_outside_the_scope_is_left_out_and_counted(
        self, service, diagram_service, exchange_service, packages, repository
    ) -> None:
        top, inside, _, outside = await self.arranged(service, packages, repository)
        whole = await diagram_service.create(name="whole")
        await diagram_service.replace_layout(whole.id, [DiagramNode(inside.id, 0, 0)])
        straddling = await diagram_service.create(name="straddling")
        await diagram_service.replace_layout(
            straddling.id, [DiagramNode(inside.id, 0, 0), DiagramNode(outside.id, 5, 5)]
        )
        request = ExportRequest(scope=ExportScope(ScopeKind.PACKAGE, package_id=top))

        export = await exchange_service.export(request)
        root = ET.fromstring(b"".join([c async for c in export.chunks]))

        views = [v.get("identifier") for v in root.findall("x:views/x:diagrams/x:view", NS)]
        assert views == ["view-whole"]
        assert export.skipped_views == 1

    async def test_a_query_scope_resolves_the_saved_query_and_hands_it_to_the_snapshot(
        self, service, exchange_store, packages, diagram_service
    ) -> None:
        queries = StubQueries()
        exchange = ExchangeService(
            exchange_store, service, diagrams=diagram_service, packages=packages,
            queries=queries, clock=lambda: FIXED_NOW,
        )
        query_id = uuid4()

        await exported(exchange, ExportRequest(scope=ExportScope(ScopeKind.QUERY, query_id=query_id)))

        assert queries.asked == [query_id]
        assert exchange_store.scopes[-1].query == ElementQuery()

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "scope",
        [
            ExportScope(ScopeKind.PACKAGE),
            ExportScope(ScopeKind.QUERY),
            ExportScope(ScopeKind.MODEL, package_id=uuid4()),
            ExportScope(ScopeKind.PACKAGE, package_id=uuid4(), query_id=uuid4()),
        ],
    )
    async def test_an_ill_formed_scope_is_refused(self, exchange_service, scope) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
        with pytest.raises(UnknownScopeError):
            await exchange_service.export(ExportRequest(scope=scope))
```

```python
"""Export scopes against a real PostgreSQL: a package subtree, a saved query, whole views."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine

from ea.db.postgres import create_session_factory
from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType as E
from ea.domain.archimate import RelationshipType as R
from ea.domain.exchange import ExportScope, ScopeKind
from ea.domain.query import ElementQuery
from ea.repositories.exchange_store import PostgresExchangeRepository
from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService
from tests.integration.exchange_support import file_element, make_package, make_view

pytestmark = [pytest.mark.asyncio, pytest.mark.postgres]


async def test_a_package_scope_keeps_elements_links_and_whole_views_inside(
    engine_at_head: AsyncEngine, graph_service: ArchitectureService
) -> None:
    sessions = create_session_factory(engine_at_head)
    store = PostgresExchangeRepository(sessions)
    top, other = await make_package(sessions, "Top"), await make_package(sessions, "Other")
    a = await graph_service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="a")
    b = await graph_service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="b")
    c = await graph_service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="c")
    for element, package in ((a, top), (b, top), (c, other)):
        await file_element(sessions, element.id, package)
    await graph_service.connect(relationship_type=R.ASSOCIATION, source_id=a.id, target_id=b.id)
    await graph_service.connect(relationship_type=R.ASSOCIATION, source_id=a.id, target_id=c.id)
    await make_view(sessions, "inside", [a.id, b.id])
    await make_view(sessions, "straddling", [a.id, c.id])
    scope = ExportScope(ScopeKind.PACKAGE, package_id=top, package_ids=frozenset({top}))

    async with store.snapshot(scope) as snapshot:
        assert [e.name for e in await snapshot.elements_page(0, 10)] == ["a", "b"]
        assert len(await snapshot.relationships_page(0, 10)) == 1
        assert [v.name for v in await snapshot.views_page(0, 10)] == ["inside"]
        assert [p.name for p in await snapshot.packages()] == ["Top"]
    assert await store.count_skipped_views(scope) == 1


async def test_a_query_scope_applies_the_saved_filter(
    engine_at_head: AsyncEngine, graph_service: ArchitectureService
) -> None:
    store = PostgresExchangeRepository(create_session_factory(engine_at_head))
    await graph_service.create_element(element_type=E.NODE, name="srv")
    await graph_service.create_element(element_type=E.BUSINESS_ACTOR, name="Alice")
    scope = ExportScope(ScopeKind.QUERY, query=ElementQuery(element_types=(E.NODE,)))

    async with store.snapshot(scope) as snapshot:
        assert [e.name for e in await snapshot.elements_page(0, 10)] == ["srv"]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implémenter**

Domain : `ScopeKind` gagne `PACKAGE = "package"` et `QUERY = "query"` ; `ExportScope` gagne `package_ids: frozenset[UUID] | None = None` et `query: ElementQuery | None = None` (`from ea.domain.query import ElementQuery`). Port : `ExchangeRepository.count_skipped_views(self, scope: ExportScope) -> int`.

Service (imports `replace` de `dataclasses`, `Protocol`, `ScopeKind`, `UnknownScopeError`, `ElementQuery`) :

```python
class Queries(Protocol):
    async def query_of(self, query_id: UUID) -> ElementQuery: ...
```
`Packages` gagne `async def subtree(self, package_id: UUID) -> frozenset[UUID]: ...` ; `__init__` gagne `queries: Queries | None = None`. Remplacer `export` :

```python
    async def export(self, request: ExportRequest) -> Export:
        require_caller()
        request = replace(request, scope=await self._resolve(request.scope))
        is_csv = request.format is ExchangeFormat.CSV
        with_views = request.with_views and not is_csv
        return Export(
            filename=f"ea-{request.scope.label}-{self._now():%Y-%m-%d}.{'zip' if is_csv else 'xml'}",
            media_type="application/zip" if is_csv else "application/xml",
            chunks=self._csv(request) if is_csv else self._xml(request),
            skipped_views=(
                await self._repository.count_skipped_views(request.scope) if with_views else 0
            ),
        )

    async def _resolve(self, scope: ExportScope) -> ExportScope:
        """Turn the client's ids into what the snapshot filters on; an unknown one is a 404."""
        match scope.kind:
            case ScopeKind.MODEL:
                if scope.package_id or scope.query_id:
                    msg = "scope=model takes neither a package nor a query"
                    raise UnknownScopeError(msg)
                return scope
            case ScopeKind.PACKAGE:
                if scope.package_id is None or scope.query_id is not None:
                    msg = "scope=package needs `package_id`, and only that"
                    raise UnknownScopeError(msg)
                if self._packages is None:
                    msg = "packages are not available on this deployment"
                    raise UnknownScopeError(msg)
                return replace(scope, package_ids=await self._packages.subtree(scope.package_id))
            case ScopeKind.QUERY:
                if scope.query_id is None or scope.package_id is not None:
                    msg = "scope=query needs `query_id`, and only that"
                    raise UnknownScopeError(msg)
                if self._queries is None:
                    msg = "saved queries are not available on this deployment"
                    raise UnknownScopeError(msg)
                return replace(scope, query=await self._queries.query_of(scope.query_id))
```
Adaptateurs (`exchange_packages.py`) : `PackagesOfTheCatalogue.subtree(package_id)` — parcours de `tree()` en mémoire qui lève l'erreur 404 « paquet inconnu » du sous-projet 1 (`PackageNotFoundError`) si l'identifiant est absent ; classe `QueriesOfTheCatalogue(queries: SavedQueryService)` avec `query_of(query_id)` qui renvoie `(await self._queries.get(query_id)).query` (nom du service et de la méthode d'après la Task 25). Câblage : `queries=QueriesOfTheCatalogue(app.state.saved_query_service)` dans le lifespan.

Store — `_Snapshot.__init__(self, session, scope)` ; fonction (imports `ColumnElement`, `exists`, `_matching` de `architecture_store`, tel que sous-projet 1 l'étend) :

```python
def _scope_clauses(scope: ExportScope) -> list[ColumnElement[bool]]:
    if scope.package_ids is not None:
        return [ElementRecord.package_id.in_(scope.package_ids)]
    if scope.query is not None:
        return _matching(scope.query)  # sub-project 1: one SQL path for filter and query
    return []
```
`elements_page` : `.where(*_scope_clauses(self._scope))` ; `relationships_page` : si `clauses`, `.where(RelationshipRecord.source_id.in_(inside), RelationshipRecord.target_id.in_(inside))` avec `inside = select(ElementRecord.id).where(*clauses)` ; `views_page` : si `clauses`, ajouter à la requête des diagrammes
`.where(~exists().where(DiagramNodeRecord.diagram_id == DiagramRecord.id, DiagramNodeRecord.element_id.not_in(inside)))` ; `packages()` : si `scope.package_ids` n'est pas `None`, `.where(_PACKAGES.c.id.in_(scope.package_ids))` ; `PostgresExchangeRepository.snapshot` passe `scope` à `_Snapshot` (la ligne `del scope` de la Task 4 disparaît) ; et

```python
    async def count_skipped_views(self, scope: ExportScope) -> int:
        clauses = _scope_clauses(scope)
        if not clauses:
            return 0
        inside = select(ElementRecord.id).where(*clauses)
        leaving = exists().where(
            DiagramNodeRecord.diagram_id == DiagramRecord.id,
            DiagramNodeRecord.element_id.not_in(inside),
        )
        async with self._sessions() as session:
            return int(
                (await session.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(DiagramRecord).where(leaving)))
                .scalar_one()
            )
```
Double mémoire : `_MemorySnapshot.__init__(self, repository, diagrams, packages, scope)` calcule `self._inside = None if scope.package_ids is None else {e.id for e in repository.elements.values() if e.package_id in scope.package_ids}` et filtre éléments, relations (deux extrémités), vues (toutes les boîtes) et paquets (`p.id in scope.package_ids`) ; `InMemoryExchange.snapshot` enregistre `self.scopes.append(scope)` ; `count_skipped_views(scope)` = nombre de vues ayant une boîte hors de `_inside` (0 si `package_ids is None`). `InMemoryPackages.subtree(package_id)` renvoie le sous-arbre par parcours de `nodes` et lève `ElementNotFoundError` si `package_id` n'y est pas.

```python
async def test_importing_an_export_again_reuses_elements_and_adds_a_suffixed_view(with_views) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]

    assert report.counts[Category.VIEWS].created == 1
```

# LOT 5 — Outils MCP

L'adaptateur MCP est un frère d'`api/` : il ne reçoit qu'un `ExchangeService`, jamais un dépôt, et ne restate aucune règle. Deux outils, trente-quatre → trente-six (ou deux de plus que le compte alors affiché si le sous-projet 1 a déjà ajouté les siens).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implémenter**

Service — `Export.chunks: AsyncGenerator[bytes, None]` (import depuis `collections.abc`) ; factoriser le remplissage des trois fichiers (le corps de `_csv` de la Task 19) :

```python
    async def _fill(
        self, request: ExportRequest, spools: dict[str, tempfile.SpooledTemporaryFile[bytes]]
    ) -> _Streams:
        async with self._repository.snapshot(request.scope) as snapshot:
            streams = _Streams(snapshot, self._monotonic)
            async for name, text in render_csv(
                streams.elements(), streams.relationships(), safe_cells=request.safe_cells
            ):
                spools[name].write(text.encode("utf-8"))
        return streams
```
`_csv` appelle `streams = await self._fill(request, spools)` à la place de son bloc `async with`. Puis :

```python
    async def export_text(self, request: ExportRequest) -> Export:
        """The export as text an agent can read: the XML, or the three CSV files one after another."""
        require_caller()
        if request.format is not ExchangeFormat.CSV:
            return await self.export(request)
        request = replace(request, scope=await self._resolve(request.scope))
        return Export(
            filename=f"ea-{request.scope.label}-{self._now():%Y-%m-%d}.txt",
            media_type="text/plain",
            chunks=self._csv_text(request),
        )

    async def _csv_text(self, request: ExportRequest) -> AsyncGenerator[bytes, None]:
        spools = {name: tempfile.SpooledTemporaryFile(max_size=1 << 20) for name in CSV_FILES}  # noqa: SIM115
        try:
            streams = await self._fill(request, spools)
            for name in CSV_FILES:
                yield f"--- {name} ---\n".encode()
                spools[name].seek(0)
                while chunk := spools[name].read(65_536):
                    yield chunk
            self._log_export(request, streams)
        finally:
            for spool in spools.values():
                spool.close()
```
(le type de retour des générateurs `_xml` et `_csv` devient `AsyncGenerator[bytes, None]`.)

MCP (`mcp/server.py`) — imports : `ImportReportRead` depuis `ea.api.schemas` (utilisé par la Task 31), `from ea.domain.exchange import ExchangeFormat, ExportRequest, ExportScope, ScopeKind`, `from ea.services.exchanges import ExchangeService` ; après `MAX_MCP_UPLOAD_BYTES` :

```python
#: What `export_model` hands back at most, in bytes of text. The same reasons as
#: `MAX_MCP_UPLOAD_BYTES`: the JSON-RPC envelope and the 2 MB the deployed NGINX
#: allows on `/api/mcp`. A bigger model is exported from the web interface.
MAX_MCP_EXPORT_BYTES: Final = 1024 * 1024
```
Alias `ExchangeProvider = Callable[[], ExchangeService]` ; `build_mcp_server` gagne `get_exchanges: ExchangeProvider` en 5e paramètre positionnel (même raison que `get_files` : requis, pour que l'outil soit toujours listé et échoue avec la faute de câblage plutôt que de disparaître) ; docstring mise à jour (« cinq services ») ; avant `# --- Metamodel ---` :

```python
    # --- Exchange files (docs/adr/NN) ----------------------------------------

    @server.tool(annotations=READS)
    @speaking_plainly
    async def export_model(
        format: ExchangeFormat = ExchangeFormat.ARCHIMATE_XML,  # noqa: A002 - the name an agent is told
        scope: ScopeKind = ScopeKind.MODEL,
        package_id: UUID | None = None,
        query_id: UUID | None = None,
    ) -> str:
        """Export the catalogue as an ArchiMate exchange file, returned as text.

        `format` is `archimate-xml` (the Open Group exchange file Sparx EA and
        Archi open) or `csv` (three files, labelled `--- elements.csv ---` and so
        on). `scope` is `model` (everything), `package` (with `package_id`) or
        `query` (with `query_id`, a saved query). Diagram views are not
        included. The answer is at most 1 MB: for a bigger model, narrow the
        scope with a package or a saved query, or use the web interface's
        Échanges screen, which downloads a file.
        """
        export = await get_exchanges().export_text(
            ExportRequest(
                format=format,
                scope=ExportScope(kind=scope, package_id=package_id, query_id=query_id),
                with_views=False,
            )
        )
        buffer = bytearray()
        async for chunk in export.chunks:
            buffer += chunk
            if len(buffer) > MAX_MCP_EXPORT_BYTES:
                await export.chunks.aclose()
                msg = (
                    f"the export is over {MAX_MCP_EXPORT_BYTES} bytes, more than an MCP answer "
                    "can carry: narrow the scope with a package or a saved query, or download "
                    "the file from the Échanges screen of the web interface"
                )
                raise ValueError(msg)
        return buffer.decode("utf-8")
```
`main._mount_mcp` : importer `exchange_service_of` depuis `ea.api.dependencies` et ajouter `lambda: exchange_service_of(app),` après `lambda: file_service_of(app),`.

### Task 31: `import_model` (écriture) et `INSTRUCTIONS`

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/src/ea/mcp/server.py` (outil ; `INSTRUCTIONS`), `backend/tests/unit/test_mcp_server.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ExchangeService.import_exchange`, `ImportReportRead.of` (Task 14), `MAX_MCP_UPLOAD_BYTES`.

```python
@pytest.mark.asyncio
class TestImportModel:
    XML = (DATA / "minimal-model.xml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    async def test_the_default_is_a_dry_run_that_writes_nothing(
        self, server: MCPServer[Any], repository: InMemoryRepository
    ) -> None:
        report = await call(server, "import_model", content=self.XML)

        assert report["dry_run"] is True and report["applied"] is False
        assert report["counts"]["elements"]["created"] == 3
        assert repository.elements == {}

    async def test_dry_run_false_writes(
        self, server: MCPServer[Any], repository: InMemoryRepository
    ) -> None:
        report = await call(server, "import_model", content=self.XML, dry_run=False)

        assert report["applied"] is True
        assert len(repository.elements) == 3

    async def test_csv_takes_its_three_texts(
        self, server: MCPServer[Any], repository: InMemoryRepository
    ) -> None:
        report = await call(
            server,
            "import_model",
            format="csv",
            elements='"ID","Type","Name"\r\n"a","Node","srv"\r\n',
            relations='"ID","Type","Source","Target"\r\n',
            dry_run=False,
        )

        assert report["format"] == "csv" and report["counts"]["elements"]["created"] == 1

    async def test_views_are_not_imported_over_mcp(
        self, server: MCPServer[Any], diagrams: InMemoryDiagrams
    ) -> None:
        text = (DATA / "views-model.xml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")

        report = await call(server, "import_model", content=text, dry_run=False)

        assert report["counts"]["views"]["ignored"] == 1
        assert diagrams.diagrams == {}

    async def test_a_reader_cannot_even_simulate(self, server: MCPServer[Any]) -> None:
        with acting_as(a_reader()), pytest.raises(ToolError):
            await call(server, "import_model", content=self.XML)

    async def test_the_text_a_format_needs_is_required(self, server: MCPServer[Any]) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ToolError, match="content"):
            await call(server, "import_model")
        with pytest.raises(ToolError, match="elements"):
            await call(server, "import_model", format="csv", content="x")

    async def test_text_over_the_ceiling_is_refused(
        self, server: MCPServer[Any], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
    ) -> None:
        monkeypatch.setattr("ea.mcp.server.MAX_MCP_UPLOAD_BYTES", 50)

        with pytest.raises(ToolError, match="50 bytes"):
            await call(server, "import_model", content=self.XML)

    async def test_a_malformed_file_is_a_tool_error_with_no_echo(self, server: MCPServer[Any]) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ToolError) as raised:
            await call(server, "import_model", content="<model><secret-tag>")

        assert "secret-tag" not in str(raised.value)

    async def test_it_writes_but_never_deletes(self, server: MCPServer[Any]) -> None:
        tool = next(t for t in await server.list_tools() if t.name == "import_model")

        assert tool.annotations is not None
        assert tool.annotations.read_only_hint is False
        assert tool.annotations.destructive_hint is False
        assert "dry_run" in (tool.description or "")

    async def test_the_server_tells_a_model_to_simulate_first(self) -> None:
        assert "dry_run" in INSTRUCTIONS and "export_model" in INSTRUCTIONS
```

Types d'arguments (près des autres, avec la raison de la borne en caractères) :

```python
#: Characters, not bytes — this only stops a runaway argument; `import_model`
#: measures the encoded total against `MAX_MCP_UPLOAD_BYTES`.
ExchangeText = Annotated[
    str | None,
    Field(max_length=MAX_MCP_UPLOAD_BYTES, description="The file's text, UTF-8."),
]
```

Outil, après `export_model` :

```python
    @server.tool(annotations=ADDS)
    @speaking_plainly
    async def import_model(
        format: ExchangeFormat = ExchangeFormat.ARCHIMATE_XML,  # noqa: A002 - the name an agent is told
        content: ExchangeText = None,
        elements: ExchangeText = None,
        relations: ExchangeText = None,
        properties: ExchangeText = None,
        dry_run: bool = True,
        on_conflict: ConflictPolicy = ConflictPolicy.REUSE,
        strict: bool = False,
        safe_cells: bool = True,
        target_package_id: UUID | None = None,
    ) -> ImportReportRead:
        """Import an ArchiMate exchange file, or simulate importing it. It creates; it deletes nothing.

        `dry_run` is **true by default**: the call goes through every rule and
        every constraint, then rolls back, and the report says what it would do.
        Always simulate first, read the report — counts per category and every
        refused row with its reason — then call again with `dry_run=false`.

        For `archimate-xml`, put the file's text in `content`. For `csv`, put the
        three files in `elements` (required), `relations` and `properties`. An
        element with the same type and name is reused, never overwritten, and a
        refused row (a relationship the metamodel forbids, say) does not stop
        the others unless `strict` is true. Junctions and diagram connections
        are not imported, and diagram views are not imported over MCP. The text
        is at most 1 MB; a larger file goes through the web interface.
        """
        if format is ExchangeFormat.CSV:
            if elements is None:
                msg = "a CSV import needs the text of `elements`"
                raise ValueError(msg)
            parts = (elements, relations, properties)
        else:
            if content is None:
                msg = "an XML import needs the file's text in `content`"
                raise ValueError(msg)
            parts = (content, None, None)
        raw = tuple(None if part is None else part.encode() for part in parts)
        if sum(len(part) for part in raw if part is not None) > MAX_MCP_UPLOAD_BYTES:
            msg = (
                f"an exchange file is at most {MAX_MCP_UPLOAD_BYTES} bytes over MCP; "
                "import a larger file from the web interface instead"
            )
            raise ValueError(msg)
        report = await get_exchanges().import_exchange(
            ImportContent(format, raw[0] or b"", raw[1], raw[2]),
            ImportOptions(
                dry_run=dry_run,
                on_conflict=on_conflict,
                strict=strict,
                with_views=False,
                safe_cells=safe_cells,
                target_package_id=target_package_id,
            ),
        )
        return ImportReportRead.of(report)
```
`INSTRUCTIONS` — ajouter un paragraphe avant la dernière phrase du texte (celui des fichiers) :

```
The catalogue can also be exchanged with other ArchiMate tools (Sparx EA,
Archi). `export_model` returns the model as ArchiMate exchange XML, or as the
three CSV files, in at most 1 MB of text — for more, narrow it to a package or
a saved query, or use the web interface. `import_model` reads such a file, and
it simulates by default: always call it with `dry_run` left true first, read the
report (counts per category, and every refused row with its reason), and only
then call it again with `dry_run=false`. It creates elements and links and
deletes nothing: an element with the same type and name is reused, not
overwritten. Junctions and diagram views are not imported over MCP.
```

```ts
export function aReport(overrides: Partial<ImportReportRead> = {}): ImportReportRead {
  const none = { created: 0, reused: 0, rejected: 0, ignored: 0 }
  return {
    dry_run: true,
    applied: false,
    format: 'archimate-xml',
    sha256: 'a.xml:1',
    counts: {
      elements: { ...none, created: 3 },
      relationships: { ...none },
      packages: { ...none },
      views: { ...none },
    },
    issues: [],
    truncated: false,
    ...overrides,
  }
}

export function anIssue(overrides: Partial<ImportReportRead['issues'][number]> = {}) {
  return {
    severity: 'error' as const,
    code: 'relationship_not_allowed',
    message: 'not allowed',
    source_id: 'r-1',
    line: null,
    path: null,
    ...overrides,
  }
}
```

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

import {
  filenameOf,
  filesOf,
  importForm,
  useExchanges,
  type ImportChoice,
} from '../src/features/exchanges/useExchanges'
import { aReport, stubApi } from './support/api'

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals()
})

const choice: ImportChoice = {
  format: 'archimate-xml',
  conflict: 'reuse',
  strict: false,
  withViews: true,
  safeCells: true,
}
const file = (name: string, content = 'x'): File => new File([content], name)
const digest = vi.fn((files: File[]) => Promise.resolve(files.map((f) => `${f.name}:${f.size}`).join('|')))

describe('the pure helpers', () => {
  it('lists the files in the order the server digests them', () => {
    const [e, r, p] = [file('e.csv'), file('r.csv'), file('p.csv')]

    expect(filesOf('csv', { properties: p, elements: e, relations: r })).toEqual([e, r, p])
    expect(filesOf('csv', { elements: e })).toEqual([e])
    expect(filesOf('archimate-xml', { file: e, elements: r })).toEqual([e])
  })

  it('builds the multipart body of a simulation by default', () => {
    const form = importForm('archimate-xml', { file: file('m.xml') }, { ...choice, strict: true }, true)

    expect(form.get('dry_run')).toBe('true')
    expect(form.get('strict')).toBe('true')
    expect(form.get('format')).toBe('archimate-xml')
    expect((form.get('file') as File).name).toBe('m.xml')
  })

  it('sends the three CSV parts under their own names and only the ones chosen', () => {
    const form = importForm('csv', { elements: file('e.csv'), relations: file('r.csv') }, choice, false)

    expect(form.get('dry_run')).toBe('false')
    expect([...form.keys()]).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['elements', 'relations']))
    expect(form.has('properties')).toBe(false)
    expect(form.has('file')).toBe(false)
  })

  it('reads the server-made file name, with a fallback', () => {
    expect(filenameOf("attachment; filename*=UTF-8''ea-model-2026-10-10.xml", 'x')).toBe(
      'ea-model-2026-10-10.xml',
    )
    expect(filenameOf(null, 'ea-export.xml')).toBe('ea-export.xml')
  })
})

describe('simulating, then confirming', () => {
  it('can be confirmed only for the very files that were simulated, with the same options', async () => {
    const picked = { file: file('a.xml') }
    stubApi([{ method: 'POST', path: '/exchanges/imports', body: aReport({ sha256: 'a.xml:1' }) }])
    const exchanges = useExchanges({ digest })

    await exchanges.track('archimate-xml', picked)
    expect(exchanges.canConfirm(choice)).toBe(false)

    await exchanges.simulate('archimate-xml', picked, choice)
    expect(exchanges.report.value?.dry_run).toBe(true)
    expect(exchanges.canConfirm(choice)).toBe(true)

    expect(exchanges.canConfirm({ ...choice, strict: true })).toBe(false)
    await exchanges.track('archimate-xml', { file: file('b.xml', 'yy') })
    expect(exchanges.canConfirm(choice)).toBe(false)
    await exchanges.track('archimate-xml', picked)
    expect(exchanges.canConfirm(choice)).toBe(true)
  })

  it('replaces the simulation by the applied report, and cannot confirm twice', async () => {
    const picked = { file: file('a.xml') }
    stubApi([{ method: 'POST', path: '/exchanges/imports', body: aReport({ sha256: 'a.xml:1' }) }])
    const exchanges = useExchanges({ digest })
    await exchanges.track('archimate-xml', picked)
    await exchanges.simulate('archimate-xml', picked, choice)

    stubApi([
      {
        method: 'POST',
        path: '/exchanges/imports',
        body: aReport({ dry_run: false, applied: true, sha256: 'a.xml:1' }),
      },
    ])
    await exchanges.confirm('archimate-xml', picked, choice)

    expect(exchanges.report.value?.applied).toBe(true)
    expect(exchanges.canConfirm(choice)).toBe(false)
  })

  it('reports a refusal in words and keeps the simulation', async () => {
    const picked = { file: file('a.xml') }
    stubApi([
      {
        method: 'POST',
        path: '/exchanges/imports',
        status: 413,
        body: { error: 'file_too_large', detail: 'at most 20971520 bytes' },
      },
    ])
    const exchanges = useExchanges({ digest })

    await exchanges.simulate('archimate-xml', picked, choice)

    expect(exchanges.simulateError.value).toBe('at most 20971520 bytes')
    expect(exchanges.report.value).toBeNull()
  })

  it('says why when the browser cannot compute the digest', async () => {
    const exchanges = useExchanges({ digest: () => Promise.reject(new Error('no subtle')) })

    await exchanges.track('archimate-xml', { file: file('a.xml') })

    expect(exchanges.digestProblem.value).toMatch(/empreinte/)
    expect(exchanges.canConfirm(choice)).toBe(false)
  })
})

  })
})
```

`frontend/src/features/exchanges/digest.ts` :

```ts
// L'empreinte d'un ou plusieurs fichiers, calculée comme le serveur (`digest_of`).
//
// Un fichier : le SHA-256 de ses octets, ce que `shasum -a 256` affiche. Plusieurs
// (les trois CSV) : chaque partie précédée de sa longueur sur 8 octets
// gros-boutiste. Le bouton « Confirmer » compare cette empreinte à celle du
// rapport de la simulation : c'est ce qui dit qu'on confirme le fichier simulé.

function bytesOf(file: File): Promise<Uint8Array> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader()
    reader.onload = () => resolve(new Uint8Array(reader.result as ArrayBuffer))
    reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error ?? new Error('lecture impossible'))
    reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file)
  })
}

function joined(parts: Uint8Array[]): Uint8Array {
  if (parts.length === 1) {
    return parts[0] as Uint8Array
  }
  const out = new Uint8Array(parts.reduce((total, part) => total + 8 + part.length, 0))
  const view = new DataView(out.buffer)
  let offset = 0
  for (const part of parts) {
    view.setBigUint64(offset, BigInt(part.length))
    out.set(part, offset + 8)
    offset += 8 + part.length
  }
  return out
}

export async function sha256Of(files: File[]): Promise<string> {
  const body = joined(await Promise.all(files.map(bytesOf)))
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', body as BufferSource)
  return [...new Uint8Array(digest)].map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('')
}
```

`frontend/src/features/exchanges/useExchanges.ts` :

```ts
// Les échanges (docs/adr/NN) : télécharger le modèle, ou en importer un fichier en
// deux temps — simuler, puis confirmer le *même* fichier avec les *mêmes* options.
//
// L'API décide de tout ; ici on garde en mémoire ce qui a été simulé pour ne
// proposer la confirmation que sur la même chose. Les fichiers ne quittent jamais
// la mémoire du composant (ni `localStorage`).
import { ref } from 'vue'

import type { components } from '../../api/schema'
import { api, unwrap } from '../../lib/api'
import { useLatestRequest } from '../../lib/latest'
import { saveAs } from '../files/useFiles'
import { sha256Of } from './digest'

export type ImportReport = components['schemas']['ImportReportRead']
export type ExchangeFormat = components['schemas']['ExchangeFormat']
export type ScopeKind = components['schemas']['ScopeKind']
export type ConflictPolicy = components['schemas']['ConflictPolicy']

export type ExportChoice = {
  format: ExchangeFormat
  scope: ScopeKind
  packageId?: string
  queryId?: string
  withViews: boolean
  safeCells: boolean
}

export type ImportChoice = {
  format: ExchangeFormat
  conflict: ConflictPolicy
  strict: boolean
  withViews: boolean
  safeCells: boolean
  targetPackageId?: string
}

export type Picked = { file?: File; elements?: File; relations?: File; properties?: File }

const NO_DIGEST =
  "Ce navigateur ne peut pas calculer l'empreinte du fichier (contexte non sécurisé ?) : l'import ne peut pas être confirmé."

/** Les fichiers dans l'ordre où le serveur les digère : le XML seul, ou elements, relations, properties. */
export function filesOf(format: ExchangeFormat, picked: Picked): File[] {
  const order = format === 'csv' ? (['elements', 'relations', 'properties'] as const) : (['file'] as const)
  return order.flatMap((part) => {
    const chosen = picked[part]
    return chosen ? [chosen] : []
  })
}

export function importForm(
  format: ExchangeFormat,
  picked: Picked,
  choice: ImportChoice,
  dryRun: boolean,
): FormData {
  const form = new FormData()
  form.append('format', format)
  for (const part of format === 'csv' ? (['elements', 'relations', 'properties'] as const) : (['file'] as const)) {
    const chosen = picked[part]
    if (chosen) {
      form.append(part, chosen)
    }
  }
  form.append('dry_run', String(dryRun))
  form.append('on_conflict', choice.conflict)
  form.append('strict', String(choice.strict))
  form.append('with_views', String(choice.withViews))
  form.append('safe_cells', String(choice.safeCells))
  if (choice.targetPackageId) {
    form.append('target_package_id', choice.targetPackageId)
  }
  return form
}

/** `attachment; filename*=UTF-8''ea-model-….xml` → le nom, ou `fallback`. */
export function filenameOf(disposition: string | null, fallback: string): string {
  const found = /filename\*=UTF-8''([^;]+)/i.exec(disposition ?? '')
  if (!found?.[1]) {
    return fallback
  }
  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(found[1])
  } catch {
    return fallback
  }
}

export function useExchanges(deps: { digest?: (files: File[]) => Promise<string> } = {}) {
  const digest = deps.digest ?? sha256Of
  const report = ref<ImportReport | null>(null)
  const skippedViews = ref(0)
  const digestProblem = ref('')
  /** L'empreinte de ce que l'utilisateur a choisi *maintenant*. */
  const currentDigest = ref<string | null>(null)
  /** Les options de la simulation en cours de validité. */
  const simulatedWith = ref('')

  const exporting = useLatestRequest()
  const simulation = useLatestRequest()
  const confirmation = useLatestRequest()
  const tracking = useLatestRequest()

  async function track(format: ExchangeFormat, picked: Picked): Promise<void> {
    const files = filesOf(format, picked)
    await tracking.run(
      async () => {
        if (files.length === 0) {
          return { value: null, problem: '' }
        }
        try {
          return { value: await digest(files), problem: '' }
        } catch {
          return { value: null, problem: NO_DIGEST }
        }
      },
      ({ value, problem }) => {
        currentDigest.value = value
        digestProblem.value = problem
      },
    )
  }

  async function simulate(format: ExchangeFormat, picked: Picked, choice: ImportChoice): Promise<void> {
    await simulation.run(
      async (signal) =>
        unwrap(
          await api.POST('/exchanges/imports', {
            body: {},
            bodySerializer: () => importForm(format, picked, choice, true),
            signal,
          }),
        ),
      (answer) => {
        report.value = answer
        simulatedWith.value = JSON.stringify(choice)
      },
    )
  }

  async function confirm(format: ExchangeFormat, picked: Picked, choice: ImportChoice): Promise<void> {
    await confirmation.run(
      async (signal) =>
        unwrap(
          await api.POST('/exchanges/imports', {
            body: {},
            bodySerializer: () => importForm(format, picked, choice, false),
            signal,
          }),
        ),
      (answer) => {
        report.value = answer
        simulatedWith.value = ''
      },
    )
  }

  /** Une simulation réussie, du même fichier (empreinte), avec les mêmes options. */
  function canConfirm(choice: ImportChoice): boolean {
    const simulated = report.value
    return (
      simulated !== null &&
      simulated.dry_run &&
      currentDigest.value !== null &&
      simulated.sha256 === currentDigest.value &&
      simulatedWith.value === JSON.stringify(choice)
    )
  }

  async function exportModel(choice: ExportChoice): Promise<void> {
    await exporting.run(
      async (signal) => {
        const result = await api.GET('/exchanges/export', {
          params: {
            query: {
              format: choice.format,
              scope: choice.scope,
              package_id: choice.packageId,
              query_id: choice.queryId,
              with_views: choice.withViews,
              safe_cells: choice.safeCells,
            },
          },
          parseAs: 'blob',
          signal,
        })
        const extension = choice.format === 'csv' ? 'zip' : 'xml'
        return {
          blob: unwrap(result),
          name: filenameOf(result.response.headers.get('content-disposition'), `ea-export.${extension}`),
          skipped: Number(result.response.headers.get('x-export-skipped-views') ?? 0),
        }
      },
      ({ blob, name, skipped }) => {
        saveAs(blob, name)
        skippedViews.value = skipped
      },
    )
  }

  /** Oublier la simulation — par exemple quand le format change. */
  function forget(): void {
    report.value = null
    simulatedWith.value = ''
    currentDigest.value = null
    digestProblem.value = ''
    simulation.cancel()
    confirmation.cancel()
  }

  return {
    report,
    skippedViews,
    digestProblem,
    exportStatus: exporting.status,
    exportError: exporting.error,
    simulateStatus: simulation.status,
    simulateError: simulation.error,
    confirmStatus: confirmation.status,
    confirmError: confirmation.error,
    track,
    simulate,
    confirm,
    canConfirm,
    exportModel,
    forget,
  }
}
```
(L'écran décide du bouton « Confirmer l'import » avec `canConfirm(choice)`, qui lit l'état réactif `report`, `currentDigest` et `simulatedWith`.)

```ts
import { fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/vue'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import ImportReportTable from '../src/features/exchanges/ImportReportTable.vue'
import { aReport, anIssue } from './support/api'

function show(overrides = {}) {
  return render(ImportReportTable, { props: { report: aReport(overrides) } })
}

describe('ImportReportTable', () => {
  it('shows the counts of every category', () => {
    show({
      counts: {
        elements: { created: 3, reused: 1, rejected: 2, ignored: 4 },
        relationships: { created: 0, reused: 0, rejected: 0, ignored: 0 },
        packages: { created: 0, reused: 0, rejected: 0, ignored: 0 },
        views: { created: 0, reused: 0, rejected: 0, ignored: 0 },
      },
    })

    const row = screen.getByRole('row', { name: /Éléments/ })
    expect(within(row).getAllByRole('cell').map((cell) => cell.textContent)).toEqual(['3', '1', '2', '4'])
  })

  it('filters by severity and searches on the identifier', async () => {
    show({
      issues: [
        anIssue({ code: 'a_error', severity: 'error', source_id: 'alpha' }),
        anIssue({ code: 'b_warning', severity: 'warning', source_id: 'beta' }),
        anIssue({ code: 'c_info', severity: 'info', source_id: 'gamma' }),
      ],
    })

    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Gravité'), 'warning')
    expect(screen.queryByText('a_error')).toBeNull()
    expect(screen.getByText('b_warning')).toBeTruthy()

    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Gravité'), 'all')
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Identifiant'), 'GAM')
    expect(screen.getByText('c_info')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.queryByText('b_warning')).toBeNull()
  })

  it('sorts by code on demand', async () => {
    show({ issues: [anIssue({ code: 'zeta' }), anIssue({ code: 'alpha' })] })

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Trier par code' }))

    const codes = screen.getAllByTestId('issue-code').map((cell) => cell.textContent)
    expect(codes).toEqual(['alpha', 'zeta'])
  })

  it('says when the lines were cut, because the counts are still complete', () => {
    show({ truncated: true })

    expect(screen.getByText(/Rapport tronqué/)).toBeTruthy()
  })

  it('renders a hostile message as text, never as markup', () => {
    show({ issues: [anIssue({ message: '<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>' })] })

    expect(screen.getByText('<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(document.querySelector('img')).toBeNull()
  })

  it('locates a row by its line or by its XML path', () => {
    show({
      issues: [
        anIssue({ code: 'csv_row', line: 7, path: null }),
        anIssue({ code: 'xml_row', line: null, path: 'elements/element[2]' }),
      ],
    })

    expect(screen.getByText('ligne 7')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByText('elements/element[2]')).toBeTruthy()
  })
})
```

```ts
import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/vue'
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { computed, ref } from 'vue'
import { createMemoryHistory } from 'vue-router'

import ExchangesScreen from '../src/features/exchanges/ExchangesScreen.vue'
import { useMe } from '../src/lib/me'
import { createAppRouter } from '../src/router'
import { aReport, anIssue, stubApi, type Route } from './support/api'

// The real digest needs `crypto.subtle`, which jsdom does not give; a name-and-size
// stand-in keeps the same property that matters: another file, another digest.
vi.mock('../src/features/exchanges/digest', () => ({
  sha256Of: (files: File[]) => Promise.resolve(files.map((f) => `${f.name}:${f.size}`).join('|')),
}))

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals()
})

const IMPORT: Route = {
  method: 'POST',
  path: '/exchanges/imports',
  body: aReport({ sha256: 'm.xml:1', issues: [anIssue({ code: 'relationship_not_allowed' })] }),
}

async function open(query = '', routes: Route[] = []) {
  const calls = stubApi(routes)
  const router = createAppRouter(createMemoryHistory())
  await router.push(`/echanges${query}`)
  await router.isReady()
  render(ExchangesScreen, { global: { plugins: [router] } })
  return { calls, router }
}

async function pick(label: string, file: File): Promise<void> {
  const input = await screen.findByLabelText<HTMLInputElement>(label)
  Object.defineProperty(input, 'files', { value: [file], configurable: true })
  await fireEvent.change(input)
}

describe('the export tab', () => {
  it('downloads through the client, never through a bare link', async () => {
    const createObjectURL = vi.fn(() => 'blob:x')
    vi.stubGlobal('URL', Object.assign(URL, { createObjectURL, revokeObjectURL: vi.fn() }))
    const { calls } = await open('', [{ path: '/exchanges/export', body: '<model/>' }])

    await fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Télécharger' }))

    await waitFor(() => expect(createObjectURL).toHaveBeenCalled())
    expect(calls[0]?.url.searchParams.get('format')).toBe('archimate-xml')
    expect(calls[0]?.url.searchParams.get('with_views')).toBe('true')
  })

  it('keeps the chosen format in the URL and asks for it', async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal('URL', Object.assign(URL, { createObjectURL: vi.fn(() => 'blob:x'), revokeObjectURL: vi.fn() }))
    const { calls, router } = await open('', [{ path: '/exchanges/export', body: 'zip' }])

    await fireEvent.update(await screen.findByLabelText('Format'), 'csv')
    expect(router.currentRoute.value.query.format).toBe('csv')
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Télécharger' }))

    await waitFor(() => expect(calls).toHaveLength(1))
    expect(calls[0]?.url.searchParams.get('format')).toBe('csv')
  })

  it('tells how many views were left out of the file', async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal('URL', Object.assign(URL, { createObjectURL: vi.fn(() => 'blob:x'), revokeObjectURL: vi.fn() }))
    await open('', [{ path: '/exchanges/export', body: '<model/>', headers: { 'x-export-skipped-views': '2' } }])

    await fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Télécharger' }))

    expect(await screen.findByText(/2 vue\(s\) omise\(s\)/)).toBeTruthy()
  })
})

describe('the import tab', () => {
  it('is opened by the URL and offers the XML file', async () => {
    await open('?tab=import')

    expect(await screen.findByLabelText("Fichier d'échange (XML)")).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Importer', selected: true })).toBeTruthy()
  })

  it('cannot be confirmed before a simulation of the same file', async () => {
    await open('?tab=import', [IMPORT])
    const confirm = await screen.findByRole('button', { name: "Confirmer l'import" })
    expect(confirm).toBeDisabled()

    await pick("Fichier d'échange (XML)", new File(['x'], 'm.xml'))
    expect(confirm).toBeDisabled()

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Simuler' }))
    await screen.findByText('relationship_not_allowed')
    expect(confirm).toBeEnabled()
  })

  it('goes back to disabled when another file is chosen', async () => {
    await open('?tab=import', [IMPORT])
    await pick("Fichier d'échange (XML)", new File(['x'], 'm.xml'))
    await fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Simuler' }))
    await screen.findByText('relationship_not_allowed')

    await pick("Fichier d'échange (XML)", new File(['other'], 'n.xml'))

    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: "Confirmer l'import" })).toBeDisabled()
  })

  it('goes back to disabled when an option changes after the simulation', async () => {
    await open('?tab=import', [IMPORT])
    await pick("Fichier d'échange (XML)", new File(['x'], 'm.xml'))
    await fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Simuler' }))
    await screen.findByText('relationship_not_allowed')

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('Tout ou rien (strict)'))

    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: "Confirmer l'import" })).toBeDisabled()
  })

  it('shows the applied report with a link to the catalogue after confirming', async () => {
    await open('?tab=import', [IMPORT])
    await pick("Fichier d'échange (XML)", new File(['x'], 'm.xml'))
    await fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Simuler' }))
    await screen.findByText('relationship_not_allowed')
    stubApi([
      { method: 'POST', path: '/exchanges/imports', body: aReport({ dry_run: false, applied: true, sha256: 'm.xml:1' }) },
    ])

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: "Confirmer l'import" }))

    expect(await screen.findByText(/Import appliqué/)).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Ouvrir le catalogue' })).toBeTruthy()
  })

  it("shows the API's own words when it refuses the file", async () => {
    await open('?tab=import', [
      {
        method: 'POST',
        path: '/exchanges/imports',
        status: 413,
        body: { error: 'file_too_large', detail: 'an exchange file is at most 20971520 bytes' },
      },
    ])
    await pick("Fichier d'échange (XML)", new File(['x'], 'm.xml'))

    await fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Simuler' }))

    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('20971520')
  })

  it('takes the three CSV files in CSV mode', async () => {
    await open('?tab=import&format=csv')

    expect(await screen.findByLabelText('elements.csv')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('relations.csv')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('properties.csv')).toBeTruthy()
  })

  it('offers a reader neither the simulation nor the confirmation', async () => {
    vi.mocked(useMe).mockReturnValueOnce({
      me: ref({ username: 'reader', can_write: false }),
      canWrite: computed(() => false),
      error: ref(null),
      load: vi.fn(() => Promise.resolve()),
    })
    await open('?tab=import')

    expect(await screen.findByText(/réservé aux éditeurs/)).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Simuler' })).toBeNull()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: "Confirmer l'import" })).toBeNull()
  })

  it('lets a reader export', async () => {
    vi.mocked(useMe).mockReturnValueOnce({
      me: ref({ username: 'reader', can_write: false }),
      canWrite: computed(() => false),
      error: ref(null),
      load: vi.fn(() => Promise.resolve()),
    })
    await open('')

    expect(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Télécharger' })).toBeEnabled()
  })
})
```

`frontend/src/features/exchanges/ImportReportTable.vue` :

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// Le rapport d'un import : des compteurs toujours complets, des lignes éventuellement
// tronquées par le serveur. Les messages viennent d'un fichier étranger : on les rend
// comme du texte (jamais `v-html`).
import { computed, ref } from 'vue'

import type { ImportReport } from './useExchanges'

type Issue = ImportReport['issues'][number]

const props = defineProps<{ report: ImportReport }>()

const CATEGORIES = [
  ['elements', 'Éléments'],
  ['relationships', 'Relations'],
  ['packages', 'Paquets'],
  ['views', 'Vues'],
] as const
const OUTCOMES = [
  ['created', 'Créés'],
  ['reused', 'Réutilisés'],
  ['rejected', 'Rejetés'],
  ['ignored', 'Ignorés'],
] as const
const SEVERITIES: Record<Issue['severity'], string> = {
  error: 'Erreur',
  warning: 'Avertissement',
  info: 'Information',
}

const severity = ref<'all' | Issue['severity']>('all')
const search = ref('')
const sortByCode = ref(false)

const lines = computed(() => {
  const wanted = search.value.trim().toLowerCase()
  const kept = props.report.issues.filter(
    (issue) =>
      (severity.value === 'all' || issue.severity === severity.value) &&
      (wanted === '' || (issue.source_id ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(wanted)),
  )
  return sortByCode.value ? [...kept].sort((a, b) => a.code.localeCompare(b.code)) : kept
})

function where(issue: Issue): string {
  return issue.line != null ? `ligne ${issue.line}` : (issue.path ?? '')
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="report" aria-label="Rapport d'import">
    <table class="counts">
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th scope="col">Catégorie</th>
          <th v-for="[key, label] in OUTCOMES" :key="key" scope="col">{{ label }}</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        <tr v-for="[category, label] in CATEGORIES" :key="category">
          <th scope="row">{{ label }}</th>
          <td v-for="[key] in OUTCOMES" :key="key">{{ report.counts[category][key] }}</td>
        </tr>
      </tbody>
    </table>

    <p v-if="report.truncated" role="status">
      Rapport tronqué : seules les premières lignes sont affichées, les compteurs sont complets.
    </p>

    <div class="filters">
      <label>
        Gravité
        <select v-model="severity">
          <option value="all">Toutes</option>
          <option v-for="(label, key) in SEVERITIES" :key="key" :value="key">{{ label }}</option>
        </select>
      </label>
      <label>
        Identifiant
        <input v-model="search" type="search" />
      </label>
      <button type="button" @click="sortByCode = !sortByCode">Trier par code</button>
    </div>

    <p v-if="lines.length === 0">Aucune ligne à afficher.</p>
    <table v-else class="issues">
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th scope="col">Gravité</th>
          <th scope="col">Code</th>
          <th scope="col">Identifiant</th>
          <th scope="col">Où</th>
          <th scope="col">Message</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        <tr v-for="(issue, index) in lines" :key="index">
          <td>{{ SEVERITIES[issue.severity] }}</td>
          <td data-testid="issue-code">{{ issue.code }}</td>
          <td>{{ issue.source_id ?? '' }}</td>
          <td>{{ where(issue) }}</td>
          <td>{{ issue.message }}</td>
        </tr>
      </tbody>
    </table>
  </section>
</template>

<style scoped>
table {
  border-collapse: collapse;
  width: 100%;
}
th,
td {
  border: 1px solid var(--border, #ccc);
  padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem;
  text-align: left;
}
.filters {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 1rem;
  margin: 1rem 0;
}
</style>
```

`frontend/src/features/exchanges/ExchangesScreen.vue` :

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// Échanger le modèle avec Sparx EA, Archi et les autres outils ArchiMate (docs/adr/NN).
//
// L'onglet, le format (et, plus loin, le périmètre) sont dans l'URL : ce sont des
// questions qu'on partage. Les fichiers choisis restent dans la mémoire du composant.
// L'API décide qui importe ; ici on cache seulement ce qu'un lecteur ne peut pas faire.
import { computed, ref, watch } from 'vue'
import { RouterLink, useRoute, useRouter } from 'vue-router'

import { useMe } from '../../lib/me'
import ImportReportTable from './ImportReportTable.vue'
import {
  filesOf,
  useExchanges,
  type ConflictPolicy,
  type ExchangeFormat,
  type ImportChoice,
  type Picked,
} from './useExchanges'

const TABS = [
  ['export', 'Exporter'],
  ['import', 'Importer'],
] as const
type Tab = (typeof TABS)[number][0]
type Part = keyof Picked

const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()
const { canWrite } = useMe()
const exchanges = useExchanges()
const {
  report,
  skippedViews,
  digestProblem,
  exportStatus,
  exportError,
  simulateStatus,
  simulateError,
  confirmStatus,
  confirmError,
} = exchanges

const tab = computed<Tab>(() => (route.query.tab === 'import' ? 'import' : 'export'))
const format = computed<ExchangeFormat>(() => (route.query.format === 'csv' ? 'csv' : 'archimate-xml'))

const withViews = ref(true)
const safeCells = ref(true)
const picked = ref<Picked>({})
const conflict = ref<ConflictPolicy>('reuse')
const strict = ref(false)

const importChoice = computed<ImportChoice>(() => ({
  format: format.value,
  conflict: conflict.value,
  strict: strict.value,
  withViews: withViews.value,
  safeCells: safeCells.value,
}))
const hasFiles = computed(() => filesOf(format.value, picked.value).length > 0)
const busy = computed(() => simulateStatus.value === 'loading' || confirmStatus.value === 'loading')
const canSimulate = computed(() => hasFiles.value && !busy.value)
const confirmable = computed(() => !busy.value && exchanges.canConfirm(importChoice.value))
const importError = computed(() => simulateError.value || confirmError.value)
const outcome = computed(() => {
  const current = report.value
  if (current === null) {
    return ''
  }
  if (current.dry_run) {
    return 'Simulation : rien n’a été écrit.'
  }
  return current.applied ? 'Import appliqué.' : 'Rien n’a été écrit.'
})

watch(format, () => {
  picked.value = {}
  exchanges.forget()
})

function setQuery(patch: Record<string, string | undefined>): void {
  void router.replace({ query: { ...route.query, ...patch } })
}

function openTab(next: Tab): void {
  void router.push({ query: { ...route.query, tab: next === 'export' ? undefined : next } })
}

function setFormat(event: Event): void {
  setQuery({ format: (event.target as HTMLSelectElement).value === 'csv' ? 'csv' : undefined })
}

async function onPick(part: Part, event: Event): Promise<void> {
  const input = event.target as HTMLInputElement
  const next: Picked = { ...picked.value }
  next[part] = input.files?.[0]
  picked.value = next
  await exchanges.track(format.value, next)
}

function onExport(): void {
  void exchanges.exportModel({
    format: format.value,
    scope: 'model',
    withViews: withViews.value,
    safeCells: safeCells.value,
  })
}

function onSimulate(): void {
  void exchanges.simulate(format.value, picked.value, importChoice.value)
}

function onConfirm(): void {
  void exchanges.confirm(format.value, picked.value, importChoice.value)
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="exchanges">
    <h1>Échanges</h1>
    <p>Ouvrir et échanger le modèle avec Sparx EA, Archi et les autres outils ArchiMate.</p>

    <div role="tablist" aria-label="Sens de l'échange">
      <button
        v-for="[id, label] in TABS"
        :key="id"
        type="button"
        role="tab"
        :aria-selected="tab === id"
        @click="openTab(id)"
      >
        {{ label }}
      </button>
    </div>

    <form v-if="tab === 'export'" class="panel" @submit.prevent="onExport">
      <label>
        Format
        <select :value="format" @change="setFormat">
          <option value="archimate-xml">XML ArchiMate Exchange</option>
          <option value="csv">CSV (trois fichiers, dans un zip)</option>
        </select>
      </label>
      <label v-if="format === 'archimate-xml'">
        <input v-model="withViews" type="checkbox" />
        Inclure les vues
      </label>
      <label v-else>
        <input v-model="safeCells" type="checkbox" />
        Neutraliser les formules (=, +, -, @)
      </label>
      <button type="submit" :disabled="exportStatus === 'loading'">Télécharger</button>
      <p v-if="skippedViews > 0" role="status">
        {{ skippedViews }} vue(s) omise(s) : une de leurs boîtes est hors du périmètre exporté.
      </p>
      <p v-if="exportError" role="alert">{{ exportError }}</p>
    </form>

    <div v-else class="panel">
      <p v-if="!canWrite" role="note">
        L'import est réservé aux éditeurs : il écrit dans le catalogue, même pour une simulation.
      </p>
      <template v-else>
        <label>
          Format
          <select :value="format" @change="setFormat">
            <option value="archimate-xml">XML ArchiMate Exchange</option>
            <option value="csv">CSV (trois fichiers)</option>
          </select>
        </label>

        <template v-if="format === 'archimate-xml'">
          <label>
            Fichier d'échange (XML)
            <input type="file" accept=".xml,application/xml,text/xml" @change="onPick('file', $event)" />
          </label>
        </template>
        <template v-else>
          <label>
            elements.csv
            <input type="file" accept=".csv,text/csv" @change="onPick('elements', $event)" />
          </label>
          <label>
            relations.csv
            <input type="file" accept=".csv,text/csv" @change="onPick('relations', $event)" />
          </label>
          <label>
            properties.csv
            <input type="file" accept=".csv,text/csv" @change="onPick('properties', $event)" />
          </label>
        </template>

        <fieldset>
          <legend>Options</legend>
          <label>
            Un élément existant (même type et même nom)
            <select v-model="conflict">
              <option value="reuse">est réutilisé</option>
              <option value="fail">est une erreur</option>
            </select>
          </label>
          <label>
            <input v-model="strict" type="checkbox" />
            Tout ou rien (strict)
          </label>
          <label v-if="format === 'archimate-xml'">
            <input v-model="withViews" type="checkbox" />
            Importer les vues
          </label>
          <label v-else>
            <input v-model="safeCells" type="checkbox" />
            Restaurer les cellules neutralisées
          </label>
        </fieldset>

        <button type="button" :disabled="!canSimulate" @click="onSimulate">Simuler</button>
        <button type="button" :disabled="!confirmable" @click="onConfirm">Confirmer l'import</button>

        <p v-if="digestProblem" role="alert">{{ digestProblem }}</p>
        <p v-if="importError" role="alert">{{ importError }}</p>

        <template v-if="report">
          <p role="status">{{ outcome }} Empreinte du fichier : {{ report.sha256.slice(0, 12) }}…</p>
          <ImportReportTable :report="report" />
          <RouterLink v-if="report.applied" :to="{ name: 'elements' }">Ouvrir le catalogue</RouterLink>
        </template>
      </template>
    </div>
  </section>
</template>

<style scoped>
.panel {

  gap: 0.75rem;

}
[role='tablist'] {

</style>
```

Dans `useExchanges.ts` : (`PackageRead` et `SavedQuerySummary` viennent du client régénéré, d'après le sous-projet 1 §5)

```ts
export type PackageOption = components['schemas']['PackageRead']
export type QueryOption = components['schemas']['SavedQuerySummary']
```
```ts
  const packages = ref<PackageOption[]>([])
  const queries = ref<QueryOption[]>([])
  const scopes = useLatestRequest()

  /** Les paquets et les requêtes enregistrées, pour choisir un périmètre. */
  async function loadScopes(): Promise<void> {
    await scopes.run(
      async (signal) => ({
        packages: unwrap(await api.GET('/packages', { signal })),
        queries: unwrap(await api.GET('/queries', { signal })),
      }),
      (answer) => {
        packages.value = answer.packages
        queries.value = answer.queries
      },
    )
  }
```
et `packages, queries, loadScopes, scopesError: scopes.error` ajoutés au `return`.

Dans `ExchangesScreen.vue` (`<script setup>`) :

```ts
const scope = computed<ScopeKind>(() =>
  route.query.scope === 'package' || route.query.scope === 'query' ? route.query.scope : 'model',
)
const packageId = computed(() => (typeof route.query.package === 'string' ? route.query.package : undefined))
const queryId = computed(() => (typeof route.query.query === 'string' ? route.query.query : undefined))
const targetPackage = ref('')
const scopeChosen = computed(
  () => (scope.value !== 'package' || packageId.value !== undefined) && (scope.value !== 'query' || queryId.value !== undefined),
)

watch(
  scope,
  (value) => {
    if (value !== 'model') {
      void exchanges.loadScopes()
    }
  },
  { immediate: true },
)

function setScope(event: Event): void {
  const value = (event.target as HTMLSelectElement).value
  setQuery({ scope: value === 'model' ? undefined : value, package: undefined, query: undefined })
}

function pickFrom(key: 'package' | 'query', event: Event): void {
  setQuery({ [key]: (event.target as HTMLSelectElement).value || undefined })
}
```
`onExport` passe `scope: scope.value, packageId: packageId.value, queryId: queryId.value` ; `importChoice` gagne `targetPackageId: targetPackage.value || undefined` ; le bouton « Télécharger » devient `:disabled="exportStatus === 'loading' || !scopeChosen"`. The model can be exchanged with Sparx EA, Archi and other ArchiMate tools: `/exchanges/export` and `/exchanges/imports`, the MCP tools `export_model` and `import_model` and the SPA's *Échanges* section read and write the ArchiMate Model Exchange XML or the Archi-style CSV (codecs in `formats/`), an import going through `ArchitectureService` inside one shared transaction (`db/unit_of_work.py`) with a savepoint per row, simulating by default (`dry_run`), reusing an element of the same type and name and never deleting or overwriting one (see `docs/adr/NN` and `docs/adr/NN`).

# Auto-revue

**Couverture de la spec (section → tâche).**

| Spec | Tâches |
|---|---|
| §3 types, jonctions, `Access`/`isDirected`/`modifier`, langues, documentation, `ea.description`, propriétés | 2, 3, 10, 17, 18 (jonctions : écart 1) |
| §3 CSV (trois fichiers, BOM, deux orthographes de type, formules) | 17, 18, 19, 20 |
| §3 règle de collision, relation refusée, `strict`, extrémité rejetée | 12, 13 |
| §3 vues (boîtes, suffixe, 500 boîtes, boîte omise), `organizations` ↔ paquets | 22, 23, 24, 26 |
| §4 transaction partagée, `SAVEPOINT`, verrou, dry-run par `ROLLBACK`, export `REPEATABLE READ` | 4, 9, 11, 12, 15 |
| §5 `GET /exchanges/export`, `POST /exchanges/imports`, `dry_run` par défaut, rapport, `sha256`, codes d'erreur | 1, 6, 12, 14, 19 |
| §6 `export_model`, `import_model`, `INSTRUCTIONS`, plafonds, pas de vues | 30, 31, 32 |
| §7 SPA : onglets en URL, trois temps, `sha256`, lecteur, `useLatestRequest`, pas de `v-html` | 33, 34 |
| §8 `defusedxml`, 20 Mo en flux, pas de zip en entrée, droits (AST), neutralisation CSV, nom serveur, journal | 5, 7, 10, 12, 14, 17, 18, 19 |
| §9 tests : unitaires, jeux d'exemple, XSD, aller-retour XML et CSV, dry-run, rejet partiel, API, MCP, Vitest | 1-3, 7, 10, 12-15, 18-20, 24, 28, 30, 31, 33, 34 |
| §10 ADR 1, 2, 3 | 8, 7, 9 |
| §11 risques (doublons du fichier, matrices 3.0/3.1, verrou, identifiants non-NCName, paquet d'un élément existant, extrémités hors périmètre, vues hors périmètre, volumétrie, délai) | 12, 13, 2, 26, 27, 23, 14, 5 |
| §12 six lots | Lot 1 (1-8), Lot 2 (9-16), Lot 3 (17-21), Lot 4 (22-29), Lot 5 (30-32), Lot 6 (33-35) |

**Review Focus.** Chaque entrée a son test dans la tâche indiquée : fichier vide, XML mal formé, DTD, entités, XXE (Task 10 et 14) ; identifiants en double (10, 12) ; octets et encodage (3, 18) ; clés de propriété (10, 12) ; références pendantes et flux coupé (13, 14, 24, 26).

**Placeholders.** Aucun « TBD », « TODO » ni « similaire à la tâche N » ; les seuls jokers sont les numéros d'ADR (`NN`, « prochain libre à l'exécution » comme demandé). Deux zones dépendent de code qui n'existe pas encore et sont donc bornées par une tâche de garde (25) : les noms réels du service de paquets et du service de requêtes du sous-projet 1 (adaptateurs de la Task 26 et 27, `build_package_service` de la Task 28) ; le plan donne le contrat exact (`Packages`, `Queries`) et dit quels appels ajuster.

**Cohérence des noms.** Port `ExchangeRepository` : `snapshot` (4), `transaction`, `savepoint`, `try_import_lock`, `known_elements`, `known_relationship_keys` (11), `count_skipped_views` (27) — chacun implémenté par `PostgresExchangeRepository` et par `InMemoryExchange` dans la tâche qui l'ajoute. `ExportSnapshot` : `elements_page`, `relationships_page` (4), `views_page` (23), `packages` (26). Service : `export`, `export_text`, `import_exchange`, `max_bytes`. Options : `ExportRequest(format, scope, with_views, safe_cells)`, `ImportOptions(dry_run, on_conflict, strict, safe_cells, with_views, target_package_id)`. Rapport : `ImportReport` (domaine) / `ImportReportRead` (HTTP) avec `applied`. Une clé d'`extra=` du journal ne doit jamais être un attribut de `LogRecord` (`created`, `name`, `module`…) : les compteurs d'import s'appellent `rows_*`.

**Risques résiduels.** (1) Le XSD de l'Open Group n'est pas versionné : la conformité de l'export n'est vérifiée qu'en local tant que sa licence n'est pas lue (Task 7), ainsi que l'espace de noms `…/3.0/` et le défaut `accessType=Write`. (2) Un import coûte plusieurs requêtes par relation : minutes à 50 000 éléments (ADR). (3) Sparx EA n'est pas testé en CI : vérification manuelle consignée dans l'ADR (Task 35). (4) Tout dépôt qu'un cas d'usage partagé traverse doit passer par `work(...)` : un dépôt oublié écrirait hors de la transaction (ADR 3). (5) Le plan ne compile pas : les signatures SQLAlchemy/defusedxml/Starlette citées (`postgresql_readonly`, `begin_nested`, `HTTP_413_CONTENT_TOO_LARGE`) sont à confirmer par le premier `mypy`/test rouge de leur tâche.
